# eRev Cloud: supervisor decisions

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Owner | Team lead (supervisor session). Ray delegated product and technical decisions ("decide for me"). |
| Date | 2026-09-11 |
| Status | Binding. Amended only by the supervisor; new decisions are appended with the next free number. |
| Inputs | `docs/research/99-gaps.md` (critic), research 01–07, legacy 01–07 |

These rulings resolve the P1 contradictions and open questions in `docs/research/99-gaps.md`. They fix the conventions every later document must use. An author who believes a ruling is wrong writes the objection in their review file, citing the D-number. The author does not deviate in their own document.

## 0. Document precedence

| Question | Authority, highest first |
|---|---|
| Scope, definition of done, standing constraints | `docs/00-GOAL.md` |
| Cross-cutting conventions and policy defaults | this file |
| Priority and 1.0 in/out of any capability | `docs/03-REQUIREMENTS.md` |
| Accounting behaviour and calculation | `docs/accounting/ENGINE_SPEC.md` and `docs/accounting/POLICIES.md`. For numbers, the machine-readable answer keys in `docs/accounting/answer-keys/` win over prose; a conflict is a spec question |
| Code structure, kernel contracts, testing, gates | `docs/dev-guide.md` |
| Entities, tables, API surface | `docs/04-DATA_MODEL.md` → `docs/05-ARCHITECTURE.md` |
| Product behaviour, journeys, copy | `docs/02-PRD.md` |
| Visuals and screens | `docs/design/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` → `docs/design/SCREENS.md` |
| Background only; never overrides the above | `docs/research/*`, `docs/legacy/*` (the golden CSVs and `golden-tests.json` are test fixtures, governed by G3 and D-32) |

Supervisor documents, read-only for the build loop:
- `docs/00-GOAL.md`, `docs/01-DECISIONS.md`, `docs/02-PRD.md`, `docs/03-REQUIREMENTS.md`, `docs/04-DATA_MODEL.md`, `docs/05-ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/dev-guide.md`, `docs/BUILD_SPEC.md`;
- everything under `docs/accounting/`, `docs/design/`, `docs/research/` and `docs/legacy/`;
- `PROMPT.md`.

## 1. Product

- **D-01 Open and complete.** MIT licence. There are no premium tiers and no licence keys. Legacy premium database functions become standard audited features:
  - backup/restore → tenant snapshot and restore to a sandbox;
  - reset → reset a sandbox tenant only;
  - append-from-another → import a legacy database;
  - purge → void a contract through an approval.
- **D-02 Our own vocabulary (clean room).**

  | Area | Our terms |
  |---|---|
  | Navigation | **Home**; **Contracts**; **Obligations** (inside a contract); **Schedules**; **Close**; **Journals**; **Reports**; **Policies** (revenue policies, SSP books, account mapping); **Data** (imports, integrations, exception queue); **Approvals**; **Settings** |
  | Amounts | "Scheduled" and "Awaiting trigger" for amounts; "Allocation adjustment" for the allocated-vs-list difference |
  | Balances | "Contract liability", "Contract asset", "Unbilled receivable" |

  Do not use "Revenue Desk 360", "Planned/Unplanned", "Carves", "Revi" or other RightRev feature names.
- **D-03 One register.** `docs/03-REQUIREMENTS.md` is the only priority authority (C-01). Every capability locked in by the legacy golden tests is P0 for 1.0. That covers returns, material rights, delta JEs, prospective and retrospective modifications, POB-specific VC, $0 POBs, cross-entity POBs, and gross and adjustment JEs.
- **D-04 1.0 includes** everything listed in `docs/00-GOAL.md` §2. The register marks each REQ as `1.0` or `later`. It may not move a `docs/00-GOAL.md` §2 capability to `later`.

## 2. Engine conventions (resolve C-02 to C-09)

- **D-10 Engine shape.** Adopt research 06 D1, D2, D4 and D21. The engine is a pure function over one contract's (post-combination) bitemporal event stream plus pinned reference-data versions (SSP book, policy, FX rates, calendar) and the engine version. It returns a computed contract version, schedules, journal lines and a calculation trace. It lives in a DB-free package `erev_engine` that runs without Postgres.
- **D-11 Money and rounding (C-06, C-07).**
  - The engine uses `decimal.Decimal` with `FloatOperation` trapped. Ratios are held as `fractions.Fraction` or as a 38-digit Decimal context; they are never floats.
  - Posted amounts (schedules, journal lines, balances) are rounded to the currency's ISO 4217 minor unit with **ROUND_HALF_UP**. They are stored as `NUMERIC(24,4)` and serialized as strings.
  - Unit rates and allocation ratios are stored at full precision as `NUMERIC(38,18)`. FX rates are stored as `NUMERIC(28,12)`.
  - Every apportionment (allocation across POBs, splits across entities or periods) uses **largest remainder** at the minor unit. Ties break by larger fractional remainder, then larger SSP weight, then ascending POB line key.
  - Schedules use **cumulative rounding**: period amount = round(cumulative exact) − round(previous cumulative exact). There is no plug-to-last-period. Research 04 answer keys that plug the last period are re-baselined to this rule.
- **D-12 Contract position sign (C-02).** The canonical engine quantity is `net_position = cumulative consideration billed or unconditionally due − cumulative revenue recognized`, per contract, per selling entity, per book. Positive means contract liability; negative means contract asset and/or unbilled receivable. This matches legacy and common practice. The UI never shows a signed "position". It shows the labelled balance ("Contract liability $X", "Contract asset $X"). Answer keys assert labelled balances.
- **D-13 Billing ownership (C-03).** Tenant setting `billing_posting ∈ {erp, engine}`, default `erp`.

  | Posting | `erp` (default) | `engine` |
  |---|---|---|
  | On invoice | The ERP or billing system posts Dr AR / Cr Contract liability. The engine ingests invoices without posting them | The engine also posts Dr AR / Cr Contract liability |
  | On recognition | Engine: Dr Contract liability / Cr Revenue | Same |
  | At period end | Engine books the netting reclass (auto-reversing next period): Dr Contract asset or Unbilled receivable / Cr Contract liability | Same |
  | Reconciliation | Billing-to-subledger reconciliation | Adds the per-contract AR tie-out (V8) |

  Legacy parity uses `erp`.
- **D-14 Account roles (C-04).** `account_role` is an enum:
  - revenue: `REVENUE`;
  - contract balances and receivables: `CONTRACT_LIABILITY`, `CONTRACT_ASSET`, `UNBILLED_RECEIVABLE`, `ACCOUNTS_RECEIVABLE`, `BILLING_CLEARING`;
  - returns, deposits and customer consideration: `REFUND_LIABILITY`, `RETURN_ASSET`, `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY`, `CONSIDERATION_PAYABLE`, `CUSTOMER_INCENTIVE_ASSET`;
  - contract costs: `COST_TO_OBTAIN_ASSET`, `COST_TO_FULFILL_ASSET`, `CONTRACT_COST_AMORTIZATION`, `CONTRACT_COST_IMPAIRMENT`;
  - loss contracts and warranties: `LOSS_PROVISION`, `LOSS_EXPENSE`, `WARRANTY_PROVISION`, `WARRANTY_EXPENSE`;
  - financing, FX and intercompany: `INTEREST_INCOME`, `INTEREST_EXPENSE`, `FX_GAIN_LOSS`, `INTERCOMPANY_DUE_TO`, `INTERCOMPANY_DUE_FROM`;
  - other: `NONCASH_CONSIDERATION_ASSET`, `SALES_TAX_PAYABLE`, `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE`, `ROUNDING`.

  A mapping (role × entity × optional product or revenue category) resolves each role to a GL account. The mapping is versioned and effective-dated. Research 04 §0.3 codes are the demo chart of accounts.
- **D-15 Contract asset vs unbilled receivable (C-21).** The engine always tracks the two separately. An unconditional right not yet invoiced (right-to-invoice, time-elapsed billing) is an unbilled receivable; a conditional right is a contract asset. The mapping may point both roles to one GL account; the legacy-parity preset does, via each POB's "Unbilled A/R Account". Refund liabilities are never netted into the contract position.
- **D-16 Journal balancing.** Every journal line pair derives from one rounded amount. A journal balances per (entity, book, currency, period) by construction, and a database constraint enforces it at batch commit. FX translation residue posts to `ROUNDING`. Corrections post only as reversal or delta lines (research 06 D3, research 07 F-10).
- **D-17 Test tolerances (C-08).**
  - **Production answer keys:** exact to the currency minor unit.
  - **Legacy parity suite:** engine full-precision values vs legacy values within an absolute 1e-4 on unit-level amounts; journal lines exact to the cent after half-up.
  - **Legacy defects:** where legacy is wrong (the probes, the 0.01 JE imbalances, split-upload allocation, the negative-quantity clamp, and so on), the case lives in `docs/legacy/DEVIATIONS.md`. Each entry records the legacy value, the corrected expected value, the rationale and the reviewer. The parity suite asserts the corrected value for those cases.
- **D-18 SSP basis for modifications (C-09).**
  - **606-10-25-13(a) prospective modifications:** allocate remaining consideration to the remaining POBs using SSPs **as of the modification date**, taken from the latest approved SSP book version effective on that date. A per-POB override to another approved version is allowed with a recorded justification and approval. The legacy-parity preset uses the SSP version named in the modification file.
  - **606-10-25-13(b) cumulative catch-up modifications:** existing POBs keep their **inception** SSPs (606-10-32-43). Added goods or services that form part of a partially satisfied POB use the modification-date SSP for their incremental weight.
  - **Mixed modifications (25-13(c))** apply each rule to the affected POBs.
  - `docs/accounting/POLICIES.md` states the precise algorithm.
- **D-19 Dual dates and late events.** Every event carries `effective_date` and `recorded_at` (UTC). An event effective in a closed period posts its catch-up to the first open period, tagged with `origin_period`. The out-of-period register lists these events. Period assignment uses the entity's time zone for effective dates; `recorded_at` is always UTC (M-ARCH-05).
- **D-20 Estimates are versioned objects.** VC estimates, constraint judgements, return rates, breakage rates, EAC and exercise likelihoods are stored as immutable estimate versions with preparer, approver, rationale and evidence. A reassessment creates a version, never an in-place update, and the catch-up is traced to the version pair.
- **D-21 Material rights.**
  - SSP of an option = discount × likelihood of exercise (606-10-55-44), or the renewal practical alternative (55-45).
  - **Exercise treatment:** tenant policy `material_right_exercise ∈ {continuation, modification}`, default `continuation`. On exercise, the allocated material-right amount plus any additional consideration is recognized as the optioned goods transfer.
  - The legacy-parity preset uses `modification` with the legacy "quantity = SSP dollars" import convention.
- **D-22 Returns.** Expected returns reduce the transaction price and create a refund liability plus a return asset, remeasured each period (606-10-55-22 to 55-29). The legacy import convention (negative quantity and negative billing rows) is accepted and mapped to return events. It never silently creates a negative POB.
- **D-23 Entities.** Each POB carries a contracting entity and a performing entity. Allocation is per contract (combination group), across entities. Balance presentation and netting are per legal entity. Cross-entity performance creates `INTERCOMPANY_DUE_TO/FROM` pairs tagged with the counterparty; eliminations belong to consolidation (research 06 D10).
- **D-24 Books.** 1.0 books are `ASC606` (primary), `IFRS15` and `LEGACY`.
  - IFRS 15 differences are book parameters taken from research 04 §13 (the full switch list, C-12).
  - The `LEGACY` book holds pre-606 revenue for the delta ("adjustment") JE mode.
  - Research 06 D11 memoisation applies.
- **D-25 FX.** Adopt research 06 D9.
  - Contract assets and receivables are remeasured at the closing rate, with the difference posted to `FX_GAIN_LOSS`.
  - Contract liabilities are layered at historical rates and not remeasured.
  - IFRIC 22 transaction dates apply in the IFRS book.
  - The US GAAP layer approach is a policy flag.
  - Answer keys cover all of these (M-RA-02).
- **D-26 Codification currency (U-03).** Accounting documents must be verified against the Codification as amended through the latest ASU affecting Topics 606, 340-40, 605-35 and 805-20 (contract assets and liabilities acquired in a business combination, ASU 2021-08). Include the scope clarification for share-based noncash consideration from customers, if issued. Record the ASU list checked, with URLs.

## 3. Legacy parity and migration (resolve C-13, C-14, M-PM-02)

- **D-30 Templates.**
  - **Legacy v1:** the four legacy Excel templates are accepted with their exact headers.
  - **Modern CSV:** modern CSV templates sit alongside them.
  - **Import flow:** upload → validate (header, type, cross-row, cross-file) → dry-run diff → maker-checker approval → atomic commit. The original file's SHA-256 is kept, with row-level lineage.
  - **Defects rejected:** legacy silent defects (dropped rows on blank memo, duplicate double-counting, swallowed TypeErrors) become explicit validation errors in the exception queue. They are never silent.
- **D-31 Legacy database import.** Import a legacy `ASC606.db` in one of two modes:
  - **(a) opening balances:** the latest version of each POB becomes opening balances as of a cutover date, and the full `Contract_Live` history is attached as read-only migrated history;
  - **(b) replay:** the legacy templates are replayed into a sandbox, then promoted.

  Both modes produce a reconciliation report against the source database.
- **D-32 Legacy-parity preset.** A policy set that reproduces legacy *policies*, not legacy defects:
  - clamp out-of-range prices to the nearest SSP range boundary;
  - the material-right dollar-quantity convention;
  - full retrospective re-allocation for 25-13(b);
  - reclass allocation keyed on cumulative SSP delivered;
  - a single unbilled A/R account per POB;
  - `billing_posting = erp`;
  - gross and adjustment JE formats.

  The golden suite runs under this preset.
- **D-33 Legacy exports.** The 71-column "Legacy contract history" export and the "Latest contract" export are retained as standard reports, using legacy column names and legacy sign.
- **D-34 Delta posting is P0 (C-14).** The `LEGACY` book and the adjustment JE mode ship in the same phase as gross JEs, because golden test GT-07 is part of the parity gate.

## 4. Architecture and stack

- **D-40 Backend.** Python 3.12 managed by uv; FastAPI; SQLAlchemy 2; Alembic; Pydantic 2; psycopg 3 (dynamically linked; licence note in NOTICE); Procrastinate for jobs; openpyxl and XlsxWriter for Excel; Hypothesis for properties. **PostgreSQL 17 only**: no SQLite anywhere, because RLS, triggers and partitioning are part of the contract. The pure engine package tests without a database.
- **D-41 Frontend.** React 19; TypeScript 5; Vite 6; Tailwind CSS 4; React Router 7; TanStack Query, Table and Virtual; Recharts for charts; Phosphor or Lucide icons (designer's choice, permissive licence); fonts under OFL. There is no AG Grid Enterprise. Vitest and Testing Library for unit tests; Playwright plus @axe-core/playwright for e2e.
- **D-42 Tenancy (research 06 D18).** Pooled tables with `ENABLE` + `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` and the tenant set per transaction with `SET LOCAL`.

  | Role | Used for | Attributes |
  |---|---|---|
  | `erev_owner` | Migrations; owns objects | `NOSUPERUSER` |
  | `erev_app` | The runtime API, worker and tests | `NOSUPERUSER NOBYPASSRLS` |

  The supervisor provisions both roles before the build. The loop never creates or alters roles. A superuser connection is never used by application code or by the isolation tests.
- **D-43 Immutability.** The subledger, calculation versions, audit events and import lineage are append-only. They are enforced by INSERT-only grants to `erev_app` plus triggers, with a per-tenant HMAC hash chain on audit events (research 07 F-03, research 06 D3, D19).
- **D-44 Identity.** Email + password (argon2id); TOTP MFA required for admin, approver, controller and close roles; OIDC login through an interface tested against a mock IdP; API clients via OAuth2 client credentials with scoped tokens; SAML and SCIM reserved (later).
- **D-45 API.** Adopt research 06 D17. REST under `/api/v1`; OpenAPI 3.1; `Idempotency-Key` on every command; RFC 9457 problem details; money as strings; `ETag`/`If-Match` on mutable resources; `as_of` and `known_at` on reads; 202 plus job resources for long runs; `/explain` endpoints for computed figures.
- **D-46 AI.** A provider interface with a fake default; Anthropic SDK adapter with a configurable model (default `claude-opus-5`), off by default per tenant. "No LLM on the money path": AI output is a proposal object with citations, and accepting it is an audited human command. Prompts, model id and response hash are logged as configuration evidence. Contract text is sent to a provider only when the tenant enables it.
- **D-47 Deploy artifacts.** Dockerfiles (api, worker, web/nginx), `compose.yaml` (web 8195, Postgres 5436), and Terraform under `deploy/terraform/gcp` (Cloud Run, Cloud SQL for PostgreSQL 17, Secret Manager, Artifact Registry, IAP-ready load balancing). Validate only; never apply.
- **D-48 Gates.** There is no hosted CI on GHES. `make ci` is the gate; `make test-pg`, `make parity`, `make answer-keys`, `make properties`, `make e2e` and `make perf` are the specialist gates (`docs/00-GOAL.md` §3).

## 5. Design

- **D-60 Look and feel.**
  - **Layout** follows the 2026-class RightRev structure documented in research 02: a top bar with an entity + period context pill and Cmd-K search; a collapsible icon rail; a record header with a KPI strip; flat tabs; an obligations list beside a detail pane; drill-down from any figure to contract, obligation, schedule line and source row; an Explain panel on every computed number.
  - **Craft:** Linear, Stripe Dashboard, Mercury, Ramp.
  - **Standards:** light and dark parity, WCAG 2.2 AA, comfortable and compact density.
  - **Accounting number formatting:** tabular numerals, right-aligned, fixed decimals per currency, one negative style, currency code in headers.
- **D-61 Brand.**
  - **Wordmark:** "eRev", with "by Chipmunk Robotics" only in About.
  - **Accent:** the designer chooses one confident accent. It must not be RightRev's green, and there must be no purple gradients, glass or neon.
  - **Status colours:** a fixed set, never used for decoration.
- **D-62 Anti-slop.** The rules of the `ui-must-not-look-ai-generated` standard apply. Avoid:
  - default Tailwind grey cards;
  - identical card grids as the main layout;
  - emoji;
  - centred hero copy inside the app;
  - decorative gradients.

  A design-check lint enforces the token-only colour rule.

## 6. Process

- **D-70 Build loop.** A Ralph loop builds `docs/BUILD_SPEC.md` in order. The supervisor steers it only through appended BUILD_SPEC items and new D-numbers, committed by pathspec between iterations. Reviews run in separate worktrees with their own databases (`erev_rv_*`), provisioned by the supervisor.
- **D-71 Answer keys first.** Engine items cite answer-key ids as acceptance. A capability is not ticked until its keys pass.

## 7. Amendments after design phase B1 (2026-09-12)

Inputs: `docs/reviews/B1-consistency.md` (39 findings), `docs/reviews/objections-*.md`, and the open questions of each B1 document. Where an amendment below conflicts with the original decision text, the amendment governs.

- **D-11a Posting composition (B1-001).**
  - Cumulative rounding rounds the exact cumulative amount: C_t = round(X × f_t). X is the exact (unrounded) allocation and f_t the exact cumulative progress. C_t is bounded by the posted allocation A (the largest-remainder allocation in minor units) and equals A at completion. This is `docs/accounting/POLICIES.md` ALG-01 §2.1.3 as published.
  - The legacy parity suite applies the same rule. `research-harness/deviations/build_deviations.py` gains posting rule `EXACT-CUM`, and `docs/legacy/golden/deviations.json` and `docs/legacy/DEVIATIONS.md` are regenerated under it.
- **D-13a Billing posting literals (B1-003).** The registry key is `billing.posting` with literals `ERP` (default) and `ENGINE` (POL-004). In D-13, `erp` and `engine` are descriptive. More generally, `docs/accounting/POLICIES.md` §1 keys and option literals are the only machine vocabulary for registry values.
- **D-14a Account roles (B1-015; OBJ-01, OBJ-PM-01, ra-policies O-1).**
  - Add for 1.0:
    - `COST_OF_REVENUE`: return-asset recognition and remeasurement; fulfilment-cost release where elected; zero-margin uninstalled materials.
    - `CONTRACT_COST_CLEARING`: the credit side of capitalizing costs to obtain or fulfil a contract.
    - `RECEIVABLE_CONTRA`: implicit price concessions and other receivable contras when `billing.posting = ENGINE`. The healthcare demo requires it, which departs from the review's default.
  - Lines and mapping rules with role `BILLING_CLEARING` carry `clearing_purpose` ∈ {`BILLING`, `UNAPPLIED_CASH`, `AP_SUPPLIER`, `INVENTORY`, `EQUITY`, `INVESTMENTS`}.
  - Reserve `RETAINED_EARNINGS` and `FINANCING_OBLIGATION` in the enum. No 1.0 posting uses them.
  - The enum therefore has 33 values.
- **D-17a Cent-difference class (O-2, B1-039).** Cent differences between legacy report rounding and posted amounts under D-11a form one deviation class, DEV-002. The revenue accountant approves the class once. The case list is regenerated mechanically by `build_deviations.py`.
- **D-21a Option SSP (O-2, B1-003).** SSP of an option = expected purchase amount × incremental discount (discount on exercise less the discount available without exercise) × likelihood of exercise (606-10-55-44), or the renewal practical alternative (55-45). The registry key is `material_right.exercise` ∈ {`CONTINUATION` (default), `MODIFICATION`}.
- **D-25a Refundable advances (O-3).** An approved contract-level override may treat refundable advance consideration as monetary, remeasured at the closing rate, in the ASC606 book. The IFRS15 book always applies IFRIC 22 (POL-163).
- **D-30a Findings (O-1, B1-019).** Legacy silent defects become explicit findings in the dry-run diff and the exception queue:
  - **blocking errors** where the input cannot be processed correctly, for example duplicate files or swallowed exceptions;
  - **warnings** where it can, for example blank memos (`PROGRESS_MEMO_BLANK`, rows processed).
- **D-40a SQLite (B1-034).** SQLite is never an application datastore or test database. Reading legacy `ASC606.db` files (D-31) and building SQLite fixtures from the golden CSVs are permitted.
- **D-48a Targets (B1-010).** Loop targets run offline and are defined in `docs/dev-guide.md` §4, including `make release-manifest`, `make perf-seed` and `make vocab-check`. Supervisor targets that need network or Docker are listed separately in that section and are never run by the loop: `make audit-deps`, `make zap-baseline`, `make docker-build`, `make compose-verify`, `make tf-validate`, `make backup`.
- **D-72 Mock adapters (B1-008).** Mock adapter servers mount in-process under `/api/v1/__mocks__/<adapter>`, and only when `EREV_ENV` ∈ {`dev`, `test`, `e2e`}. They are excluded from OpenAPI. No process binds a port outside `docs/00-GOAL.md` §5.
- **D-73 Identifier authority (B1-006, B1-007, B1-013, B1-014).** A precedence row is added: enumeration literals, permission codes, problem slugs, and finding and exception codes are owned by `docs/04-DATA_MODEL.md` (§3, T-PLT-11, §15.2, §15.4). Other documents cite them verbatim; UI labels are copy. For routes, PRD SF ids are stable, and `docs/design/SCREENS.md` owns the route path of each SF id.
- **D-74 Split contracts.** Two contracts are split across two files each:
  - the engine specification: `docs/accounting/ENGINE_SPEC.md` (index and stages 01–08) and `docs/accounting/ENGINE_SPEC_B.md` (stages 09–15);
  - the screen specifications: `docs/design/SCREENS.md` (index, IA, core workbench) and `docs/design/SCREENS_B.md` (close, reports, admin, AI, onboarding).

  Each pair has one precedence rank. The index file lists every section in both files.
- **D-75 Rulings on B1 open questions.** Unless listed below, every recommended default in these open-question lists is adopted:
  - B1-consistency §8 (Q1–Q13);
  - 03 §11 (Q1–Q3);
  - 04 §20 (OQ-01–OQ-07);
  - POLICIES OQ-01–OQ-12;
  - DEVIATIONS OQ-D1–OQ-D10;
  - PRD §9 (Q1–Q9);
  - 05 OQ-ARC-01–09;
  - DG-OQ-01–10;
  - DS OQ-01–05.

  Specific rulings:

  | Question | Ruling |
  |---|---|
  | Ratable conventions (B1-004, Q3) | POL-090 and E-21 are `DAILY`, `MONTHLY_EVEN` (replaces `MONTHLY_WHOLE_MONTHS`; whole-month terms unchanged) and `MID_MONTH`, each with CHK ids |
  | Job queues (Q5) | The eight queues of `docs/05-ARCHITECTURE.md` §5.6; workers consume all queues by default |
  | Performance tenant (Q7) | Persistent tenant `perf-volume` in database `erev`, built by an idempotent `make perf-seed` through month 23 closed; `make perf` runs the month-24 close in a sandbox copy |
  | Local keys (Q8) | Master keys in `.env` through `EnvSecretStore` with per-tenant HKDF; `LocalKeyProvider` is an adapter over the same protocol |
  | Tenant provisioning (Q9) | Add REQ-PLT-038 (P0, 1.0): `erev tenant create` CLI plus route |
  | Upload limits (Q10) | Per-purpose limits of 04 T-PLT-29 |
  | G4 coverage lists (Q11) | Research 05 §26 scenarios of the six 1.0 demo clusters, plus research 04 §1–§13 topics, plus every CHK id in POLICIES |
  | Reopen approvers (Q12) | Grant `period.reopen_approve` to Revenue Reviewer; routing keeps at least one Controller |
  | M-RA-04 industries (Q13) | Recorded as `later` |
  | CONTRACT_LIABILITY as control role (POLICIES OQ-05) | Contract asset and unbilled receivable are presentation balances derived at period end. The netting reclass (D-13) is still posted as an auto-reversing journal |
  | Negative number style (PRD Q8, B1-030) | Tenant setting; default parentheses |
  | Demo users (PRD Q1, B1-011) | Named persona users `<name>@demo.erev`; password only via `EREV_DEMO_PASSWORD` (default `Demo1234!Demo` in `.env.example`) |
  | Mock ports (05 OQ-ARC-07) | Superseded by D-72: no mock ports |
  | Copyright (03 Q2) | `Copyright (c) 2025-2026 ChipmunkRPA`; About shows "eRev by Chipmunk Robotics" |
  | Single transaction currency per combination group (03 Q3) | Accepted for 1.0 |

## 8. Amendments after design phase B2 (2026-09-12)

Inputs: the B2 fix reports `docs/reviews/B2-fix-*.md`, `docs/reviews/objections-engine-spec-b.md`, the open-question sections of ENGINE_SPEC / ENGINE_SPEC_B / SCREENS / SCREENS_B, and the three answer-key coverage files in `docs/accounting/answer-keys/_coverage/`. Open-question ids collide across documents, so they are cited as `<document>:<id>` (for example `ENGINE_SPEC_B:OQ-B-09`, `SCREENS_B:OQ-B-07`).

- **D-25b Monetary liabilities (OBJ-B-01).** Refund liabilities, deposit liabilities and consideration payable to a customer that are denominated in a currency other than the entity's functional currency are monetary items. They are remeasured at the closing rate at every period end and at settlement, against `FX_GAIN_LOSS`, in every book (ASC 830-20-35-1; IAS 21.16, 21.23(a)).
  - POLICIES adds JET-10d, and POL-164 becomes `ASSET_POSITIONS_ENGINE_AR_AND_MONETARY_LIABILITIES`.
  - Stage 12 remeasures these balances. E-86 gains `LIABILITY_LAYER_REMEASURED`.
  - Contract liabilities stay non-monetary (D-25, D-25a).
- **D-76 Rulings on B2 open questions.** Unless listed below, every recommended default is adopted in all of:
  - `DEVIATIONS:OQ-D11`; `03:§11 Q4`; `PRD:§9 Q10–Q14`; `04:OQ-08–OQ-12`; `dev-guide:DG-OQ-12–13`; `05:OQ-ARC-10–14`;
  - `POLICIES:OQ-13–15`; `DESIGN_SYSTEM:OQ-06–07`;
  - `ENGINE_SPEC:OQ-A-01–24`; `ENGINE_SPEC_B:OQ-B-01–24`;
  - `_coverage/answer-keys-topics:OQ-AKT-*`, `_coverage/answer-keys-industries:OQ-AKI-*`, `_coverage/answer-keys-fx-entity-books:OQ-AK-*`;
  - `SCREENS:OQ-S-01–21` and API gaps `SCREENS:R-01–R-40`; `SCREENS_B:OQ-B-01–32`.

  | Topic | Ruling |
  |---|---|
  | Financing interest in the net position (`ENGINE_SPEC_B:OQ-B-09`, `OQ-AK-04`, industries D-5) | Interest accreted under JET-11 is part of the carrying amount and therefore of the net position (ALG-02, CHK-137). 05 EMOD-18 and REQ-BIL-007 are amended to include it |
  | Significant financing component rate (`OQ-AK-05`, `OQ-AKT-13`, `ENGINE_SPEC:OQ-A-16`) | `sfc.discount_rate_basis` gains member `compounding` ∈ {`MONTHLY` (default), `ANNUAL`}. MONTHLY uses annual_rate / 12 with cumulative rounding. CHK-136 is re-baselined to the MONTHLY values; the ANNUAL variant stays as a second CHK row |
  | CHK re-baselines (`OQ-AKT-01`, `OQ-AK-02`) | CHK-053 and the CHK-006 rows S2-WARRANTY-OWN, S5-EX63-OWNSSP and S12-FRANCHISOR-OWN are re-baselined to D-11a values |
  | Contract-cost impairment (industries D-6) | The impairment test includes consideration from anticipated renewals and extensions (ASC 340-40-35-4 as amended by ASU 2016-20). 05 EMOD-16 is amended |
  | Uninstalled materials (industries D-7, `POLICIES:OQ-15`) | The ERP posts the cost. The engine recognizes revenue equal to cost (zero margin) and posts no COST_OF_REVENUE line. REQ-REC-007 is amended |
  | Workbench tabs and KPI strip (`SCREENS:OQ-S-01`, `OQ-S-02`) | SCREENS placement governs; REQ-UX-004 is amended to match |
  | Schema and API additions | 04 adopts, verbatim or with mechanical naming alignment: `SCREENS:R-01–R-40`; the API additions in `SCREENS_B:OQ-B-*`; `ENGINE_SPEC:OQ-A-06–13` (warranty cost rate, noncash consideration, consideration payable, payment schedule, observable point, proposed codes); `ENGINE_SPEC_B:OQ-B-*` literals (BILL_AND_HOLD_CRITERIA_UNMET, INVOICE_STATUS_UPDATE_MISMATCH, E-56 BILL_AND_HOLD, E-29 RECEIVABLE_CONTRA, E-86 LIABILITY_LAYER_REMEASURED, JET-09f, JET-10c, cost_adjustment CLAWBACK, EAC uninstalled_materials_cost); `05:OQ-ARC-12` codes; `POLICIES:OQ-13–14` |
  | Answer-key schema (DG §9.5) | Adopts the inputs the three corpora need: estimate `parameters`; `world.portfolios`; observable point; `scope_605_35`; billing plan; noncash consideration; consideration payable; warranty cost rate; per-book judgements; tax lines; renewal handle; report cell keys. The loader ignores `_coverage/`. CHK containment in key ids is case-insensitive. Timelines order by (effective_date, seq) |
  | Duplicate keys across slices (`OQ-AKT-18`) | Keep. The B3 adjudicator withdraws true duplicates and keeps variants with distinct inputs |
  | Perf protocol (`05:OQ-ARC-10`) | Perf-worker processes and the PERF-01 step list; DG-PERF-03 remains the pass criterion |
  | Loop network use (`dev-guide:DG-OQ-13`) | Dependency installation in `make setup` is the only network use permitted to the loop |
  | RPO bands (`03:§11 Q4`) | POL-201 governs; DS-CH-03 renders the bands the API returns |

- **D-77 Design freeze after B3.**
  - **B3 fix authors:** apply D-25b and D-76. They do not open new open questions except genuine blockers. They take the most reasonable default and log it under "Decisions taken in B3" in their revision log.
  - **After B3 completes:** the design documents and the answer-key corpus are frozen. Gaps found during the build are raised as **Spec questions** in `PROGRESS.md`. The supervisor resolves them only through appended BUILD_SPEC supervisor items and new D-numbers (D-70).

## 9. Rulings after the phase FND review (2026-09-12)

Input: `docs/reviews/loop/review-FND.md`, the adversarial review of FND-1..FND-18 at 374edb3, run in an isolated worktree on the `erev_rv_*` databases. It found no P1 findings, 7 P2 and 15 P3. The remediation is BUILD_SPEC supervisor items EKC-5a, EKC-5b and EKC-5c. Where a ruling below differs from `docs/dev-guide.md` text, the ruling governs.

- **D-78 FND review rulings.**

  | Topic | Ruling |
  |---|---|
  | Gate integrity (FR-G-01, FR-G-02, FR-G-04) | `make ci` takes no narrowing variables: `TESTS`, `K` and `FRONTEND` are refused before the first stage. A gate whose JUnit summary is missing fails. DG-TST-07 and DG-TST-09 apply to all five markers (`parity`, `answer_key`, `property`, `pg`, `perf`), and `make test-pg` fails on any skipped test. This overrules the narrower readings in SPEC-Q-11 and SPEC-Q-45 |
  | Engine test isolation (FR-C-01) | DG-TST-18 is enforced at collection in `backend/tests/engine/conftest.py` |
  | Ports in review worktrees (FR-B-01, FR-C-03) | `scripts/proc.sh start` checks readiness on the port it started. `make backend` and `make frontend` honour `EREV_API_PORT` (default 8190) |
  | Build imports (FR-B-03) | `make build` imports `erev_api.worker` whenever it exists |
  | Partition immutability (FR-M-01) | DB-01 truncate triggers extend to every child partition of IM-A and IM-S parents. DB-14 (g) checks partitions |
  | Database allow-list (FR-S-01, FR-M-06) | DG-ENV-13 refuses the URL query parameters `dbname`, `service` and `servicefile`. The connected database must equal the URL's database. One shared pattern |
  | `identity_session` importers (FR-C-04) | DG-KRN-DB-02 is amended: `backend/erev_api/auth/**`, `backend/erev_api/api/v1/health.py` and `backend/erev_api/db/lint.py` only |
  | Database administration rule (FR-M-03) | DG-ENV-14 and DG-ARC-05 forbid `CREATE`, `ALTER` or `DROP` of a database, role, user, group, extension or tablespace, plus `ALTER SYSTEM`. The ITGC guide states exactly this |
  | Engine purity (FR-C-02, FR-G-03) | The purity scanner resolves import aliases for clock reads |
  | Runbook (FR-M-04) | A failed hosted migration is rolled back by clone and cutover at the pre-migration backup point (05 OPR-11, OPR-16) |
  | Hosted keys (FR-S-04, FR-S-03) | `GcpKeyProvider` wraps and unwraps DEKs through Cloud KMS `encrypt`/`decrypt`, with the canonical context as AAD. It never reads the KEK into memory. The envelope blob carries a 2-byte big-endian wrapped-DEK length (DG-KRN-KEY-04 amended; SPEC-Q-15 overruled). Tests may exercise the GCP providers with fake clients and no network (DG-ENV-17 amended). `KeyProvider.secret` refuses master-key names |
  | Logs (FR-S-02, FR-S-06, FR-S-07) | SQLAlchemy engines use `hide_parameters=True`. Unhandled database errors log class, SQLSTATE and constraint, never the message. Secret keys are dropped at every depth, and passphrases are redacted in full. uvicorn loggers are bridged into the structlog pipeline |
  | Money strings (FR-S-05) | `MoneyStr`, `DecimalStr` and `RateStr` accept ASCII digits only (04 API-C-06) |
  | CORS (FR-S-09) | `create_app` installs the 05 SAR-12 CORS middleware from `EREV_CORS_ORIGINS`, and refuses `*` or a malformed origin at app creation |

## 10. Rulings after the phase EKC review (2026-09-12)

Input: `docs/reviews/loop/review-EKC.md`, the adversarial review of EKC-1..EKC-12 and EKC-7a..7c at 94e54d7 in an isolated worktree.
- Findings: no P1, 6 P2, 8 P3.
- A Fraction oracle matched the money kernel with 0 mismatches.
- All 23 FND remediation findings are verified.
- Remediation: BUILD_SPEC supervisor items PLF-3a, PLF-3b and PLF-3c.

- **D-79 EKC review rulings.**

  | Topic | Ruling |
  |---|---|
  | Posting-period search (ER-N-01; SPEC-Q-79 overruled) | The engine helper `first_open_period_on_or_after` searches `open`, `closing` and `reopened` periods, as ENGINE_SPEC S08-R-08, 05 RCP-04, CV-13 and DB-07 require. dev-guide DG-KRN-TIME-04 is amended to `POSTABLE_STATES` |
  | MID_MONTH completion (ER-C-03; SPEC-Q-78 overruled) | `time_fraction` returns 1 on and after the term end only under `DAILY` and `MONTHLY_EVEN`. `MID_MONTH` evaluates POLICIES ALG-11 at every date. The EKC-4 `test_time_fraction_edges` wording is amended accordingly |
  | Expected problems in answer keys (ER-C-02; SPEC-Q-108 overruled) | An item carrying `expect_problem` enters only the bundle of its own step, which must raise `EngineError` with that code. It never enters a checkpoint bundle or a later step bundle. Its CV-15 findings are the checkpoint's `ERROR` exception rows. dev-guide §9.5.5 is amended |
  | Returns basis identity (ER-C-01; SPEC-Q-112 completed) | DG-AK-54 identity 1 compares node `tp_allocation_basis:<group>:-` with Σ obligation `allocated_amount` − `contract_version.expected_returns_amount` (Σ a_posted) |
  | Key selection (ER-G-01; SPEC-Q-106, SPEC-Q-113 completed) | Selection runs over every key. Withdrawn keys are validated and reported but never collected, and a selection holding no active key fails the run |
  | PROP:P4 (ER-N-02) | A cumulative value is exempt from the half-unit bound only when the bound binds: for A ≥ 0, C_t = 0 with X × f_t × 10^μ < 0, or C_t = A with X × f_t × 10^μ > A; symmetrically for A < 0. This amends the EKC-7 and ENC-10 P4 test wording |
  | Engine purity (ER-S-03, extends D-78) | DG-ARC-02 also forbids the bare calls `eval`, `exec`, `compile`, `globals`, `vars`, `breakpoint` and `input`; the name `__builtins__`; `now`, `today`, `utcnow`, `fromtimestamp` and `utcfromtimestamp` on any owner; and `getattr` of those names |
  | Float guard determinism (ER-S-06) | `no_floats` walks set members in a deterministic order (DG-ENG-02) |
  | Checkpoint assertions (ER-C-05, ER-G-02) | Every `BookInput` needs a `BookOutput`, and every book except LEGACY needs a `contract_version`. Every optional comparison block has a test that can fail |
  | Gate integrity (ER-G-03, ER-G-04, ER-G-05; extends D-78) | `make properties` fails on any skipped test (DG-TST-09, G5). `make ci` refuses every command-line variable. The root collection hook and coverage guards have regression tests |
  | Registry seed compound approval codes (ER-S-02, P3) | Deferred: the first code is kept until a T-PLT-31 amendment defines storage for compound codes. It is recorded as a supervisor verification item |

## 11. Rulings after the phase PLF review (2026-09-13)

Input: `docs/reviews/loop/review-PLF.md`, the adversarial review of PLF-1..PLF-30 and PLF-3a..3c at 0eb193d.
- Findings: 4 P1 (MFA brute force; cross-tenant MFA reset through invitations; unserialised SoD approval; unanchored audit-chain verification), 15 P2, 6 P3.
- All 14 EKC remediation findings were verified.
- Remediation: BUILD_SPEC supervisor items WEB-3a, WEB-3b, WEB-3c and WEB-3d.

Where a ruling below differs from dev-guide, 04 or 05 text, the ruling governs (as under D-78 and D-79). The PLF-15 `test_change_password` wording ("keeps the current session") is amended by the password-change row.

- **D-80 PLF review rulings.**

  | Topic | Ruling | Departs from |
  |---|---|---|
  | MFA challenge limits (PR-A-01) | 1. `POST /session/mfa` is admitted through the SAR-13 login buckets, keyed by client IP and the session user's normalised email. The admission runs before verification. A refused attempt returns 429 `rate-limited` with `Retry-After` and writes no `MFA_CHALLENGE_FAILED`.<br>2. A wrong recovery code counts as one challenge.<br>3. Five `MFA_CHALLENGE_FAILED` events for a user after the later of the factor's last successful verification (`user_mfa_factor.updated_at`) and `app_user.locked_until` lock the account for 15 minutes: `locked_until` is set and `ACCOUNT_LOCKED` is written, and the fifth failure returns 423 `account-locked`.<br>4. While the account is locked, the route returns 423 without verifying anything.<br>5. A password sign-in does not restart the count | 05 SAR-06 (lockout counted password failures only); NFR-43 is read as covering MFA |
  | MFA reset and invitations of existing identities (PR-Z-01, PR-Z-02, PR-C-04) | 1. `POST /users/{id}/reset-mfa` needs membership `ACTIVE` or `SUSPENDED`; otherwise it returns 409 `invalid-transition`.<br>2. A target whose `app_user.is_operator` is true returns 404 `not-found`.<br>3. `POST /users` refuses an operator email with 422 `validation-failed` on `email`, rule `T-PLT-02`, copy "This email cannot be invited to a workspace."<br>4. The reset writes `user_mfa_factor.reset` in every ACTIVE membership's tenant.<br>5. Until the membership is accepted, the API-S-User of an `INVITED` membership whose `app_user` was not created by that invitation shows `display_name` = the email, `last_login_at` null and `mfa_enrolled` null.<br>6. `PATCH /users/{id}` renames a user only when the invitation created the `app_user` row (`app_user.created_at = tenant_membership.invited_at` and `app_user.created_by = tenant_membership.created_by`); otherwise it returns 409 `invalid-transition` | 04 API-R-05 (no status precondition); SPEC-Q-182 (b), (c), (d) |
  | Audit chain anchoring (PR-K-01) | 1. `verify_tenant_chain` reads `audit_chain_head` in the same transaction. It fails with reason "the chain ends before the head", and `first_failure_seq` = last checked seq + 1, when the last checked `chain_seq` or `hmac` differs from the head.<br>2. `record_tenant_verification` also fails, with reason "a verified event is missing or changed", when the event at the latest earlier PASS row's `to_chain_seq` is missing or its `hmac` differs from that row's `digest_last_hmac`.<br>3. DB-09 gains `tg_audit_chain_head__guard` (BEFORE UPDATE). It refuses with `EREV-AUD-001` any update not issued from inside `tg_audit_event__chain` (`pg_trigger_depth() < 2`), and any update that does not set `last_chain_seq = OLD.last_chain_seq + 1` | dev-guide DG-KRN-AUD-07; 04 DB-09, T-PLT-22 (IM-X free update) |
  | One-time secrets and replay (PR-A-02) | The stored response of `POST /api-clients`, `POST /api-clients/{id}/rotate-secret` and `POST /webhook-endpoints` carries `client_secret` or `signing_secret` as null. A replay returns that stored body with `Idempotent-Replay: true`, and the schemas declare the member nullable. The value appears only in the first response | dev-guide DG-KRN-IDEM-03, DG-KRN-IDEM-04 |
  | Password change (PR-A-03) | "Keeps the current session" (04 §16.12; PLF-15) means the user stays signed in under a rotated token (SAR-09). The presented session ends `REVOKED`, and the 204 carries the new `erev_session` cookie | BUILD_SPEC PLF-15 `test_change_password` wording; the loop's `test_me_password.py:82-84` |
  | Outbound destinations (PR-A-04, PR-R-08) | 1. Outside `dev`, `test` and `e2e`, the guard refuses every resolved address that is not global unicast (`is_global` false, `is_multicast`, `is_reserved` or `is_unspecified`). NAT64 (`64:ff9b::/96`), 6to4 (`2002::/16`), IPv4-mapped and IPv4-compatible addresses are judged by their embedded IPv4 address.<br>2. OIDC discovery, JWKS and token requests, and SMTP connections, pass through the guard and connect to the checked address.<br>3. `_issuer_valid` and `erev idp create` take the environment and refuse http or loopback issuers under `production` (rule `T-PLT-03`) | 05 SAR-15 (enumerated ranges) |
  | Configuration inserts (PR-K-03) | `erev.tg_config_version()` refuses an INSERT with `status <> 'DRAFT'` unless `current_setting('app.platform_scope', true) = 'provisioning'` (`EREV-CFG-002`) | 04 DB-04; SPEC-Q-151 |
  | SoD rules as of a date (PR-C-05) | 1. For instant `at`, a code's rule is its PUBLISHED version with `published_at <= at`, or its SUPERSEDED version with `published_at <= at < effective_to`. A provisioning seed without `published_at` counts from the tenant's `created_at`.<br>2. `conflict_report(as_of)` counts assignments with `valid_from <= as_of` and (`revoked_at` null or `revoked_at > as_of`).<br>3. Commands keep `at = now` | dev-guide DG-KRN-PERM-03; SPEC-Q-150 |
  | Dispatch and job recovery (PR-R-01 to PR-R-04) | 1. Webhook deliveries and outbox messages are claimed and committed one at a time, immediately before dispatch. The record step matches the claim stamp, and a lost claim settles without raising.<br>2. The OUTBOX_RELAY, EMAIL_DELIVERY, WEBHOOK_DELIVERY and AUDIT_CHAIN_VERIFY handlers heartbeat after each message, delivery or 1,000 verified events.<br>3. `run_job` records completion only on the RUNNING row of its own attempt.<br>4. The sweeper re-dispatches QUEUED jobs whose Procrastinate task is `failed`, `cancelled` or `aborted`, and retries tasks stalled in `doing` through `JobManager.get_stalled_jobs` (Procrastinate 3.9.0).<br>5. `_defer_unless_pending` counts only QUEUED rows whose task is `todo` or `doing` | dev-guide DG-KRN-EVT-05, DG-KRN-JOB-06; SPEC-Q-187 (b), SPEC-Q-189 (c) |
  | Upload bodies (PR-R-06) | 1. The middleware counts body bytes while streaming and never holds more than one received message.<br>2. `POST /api/v1/files` without `Content-Length` returns 422 `validation-failed` with `errors[0].rule_id = "API-C-17"` before reading the body | 04 API-C-17 (silent on chunked uploads) |
  | Launcher ports (PR-R-07, S-2) | 1. Make and `scripts/proc.sh` take `EREV_API_PORT`, `EREV_WEB_PORT`, `EREV_E2E_API_PORT` and `EREV_E2E_WEB_PORT` from the environment, else from the `.env` file named by `EREV_DOTENV` (default `.env`), else the defaults. Values are never printed.<br>2. Make passes `EREV_WEB_PORT` to the web process, which Vite reads (DG-RUN-21) | dev-guide DG-MK-dev-up, DG-RUN-03, DG-RUN-21; D-78 port row |

- **D-80a Spec-question rulings (PLF).**

  | Spec question | Loop's call | Ruling |
  |---|---|---|
  | SPEC-Q-190 (b), SPEC-Q-189 (b) | The idempotency store keeps the one-time 201 body, secret included, for replay | **Overruled** by REQ-SEC-003, SAR-18 and T-PLT-15 ("shown once"). The stored and replayed body carries the secret member as null (D-80) |
  | SPEC-Q-192 (f) | The issuer URL form is checked at creation only; loopback http is always allowed | **Overruled** by SAR-15. Every provider URL passes `check_destination` at request time, and loopback or http issuers are refused under `production` (D-80) |
  | SPEC-Q-182 (c) | A known email reuses the `app_user`, keeps its name, and API-S-User shows its global state | **Partly overruled** by THR-07 and PRV-01. Before acceptance, an invitation of a pre-existing user exposes no global identity data, and only an invitation that created the user may rename it (D-80) |
  | SPEC-Q-182 (d) | The MFA reset is audited in the acting workspace only | **Overruled** by 04 T-PLT-04: the reset is written to each ACTIVE membership's audit log |
  | SPEC-Q-151 | A non-DRAFT INSERT with `content_sha256` is allowed in any session | **Overruled** by DB-04 and IM-P ("only along E-12"). A non-DRAFT INSERT is allowed only under `app.platform_scope = 'provisioning'`, as SPEC-Q-175 already does for child rows (D-80) |
  | SPEC-Q-150 (with SPEC-Q-184 a) | Only PUBLISHED rows count, at any date | **Overruled** by REQ-PLT-010 "as of any date". A SUPERSEDED version counts for instants in `[published_at, effective_to)`, and the report counts assignments in force at `as_of` (D-80) |
  | SPEC-Q-187 (b) | The sweeper neither retries nor finishes a dead worker's Procrastinate task | **Overruled** by 05 JOB-06 part one and SCH-04. Failed, cancelled, aborted and stalled tasks of QUEUED jobs are re-dispatched (D-80) |
  | SPEC-Q-189 (c) | A 120 s claim lease means no attempt is posted twice | **Corrected**: this holds only if the claim covers one delivery at a time. Deliveries and outbox messages are claimed one at a time immediately before dispatch (D-80) |
  | SPEC-Q-128 | `common-passwords.txt` is the user's generator output | **Weak, not overruled.** The supervisor supplies a frequency-ranked list with a recorded licence before REL; this goes on the supervisor verification register |

## 12. Tonight sprint to 1.0-rc (2026-09-13)

Ray asked to cut iterations and finish tonight. The serial loop was at 77 of 427 items after about 26 hours, so the supervisor switches to a parallel sprint toward a release candidate.

- **D-81 Sprint rules.**
  - **Scope.** Tonight's release candidate is the `in_scope` list of `docs/build-spec/sprint/plan.json`: the prerequisite closure of the MVP capabilities (engine stages for legacy parity and SaaS basics; reference data; contracts; legacy template imports; journal runs and CSV export; waterfall, balance rollforward and RPO reports; the MVP screens; container images and compose). Every other item stays in `docs/BUILD_SPEC.md` as the post-rc backlog, listed in `plan.json` `deferred`.
  - **Gates for the rc.** The `release_gate` of `plan.json` defines which answer-key families, golden kinds and smoke e2e specs must pass. `make ci` and `make test-pg` must be green on a clean tree. `docs/00-GOAL.md` G1–G12 remain the gates of 1.0 final.
  - **Build mode.**
    - Lanes build concurrently in worktrees `~/dev/erev-wt/<lane>` on branches `sprint/<level>-<lane>`.
    - Each lane has its own databases `erev_rv_l<n>_{dev,test,e2e}` and review ports from the plan; ports come from the lane `.env` `EREV_*_PORT` values (D-70, D-78, D-80).
    - At the end of each level the supervisor merges lanes into `main` in the plan's `merge_order` and runs the merge gate.
    - Only one lane per level adds migrations.
  - **Ceremony.**
    - After each item: targeted tests, lint and typecheck.
    - `make ci` and `make test-pg` run once per batch, before the batch's last commit.
    - Evidence and spec questions go to `docs/reviews/loop/sprint/<lane>.md`, not `PROGRESS.md`. The merge step ticks items in `PROGRESS.md`.
    - In-scope GATE items are replaced by the per-level merge gate. There are no mid-sprint adversarial reviews; one final review with browser QA runs after the last merge.
  - **Integration after merge.** An item that consumes an interface built in the same level codes against the documented contract (ENGINE_SPEC §0.5, 04 API schemas) with fakes. The merge gate integrates it, and a failing integration becomes a fix batch in the next level.

- **D-82 Sprint target and rulings (answers to SPRINT-1.0rc.md §8).**

  | Decision | Ruling |
  |---|---|
  | Target | Variant **V-ABC** (about 39 h, 144 items). Level L1 runs as validated; levels from L2 onward follow the re-validated V-ABC plan (`docs/build-spec/sprint/plan-vabc.json`) |
  | R-RC-1 | **Approved.** The listed acceptance fragments are hidden or moved post-rc, and each consuming item records them as a spec question under XR-14. That covers seeded close-history captures, NetSuite acknowledgements and as-locked views; the CTR-22 void and regroup drawers; the CTR-23 billing-plan and cost panels; CTR-20 K-06 and K-09; the RPS-3 and RPS-4 fragments; and the CLO-11 reopened-period test. The moved items are CTR-11, CTR-14, CTR-17, DIN-12, DIN-14, EDS-4, EDS-5, EDS-6, CLO-5, CLO-6, CLO-7, CLO-14 to CLO-20 and CLO-22 |
  | V-A merge re-parenting | **Approved.** Two lanes per level may add Alembic revisions. At merge, the second lane's revisions are re-parented onto the first (`down_revision` edit and `NNNN_` renumber), and `make test-pg` must pass `test_single_head` and `test_upgrade_downgrade_upgrade` |
  | V-C | **Approved.** Platform consumers of engine functions code against ENGINE_SPEC §0.5 with fakes, as integration-after-merge |
  | SUP-RC-SMOKE | **Approved.** Appended as a supervisor item in the last level. It replaces J-01 for the rc; J-01 stays in the post-rc backlog |

- **D-82a Ruling on V-ABC validation VV-03 (CSV v2 progress template).** DIN-9 (modern CSV v2 templates and invoice ingestion) moves into rc scope, in the Level 5 import lane directly after DIN-4, since they share the importer. DIN-15, DIN-16, DIN-17 and SUP-RC-SMOKE are therefore not partial for this reason. RC-SMOKE.4 uses the CSV v2 progress upload shown in SCREENS §12.2 (open item OI-2 resolved).

- **D-83 Rulings after sprint Level 3.**

  | Question | Ruling |
  |---|---|
  | Answer-key products without `principal_agent` (L3-2-Q-18) | The answer-key runner resolves a key product that omits `principal_agent` as `PRINCIPAL`, because the keys' figures assume gross revenue. Keys stay frozen. Platform products keep the 04 T-REF-20 default `NOT_ASSESSED`, and S03-R-09 still blocks them |
  | Sign-in and `GET /me` without an active tenant (L3-3-Q-10) | Password and MFA sign-in open the user's workspace when the user has exactly one ACTIVE membership, or the membership marked default. Otherwise the session stays tenantless and the client shows workspace selection. `GET /me` answers a tenantless session with 200 and the membership list instead of 401 |

There is no gap test; a missing rate still raises `SFC_RATE_MISSING`. T: over time as today; point in time `start_date`, else the delivery at which net delivered reaches the line quantity, else open (ADVANCE accretion with no relief; no CSP published while open). ADVANCE CSP for every schedule: X = Σ pay_i·v^{n_i} ÷ Σ_k g_k·v^k (v = 1/(1+j); g_k the obligation's exact revenue share at the k-th month end; point in time g = 1 at m(start, T)); B_k = B_{k−1}(1+j) + pay_k − X·g_k; the balance settles to 0 at the last revenue month (no midpoint zeroing). Must reproduce SFC-FS-11-CASEB X 295,701.23, SFC-S3-EX29 4,020.00 / 4,246.71 / 4,508.64, SFC-CHK-136 4,494.40, JE-CHK-136 4,508.64, with SFC-FS-11-CASEA (not significant) still 0 and the passing schedule keys unchanged | L7-5 (`s04_transaction_price/financing.py`) |

CHANGE otherwise (ΔQ = quantity − quantity in force). - **D-87b Probe `detail` comparison and lane lock contention (2026-09-16; L7-1-Q-1).**
  - Probe P3 `detail`. `deviations.json` holds legacy prose ("Contract 1 POB #1 Hardware 1: delivery 7 exceeds remaining quantity 5"), while the platform stores the PRD IMP-22 copy with the DIN-7 location prefix, which SCREENS J-04-ALT-1 asserts. DEVIATIONS.md §7.3 requires only that the detail name `Contract 1 POB #1 Hardware 1`, requested 7 and remaining 5. Ruling (a): for `legacy_probe` only, DG-PAR-10 `detail` passes when the stored finding message names the expected aggregated key's contract (`Contract 1`), obligation (`POB #1`) and product (`Hardware 1`) as whole tokens, plus "requested <n>" and "remaining <m>" with the expected quantities. The runner parses the expected detail `<contract> <pob> <product>: delivery <n> exceeds remaining quantity <m>` and fails closed on any other shape. DG-PAR-07's equal-text rule stands for every other text. Expected values are never edited, so option (b) is refused; option (c) contradicts the PRD. Owner: `backend/tests/support/parity/probes.py` (lane L7-1). If the L7 merge lands without it, the supervisor implements it on main before the release gate is declared.
  - Lock contention. Lane `make ci` runs fail when five lanes run migration suites at once: `test_doctor` and `test_upgrade_downgrade_upgrade` exhaust max_locks_per_transaction on the shared server, and the half-migrated test database cascades. Lane ci results under contention are noise when the failures trace to lock exhaustion; the L7 merge gate on main, run alone, is authoritative. Server settings stay unchanged (D-85a). Post-rc hardening, as D-85 did for e2e: the migration tests drop partitioned tables one per transaction.

- **D-89b P12 property after D-87 L6-5-Q-26 (2026-09-17; L7 release gate).** The L7 release gate on main (f5dd350) failed `make properties` on P12 (`test_prop_p12_fx_layers`). A diagnosis workflow (investigator and adversarial verifier) reproduced it and classified it as an out-of-date property.
  - What happens: a refund-liability release of 64 settles a contract-asset layer of 63 first and creates the contract-liability layer for the remaining 1 at 119,824 − 117,951 = 1,873 (spot 239647/128). P12 expects round(1 × spot) = 1,872. The engine follows D-87 L6-5-Q-26 (merged 099bf88): the GL and the layers tie with no residue. The pre-099bf88 path gives 1,872 but brings back the one-unit residue.
  - Ruling: change the property, not the engine. Owner is lane L8-E, in the same batch as D-88 L7-6-Q-2, as `docs/reviews/loop/sprint/L7-merge.md` section P12 states.
    - A contract-liability layer created by a release is checked against the release carrying less the asset settlements, bounded against spot by the number of layers involved.
    - With L7-6-Q-2, release settlements are checked by the per-source tie.
  - ENGINE_SPEC_B S12-R-20 and POLICIES ALG-08 §2.9.1 ("at that spot amount") are amended to the release carrying. S12-INV-01 and S12-INV-02 stand.

JET-05c credits REFUND_LIABILITY per obligation (ENGINE_SPEC_B Table 14-A, JET-05c subject obligation; S14-R-13; D-87 L6-5-Q-10; D-88 L7-5-Q-4 keeps JET-05c parts per obligation), and the engine posts POB-001 Cr 33.34, POB-002 Cr 33.33, POB-003 Cr 33.33 at FY2026-P02. The key's `after-credit` block (grain `role_account_obligation`, match `exact`) listed one REFUND_LIABILITY 2110 Cr 100.00 line without `obligation_key`, because the topics oracle `sat_credit_oracle` (`research-harness/answer-keys/answer-keys-topics/specs_mr_mod.py`) posted the credit side without `cr_obl`.

Revenue debits, balances and the exact-grain checks are unchanged; the summary line keeps its unsigned wording, mirrored in the oracle.
    - This line is read as amended against D-77 (keys change only through new D-numbers; this is that D-number), D-83, D-84 and D-87, the D-89a membership and the ADJUDICATION status of the key (revision row 1.4).
    - Rejected: a runner reading that sums an undimensioned expected line over obligations (it would weaken the exact grain merely to pass), and leaving the key out of the selection (the engine is right; only the representation is wrong). Precedent for a harness-driven key change: 8a8fe9e (regeneration before the sprint levels; no key has changed since).
    - The D-85 run note quotes the first REFUND_LIABILITY credit line (cr 33.34), not the total.
    - Owners: supervisor (oracle, key, ADJUDICATION); runner lane (the `test_runner_engine.py` docstring and note expectation).
  - **RET-BR-03 answer-key runner contract attribution.** Not an engine defect. Stage 14 posts JET-04b one entry per refund-liability component with subject `<group>@<entity>/<KIND>/<source>` (refund_liability.py), and its lines carry `dimensions.contract_key` (intents.py `_dimensions`; S14-R-13). The runner's `_CheckpointComparison.aggregate` attributed intents with `_subject_parts(subject_key)[0]`, a CV-21 parser that yields `CG-<contract>@<entity>` for a component key, so every JET-04b entry left the contract-filtered blocks (the 10 RET-BR-03 mismatches: March 2,000; May 12,000; June 12,000 and 1,600; August 400). Ruling:
    - The runner attributes an intent to a contract by the `contract_key` its lines carry. Attribution fails closed: every line agrees, the value is a member of the checkpoint's combination group, and it does not contradict a contract embedded in the subject or in a component source. An intent that cannot be attributed is reported as a mismatch through `subledger()` (never dropped silently, never reported twice).
    - Unfiltered blocks are unchanged. The rule covers `runner: engine`; platform-runner blocks are untouched.
    - dev-guide §9.5.6 records the rule, citing S14-R-13 and intents.py `_dimensions` as the source of `contract_key`.
    - Tests: combined group, group code equal to a member id, non-member head in a singleton group, group@entity FX_REMEASUREMENT subjects in a contract-filtered block, mixed and missing dimensions, CV-21 encoded delimiters, unfiltered totals unchanged, and the RET-BR-03 engine case.
    - No engine or key change. Owner: runner lane.

- **D-90a G12 QA rulings, L8-C-Q-2 and the waterfall tie-out (2026-09-17).** Inputs: the G12 multi-role browser QA on b634b1e (`docs/qa/` record, pass 1: maya, priya, marcus, robert) and the triage workflow erev-l9-qa-triage (investigator and adversarial skeptic per defect group). Supervisor rulings under the delegated authority; none is attributed to Ray.
  - **QA-L9-7 and L8-C-Q-2 (SF-05 blocker counts depend on the reader).** Observed: Blockers 23 for maya, marcus and priya, 24 for robert (extra "Judgements not reviewed 1"). SCREENS_B §1.1 binds BLK-06 and BLK-15 to request counts read through GET /approvals, which applies API-R-09 visibility, while the blocker rule counts period facts. Ruling: close blockers are period facts; BLK-06 and BLK-15 read reader-independent request counts, so every reader of the cockpit sees the same values for these two blocker classes. BLK-03 still reads the permission-filtered `GET /exceptions` count (a 403 reads as 0) and is not claimed reader-independent; the G12 record states the measured four-persona result.
    - 04 API-S-PeriodCockpit gains `pending_requests`: `[{subject_type, count}]` for JUDGEMENT_RECORD and MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT, in that fixed order, counting PENDING requests of the entity (entity-only, like `approvals_pending`; no ids or summaries are disclosed; the entity total is already public to cockpit readers).
    - The cockpit subtracts these counts for BLK-06 and BLK-15 and no longer reads GET /approvals for blockers; SCREENS_B §1.1 binding amended; `make openapi`.
    - Tests: a reader without approval permission and an approver see the same BLK-06 and BLK-15; exact member order.
    - Owner: platform lane (rc).
  - **QA-L9-4 (SF-01 Waiting for you entity scope; builder question L7-2-Q-22).** Ruled as built for the rc. Under the current API-R-09 `entity` semantics (`gates.request_of_entity`), an entity filter excludes tenant-wide requests (ROLE_CHANGE, ROLE_ASSIGNMENT, SOD_EXCEPTION, SUPPORT_GRANT, IMPORT_COMMIT, configuration versions), so binding the context entity would hide decidable work from approvers. SCREENS §2.5 is amended: Waiting for you lists the pending requests assigned to the user (API-R-09 `assigned_to_me`; the assigned clause of `approval_queries`), across entities. The Pending approvals key figure counts requests the user can decide in the context entity (04 API-S-DashboardHome; `can_decide`, including the required-role and delegator exclusions the assigned clause does not apply), so the two may differ by entity scope and by those decision checks. This accepts the global queue scope for the rc; it does not resolve the decision-eligibility differences. Post-rc: entity scoping that keeps tenant-wide requests listed, together with SF-12 filters.
  - **QA-L9-2 (SF-12:all ignores `entity` and `f.status` from the SF-05 link).** Post-rc as already recorded by D-88 L7-2-Q-13 (i); the G12 QA confirms it live (417 requests listed).
  - **Records and scope.** The records separate fixed defects from accepted and deferred behaviour. Fixed in the rc once merged and verified: QA-L9-5a (generic request body formatting) and QA-L9-7 for BLK-06 and BLK-15. Accepted rc behaviour under this ruling: QA-L9-4 (global queue scope). Deferred limitations: QA-L9-2 (D-88 L7-2-Q-13 (i)), QA-L9-5b (the specialised CONTRACT_ACTIVATION body stays unbuilt after the generic formatting), QA-L9-3a and the activity verb catalogue. The same text is aligned in BUILD_SPEC BS1-D-17 and the duplicate RT-57 row of docs/build-spec/PHASES.md; 04 keeps the delegation-route permission text.

  - **Waterfall tie-out TO_WATERFALL_EQ_JE_REVENUE (SF-08:report and SF-04 answer FAIL in the rc world).** Accepted artefact, not a defect. RPT-01 defaults the range to the context fiscal year, and both sides cover the same entity, book and periods (SCREENS_B §5.6.1 RPT-01). The rc world recognises revenue in FY2026-P01 to P09 but holds a journal run for one period only (the journey's Sep 2026 run; screens.spec's Aug 2026 run), because seeded close history is post-rc (R-RC-1; CLO-22). The difference is the recognised revenue of the periods without a run plus the WLD-B-05 hold on BG-AVM-0021 in the run period (S14-R-17); for the L8-J capture (L8-J.md, rc-smoke-10-waterfall) the difference 2,359,042.91 decomposes into FY2026-P01 to P08 2,356,478.53 and BG-AVM-0021 September 2,564.38. A FAIL is the correct control answer.
