Post-rc supervisor questions: ENGINE_SPEC_B S15-R-02 checks each period while RPT-01 and the build compare range totals; after a hold is released a new GROSS run does not pick up lines from seals an earlier run covered (summarise._covered_from).

- **D-90b S06-R-11 remaining service of an existing time-elapsed obligation (MOD-JS-06; L8-D-Q-1) (2026-09-17).** Inputs: the verification workflow erev-l9-verify-findings (finding f1), the design panel erev-l9-mod-js-06-design (candidates A, B and C prototyped through the stage 01 to 06 fold; a policy judge and an engineering judge; synthesis `.run/l9/mod-js-06-synthesis.json`) and the independent layer-lineage review erev-l9-mod-layer-lineage. Supervisor ruling under the delegated authority; none is attributed to Ray. The approved key MOD-JS-06 and D-88 L7-5-Q-2 (ii) figures stand (weights 39,000 / 40,000; shares 53,810.13 / 55,189.87; L1 54,810.13; October revenue_cum 1,459.92; year-end 2,379.75).
  - **Gap.** S06-R-11 and ALG-04 §2.5.4 resolve the SSP of the booked service for a class D existing obligation. A time-elapsed obligation has no unit ledger (segments.py), so the engine weighs the full booked service where the ruling needs the remaining service.
  - **Rule.** For a class D existing obligation that is not a VC line and not a series, whose FIXED segment in force before the event measures TIME_ELAPSED with a cause other than TERMINATION:
    - w_p = ρ_p × resolve_ssp(at d, RQ⁰_p, RQ⁰_p × ū_p).selected + Σ added-unit parts (not scaled);
    - ρ_p = Σ_L q_L × (1 − f_L(d − 1)) ÷ Σ_L q_L, where f_L is ALG-11 progress under the pinned E-21 convention (S09-R-08; else POL-090), measured as on the INCEPTION basis at the close of d − 1 over [s_L, e_p]; [s_p, e_p] is the term in force of that segment (start clamped by `recognition_start_date`).
    - Unit layers: the booked units start at s_p; units added at an earlier boundary k start at max(d_k, s_p); an earlier decrease reduces every layer pro rata; removals at d reduce RQ⁰ before scaling. The layers are reconstructed from the FIXED segment history of `totals.quantity`, which carries net changes only.
    - **MOD_UNIT_HISTORY_AMBIGUOUS** (independent review erev-l9-mod-layer-lineage; option (ii) with both verifiers' corrections). S06-R-08 gains an ERROR finding for an eligible obligation (FIXED segment in force measuring TIME_ELAPSED, not a series, not a VC line, cause other than TERMINATION), whatever its D or N treatment, when the service has started (d > s_p) and (a) one modification's lines on that obligation carry both a negative and a positive `quantity_delta`, or (b) a line adding units carries a start date other than the term in force (the former same-date condition is withdrawn by D-90c: ordered same-date modifications are accepted). The finding is emitted in the `_check_remaining` loop over every pooled obligation before any weight, blocks the event and appends nothing, so no `mod_weight` node exists for it. Net reconstruction cannot represent these histories (review world W1: 74,000 against the explicit old-unit/new-unit 2,923,000/39). A mixed event before the service start reconstructs exactly and stays accepted. Reachability: no supported rc input path produces these shapes (payload keys unique; CONTRACT_AMENDED not a recorded event type; CTR-17 native modifications and CSV v2 modifications post-rc; legacy template duplicates refused; none in the answer-key corpus); the finding closes the engine contract.
    - Fail-closed invariant: a FIXED boundary that changes `totals.quantity` without a modification or material-right exercise raises ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED, with an OPENING_BALANCE carve-out (a layer reset at the opening start); the lane prototypes and measures it over the corpus before merging, else it is recorded post-rc. The trace ties Σ layer quantities to the remaining quantity plus removed units (mutation test).
    - Post-rc (CTR-17): state-carried unit layers with gross event deltas (option (i)) once native T-CON-06 modification lines are reachable, with a human accounting sign-off on removal attribution (pro rata, oldest first or newest first move the review world W2 weight by up to 1,947.37); CTR-17 rejects repeated obligation keys in T-CON-06 lines.
    - Additional tests: the W1 real fixture (mixed event refused; split removal-first equals the explicit representation; addition-first same-date sequence accepted with the pro rata figures, D-90c), a class N mixed history followed by a class D event, a pre-start mixed event accepted, a re-terming added-unit line refused, the pro rata sequential attribution world W2, and fixtures that pin `recognition.time_convention` (POL-090 otherwise overrides the template convention).
    - ρ is never read from the progress g of a PROSPECTIVE segment and is never compounded across boundaries.
    - Inside a period under MONTHLY_EVEN or MID_MONTH the current period counts as remaining (1 − f(d − 1)): a modification priced at SSP then moves no consideration between obligations (the term-weight ratio moves 108.58 under MONTHLY_EVEN and 224.72 under MID_MONTH off L1 in the design world).
    - Fail closed: ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED when the unit layers do not reconcile with the segment history, or when ρ = 0 for a class D obligation.
    - INCEPTION_ALL: ρ × RQ⁰ × u0 + added parts.
    - Unchanged (ρ = 1): series ("the d SSP of the remaining increments"), unit, cost and ratio measures, class N, added obligations and terminations.
    - ΔQ of an added-unit line counts units over [d, e_p]; the SSP entry prices that unit over [d, e_p].
  - **Trace.** `mod.weights.d18.v1` and `mod.weights.inception_all.v1` keep their ids with additive params (`measure`, `convention`, `term_start`, `term_end`, `progress_as_of`, the layers and `remaining_scale`); a node without `measure` evaluates as today, and reevaluate(trace) stays exact.
  - **Fixtures.** EX-09-A and EX-15-A model PRD K-02 O1 with a distinct TPL-SAAS whose SSP book prices the remaining service; PRD K-02 O1 is a series (TPL-SUB-DAILY). The fixture template becomes a series (increment day) in `test_s06_prospective.py` and `test_s15_rpo.py`, with docstrings; no figure, answer key or golden value changes.
  - **Rejected.** Candidate C term-weight ratio (moves consideration inside a period; new ALG-11 normative text); candidate B MONTHLY_EVEN whatever the pin (a second day-count inside one obligation; contrary to S09-R-08); candidate A booked-term ΔQ (contradicts J-05.1 and the in-force conversion rule); (1 − before.progress) (re-based g after a PROSPECTIVE boundary); compounded factors (path-dependent); no scaling (the engine today; MOD-JS-06 fails); scaling series (double-counts MOD-CHK-043); re-pricing the fixture book (ties the fixture to a contested day-count); a silent single-layer fallback (a hidden transformation of a published weight); taking MOD-JS-06 out of the selection (the key is ruled; the engine is wrong).
  - **Post-rc questions.** Counting the remaining increments of a per-seat-month series SSP (seeded K-02, PRD WLD-X-06); band selection over [d, e_p] for added units of per-term-priced SKUs; class N re-basing after a PROSPECTIVE boundary (weights.py before.progress); a term extension on the weighed obligation in the same modification; ρ on the S06-R-26 corrected SSP; the pre-existing MID_MONTH d > e class D invariant.
  - **Owner.** Engine lane (stage 06 segments.py, weights.py, inception_basis.py; formulas.py; fixture templates), with the tests of the synthesis plus the layer-lineage cases; gates: backend/tests/engine, the release selection (173), all active keys, make parity, make properties, make ci and make test-pg.

- **D-90c Same-date modifications under S06-R-08 (2026-09-18; amends D-90b).** The independent live review of the D-90b implementation (`eRev ASC 606 Review/erev-d90b-live-review.md`) found the ruling self-contradictory: condition (b) refused every pair of same-date modifications moving an eligible obligation's quantity in opposite directions, while the additional tests required the split removal-first history to equal the explicit old-unit/new-unit representation. Ruling: condition (b) is withdrawn. Modifications effective on the same date are applied in event order and each is a boundary of its own; the S06-R-11 unit layers reconstruct both orders deterministically under the D-90b pro rata decrease rule, so the history is representable, not ambiguous. Figures (one-year MONTHLY_EVEN service from 1 January, ten booked units, changes on 1 March, measured 1 October): remove five then add five gives layers 5 at 1 January and 5 at 1 March, ρ = 11/40; add five then remove five gives 20/3 at 1 January and 10/3 at 1 March, ρ = 4/15 (at a 1,000 full-term unit SSP: 2,750 against 2,666.67 before rounding). The order is the preparer's explicit representation of which units were removed. `MOD_UNIT_HISTORY_AMBIGUOUS` keeps (a) mixed-sign lines within one modification, where no order exists, and (b, formerly c) an added-unit line that re-terms the obligation. Tests: removal-first equals the explicit representation; addition-first is accepted with the pro rata figures; each same-date boundary creates its own FIXED segment. ENGINE_SPEC S06-R-08 and table 0.8-A (rev 1.5), 04 table 15.4-C (rev 1.10) and the D-90b text are amended; the engine lane revises its S06-R-08 checks and tests (sprint/l6 commit 0020a1e) before the merge. The opening-balance reset (one new layer at the opening start) was reviewed without a defect.

- **D-90d Runner-lane questions L9-RUN-Q-1 to Q-8 (2026-09-18).** Inputs: docs/reviews/loop/sprint/L9-RUN.md (sprint/l5) and the triage workflow erev-l9-run-questions-triage (investigator plus adversarial skeptic; record .run/l9/run-questions-triage.json). Corpus scan: 234 key files, 300 contracts, 2 multi-member groups (G-ENT-01, G-PKG-1) with no refund component; 0 CV-21 delimiters in contract ids, group codes or entity codes; 0 group codes equal to a member id; 24 contract-filtered subledger blocks in 9 keys, all on singleton groups. Supervisor rulings under the delegated authority.
  - **Q-1 (exceptions attribution): as built.** An `exceptions` row's `contract` and `obligation_key` match the decoded leading CV-21 component of the finding's `subject_key` (`_subject_parts`); findings carry no line dimensions, so the D-90 `contract_key` attribution does not apply to them. A finding whose subject is a group-level key (`<group>@<entity>`: stage 12 FX_RATE_MISSING and S12-INV-03; a stage 10 refund component key) is listed by a row without `contract`. dev-guide §9.5.6 `exceptions` row gains that sentence.
  - **Q-2 (group code equal to a member id): post-rc.** Loader rule DG-AK-58 (§9.5.4, §9.5.8; `_CrossValidator`): `combination_group` differs from every `external_id` of the key and from `CG-<external_id>` of every contract (0 corpus violations). Platform guard, post-rc: an `external_id` may not equal an existing combination group code or match `^CG-CON-[0-9]+$` (generated group codes are `CG-<contract number>`; contract numbers are `CON-<n>`, numbering.py SERIES_PREFIXES).
  - **Q-3 (raw group and entity codes in stage 10 component keys): rc, engine lane.** `refund_liability._key` percent-encodes the group code and the entity (CV-21; `encode_key` as stage 05 does), and `position.py` encodes the `<contract>@<entity>` balance-subject heads the same way; byte-identical for the corpus and the demo seed (no delimiter). Reachable only through an entity code carrying a delimiter (T-REF-01 `code` is length-limited only).
  - **Q-4 (multi-member groups: refund component keys carry no `contract_key`): rc, engine lane.** `assign.subject_contracts` parses the stage 10 component key `<group>@<entity>/<KIND>/<source>` and resolves the owning member from the source (the component's contract) before the every-member fallback, so intents `_dimensions` sets `contract_key` and `_contract_scope` sets product and revenue_category; the same resolution drives `_drafts` (S02-R-02) and the void set, which the tests cover. Reachable through POST /combination-groups plus any refund component; today the platform mapper `computation._line_contract` raises ValueError "names no member contract" at persist. Q-3 and Q-4 land together (the parse uses the encoded head). Genuinely group-level entries of a multi-member group (stage 12 FX_REMEASUREMENT with subject `<group>@<entity>`) stay unattributed and are recorded as post-rc question L9-RUN-Q-9.
  - **Q-5 (reported once): editorial.** "Never reported twice" is per contract-filtered block key (period_key, entity, contract, entry key); each block is one assertion, so an entry no member can claim appears once in each member's block of the same period and entity; unfiltered blocks never report it. dev-guide §9.5.6 `contract` row gains the sentence.
  - **Q-6 (UNCLAIMED_PROPERTY grammar): already handled.** The kind has no producer (ENC-8, post-rc; L2-4-Q-16) and the runner fails closed on any such component key, as D-90 requires. The ENC-8 lane defines the kind's `_key` source form (an obligation subject key is preferred) and adds its runner grammar with the producer.
  - **Q-7 (cross-member concession boundary): post-rc latent defect, fix recorded.** `refund_liability._concession_event` skips a boundary whose event belongs to another member of the group (the event's contract differs from the obligation's); reachable only as an S06-R-26 re-pin first boundary of a multi-member group (attributes.reallocate stamps MODIFICATION segments through segments.inception).
  - **Q-8 (encoded estimate head against raw ids): rc, engine lane, with Q-3.** `refund_liability.measure` and its other raw-id lookups compare `encode_key(external_id)` with the encoded estimate head (a `PORTFOLIO:<code>` head never equals an encoded id, so the portfolio skip stands), and `s08 routing._element_contract` resolves the same way, so a contract id with a CV-21 delimiter keeps its VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION component instead of losing it silently, and stage 08 no longer raises "no obligation receives the transaction price change" for it. Reachable: `external_id` is free text on POST /contracts, the legacy v1 template and CSV v2.
  - **Tests (engine lane, in the existing s08, s10 and s14 test modules):** the RET-BR-03 world with contract ids "BR-03/ARMS" and "BR-03/SPARES" passes with unchanged figures and encoded subjects; a two-member group with a RETURN component attributes JET-04b lines to the owning member and its `_drafts` and void set are unchanged for singletons; the corpus, parity and properties are unchanged (measured).
  - **Owner:** engine lane L9-ENG, next batch, together with the D-90c revision of the S06-R-08 checks. Docs: dev-guide §9.5.6 notes (Q-1, Q-5) with a revision row; this decision.

- **D-90e Level 9 lane questions and QA pass 2 verdicts (2026-09-18).** Inputs: docs/reviews/loop/sprint/L9-PLT.md (sprint/l2) and L9-ENG.md (sprint/l6), the merge record docs/reviews/loop/sprint/L9-merge.md, the release gate on main at 22d6c6e (.run/l9gate/; release selection 173 of 173, all active 174 of 232 with the standing 58) and QA pass 2 (docs/qa/G12-multi-role-qa-2026-09-17.md "Pass 2 (final revision)"; .run/l9/qa/pass2-notes.md). Supervisor rulings under the delegated authority.
  - **(i) Platform-lane questions L9-PLT-Q-1 to Q-7.** Q-1 (the generic body's `approvals.diff.member.*` catalogue keys with SCREENS copy as their values), Q-2 (the generic body shapes D-90a left open: record arrays one per line at the top level and "; " when nested; a record deeper than depth 2 as the no-value mark; API-S-Ref by its code and `account_role` through the `accountRole.*` label; `*_id` hidden with `*_ids` while `*external_id` stays visible; IMPORT_COMMIT `diff_summary` amounts as the API string; status literals and ISO dates raw) and Q-3 (period labels read from `GET /periods?entity=` for a request that names an entity and from the first `STRUCTURE_LIMIT` page of `GET /periods` for a tenant-wide request; a key outside that page or disagreeing across calendars reads as the raw key): ratified as built for the rc. SCREENS §15.8 gains the `approvals.diff.member.*` key list; editorial owed. Q-4 (`make ci` red on `test_d85_key_implied_price_change_settlement`, outside the lane): closed by 9df7678 (runner lane) at the merge. Q-5 (a stored failed run opened by `run=` shows SCR-ST-12 without a "Reference <job id prefix>" line, because API-S-ReportRun does not expose `report_run.job_id`): accepted for the rc as the SCR-ST-12 reading ("a command started on the screen"); post-rc, platform lane: expose `report_run.job_id` on API-S-ReportRun (OpenAPI, backend, frontend) so a stored failed run names its own job and RV-14 with DS-CMP-24 read literally. Q-6 (`create.problem` from a failed `POST /report-runs` is not bound to a run and persists across a later `run=` navigation until `restart()`): post-rc, the same (run, problem) binding in `useReportRun`. Q-7: the rc wording is "the new job is bound to the run the retry created" (Retry issues `POST /report-runs`, which creates a new run; the pair binds to the run the API names in `X-Erev-Report-Run-Id`, and the URL follows it).
  - **(ii) Engine-lane question L9-ENG-Q-3: accepted.** The batch text scoped D-90d to Q-3 and Q-4; D-90d itself rules Q-8 "rc, engine lane, with Q-3", and the required RET-BR-03 delimiter test (`BR-03/ARMS`, a routed PRICE_PROTECTION element) cannot pass without the stage 08 `_element_contract` resolution and the stage 10 encoded-head lookup. The lane implemented D-90d as ruled (d06f3a8). L9-ENG-Q-1 was answered by D-90c and L9-ENG-Q-2 closed by IMP-106 (1dda2d1) before the merge.
  - **(iii) QA pass 2 verdicts (D-90a status vocabulary).** QA-L9-7 (BLK-06 and BLK-15 read the reader-independent API-S-PeriodCockpit `pending_requests`; ef4837c) and QA-L9-5a (generic SF-12:request field-diff formatting; b5c4f0d) are merged (ad06f60), gate-measured at 22d6c6e and verified in pass 2, maya, priya and marcus in the browser and robert at the API level (his cockpit payload byte-identical to maya's; 0 visible pending requests; 404 on the judgement request detail), so they move to "fixed". robert's SF-05 browser rendering was not captured in pass 2 (the chrome-devtools connection was lost after a hung header read) and is recorded as an owed confirmation in the G12 record. QA-L9-4 stays accepted rc behaviour (pass 2 observed the tenant-wide "Waiting for you" list for priya). The SF-08/SF-06 job-reference binding (14100e7) is verified on the success path in the browser (two runs created from SF-08:report, the URL `run=` following the created run, no Reference line on a succeeded run) and on the failed-run banner by six vitest cases (`frontend/src/routes/reports/__tests__/viewer.test.tsx`), which the demo world cannot reproduce because it has no failing report job.
  - **(iv) Commit trailers.** 198491c (D-90d) and 08d72b7 (L9-PLT batch 1 evidence) carry "Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>" per the harness attribution rule now in force for the supervisor session, and this record's commit carries the same; the lane and merge commits, including those after 198491c (1dda2d1 to 22d6c6e), carry the repository's earlier trailer "Co-Authored-By: Claude Code <noreply@anthropic.com>"; the two `main` into lane merges (ef985d6, c860fe1) carry none. Both trailers are recorded; neither is a defect; history is not amended.

- **D-91 ASC 606 review dispositions: refund identity, late-event routing, share-based release rounding, RPO rollforward basis, financing payment points, and the platform job reference (2026-09-18).** Inputs: the independent review (`eRev ASC 606 Review/REVIEW.md`, `FOLLOWUP-20260918.md`, `ROUND2-20260918.md`, `ROUND3-20260918.md`, `CONTINUE.md`, the dated verification, supplement, crosscheck and proposal-review notes named per topic below), verification pass 1 (`.run/l9/asc606-verify.json`, workflow erev-l9-asc606-review-verify: one investigator plus accounting and engineering adversarial verifiers per finding, prototypes measured in process over the 232 active keys and the 173-id selection) and pass 2 (`.run/l9/asc606-followup-verify.json`, erev-l9-asc606-followup-verify), and the synthesis workflow wf_d3c50517-dd5 (one synthesizer and one adversarial reviewer per topic; every reviewer returned NEEDS_REVISION; a reviewer's correction is applied below where it cites measured evidence or governing text, otherwise the synthesis stands and the disagreement is recorded in `.run/supervisor/d91/open-questions.md`). Main at assembly is cdf1206 (the ordered L9 merges ad06f60 platform, ea545fd runner, 22d6c6e engine, then the Level 9 round A close-out docs commits 3f1db05 with D-90e and cdf1206); D-91 is appended after D-90e; the accounting documents are unchanged since 198491c, so the document anchors below hold, while every engine code anchor quoted from the packages is at 908a5fa and is re-anchored by the lane. The combined release gate on 22d6c6e completed at 12:26 local (`.run/l9gate/summary.txt`, DONE head 22d6c6e dirty 0): openapi OK; heads 3 passed; ci OK (backend 2247 passed, vitest 614 passed); test-pg 290 passed; properties 10 passed (the explicit p01/p04/p05/p07/p10/p12/p13 selection; no P14 test exists); parity-all 121 selected, 121 passed; keys-rg 173 selected, 173 passed; keys-all FAIL rc=2 with 174 passed, 58 failed, 2 withdrawn of 234 (the pre-existing full-corpus failures outside the 173-id selection, which the ordered-merge check reported as a strict subset of the prior failure sets); e2e-screens 52 passed; e2e-smoke 1 passed. Supervisor rulings under the delegated authority; no accounting interpretation is attributed to Ray. Nothing below is fixed: every figure is a prototype or hand-derived figure until committed (lane), merged (main), focused-regression passed and combined-release-gate measured, except the platform job reference, which is merged at ad06f60 and measured by that gate. Formula ids keep their ids with additive params wherever trace re-evaluation must stay exact (DG-ENG-04; PROP:P14); new semantics take a new versioned id and the old id stays registered for stored traces (DG-KRN-EXP-04). Fraction arithmetic only. No answer-key expected value or golden file changes; the one new key per topic is authored from the rules, never from engine output. D-90d Q-8 (encoded estimate heads; `routing._element_contract`) is implemented on main (lane commit d06f3a8, merged 22d6c6e, independent committed-code check `d90d-d06f3a8-independent-review.md`) and is not assigned again.
  - **C606-01 Stage 09 refund billing identity and the E_b basis (rc; lane ENG-B1).** Inputs: REVIEW.md C606-01, `2026-09-18-refund-attribution-public-supplement.md`, `2026-09-18-refund-rpo-followup-review.md`, pass-1 c606-01-refund-dedup, pass-2 c606-01-followup, prototypes v2 (`.run/l9/c606-01-refund-dedup/dedup_patch.py`, sha256 54cda993…4b4da1) and v3 (`.run/l9/c606-01-followup/dedup_patch_v3.py`, 667aa786…cc933). Classification: (1) to (3) and (6), (7) code contradicts spec; (5) supervisor call; (2), (3) and (4) data-identity conventions confirmed by ruling.
    - **(1) Identity rule.** S10-R-07 ("adds no billing", ENGINE_SPEC_B:789) is unconditional and applies to every `BILLING_RECORDED` line stage 09 counts toward E_b (ALG-06 §2.7.2 step 3, POLICIES:933), whether the line names the obligation or is apportioned under S10-R-01 (D-88 L7-5-Q-3). Identity is (contract_key, `invoice_number`, `line_external_id`) tracked at contract scope over the ENG-06 stream before `admitted()` and before the direct/unreferenced filter, as stage 10 keys it (classification.py:334-345). On main (returns.py:458-465) direct lines are accumulated before the `seen` check, so the defect is confined to lines stage 09 treats as direct and to the direct-then-unreferenced hybrid X11 (L1 1,500.00 / L2 500.00 against stage 10's 1,000.00 / 0); genuinely unreferenced lines are already deduplicated (L8-D.md:162; the pass-1 "B2 unreferenced doubling" is withdrawn as a mixed fixture). The ERP re-send shape (noncancellable original with `is_cancellable` false or absent, L2-4-Q-8, plus an identical repeat) doubles E_b 10 → 20 on main in ERP and ENGINE alike and joins the regression set. Figures (RET-CHK-029 derivative: 100 units at 100.00 transferred 10 Jan 2026, E 20, one 1,000.00 line, same-amount noncancellable update 20 Jan): U_b = 1,000 ÷ 100 = 10, E_b = min(20, 10 − 0) = 10, RL 1,000.00, revenue 8,000.00, position 1,000 − 8,000 − 1,000 = −8,000.00 with or without the update; main posts RL 2,000.00 and −9,000.00. Recorded as a clarification of D-88 L7-5-Q-3 (binding by reference). 0 of 232 active keys change under v2 or v3 (in-process at the 908a5fa engine); the corpus holds no status-update event, so a green corpus is not evidence for this class until the D-91 tests and key exist.
    - **(2) A repeated identity still cancellable is a new line.** S10-R-07 defines a status update by `is_cancellable = false` (absent reads false, 04:2648, L2-4-Q-8); a repeat flagged `true` is a further cancellable line in both stages (stage 09 today, returns.py:463, misapplies it to unreferenced lines: pass-2 M2). Expected in ERP: two 1,000.00 lines, U_b 20, E_b 20, RL 2,000.00, position 2,000 − 8,000 − 2,000 = −8,000.00. The noncancellable-then-cancellable ordering edge takes the same treatment. A data-identity convention, not a GAAP measurement; a WARNING-class finding for repeated identities that are not status updates is a post-rc 04 table 15.4-B catalogue item (D-73), shared with C606-05e.
    - **(3) A status update never changes attribution.** Stage 10 skips the update before any `Line` exists (classification.py:336-344) and never reads its `obligation_key`; stage 09 deduplicates at contract scope before attribution, so the A→B update (Codex six public cases) gives billed A 1,000.00 / B 0, RETURN component 1,000.00 on A and 0 on B, position −16,000.00 (main: B 1,000.00, −17,000.00). Because the API and the CSV import accept such an update with no check (events.py:286-353; csv_v2/invoices.py:230-262), stage 10 raises `INVOICE_STATUS_UPDATE_MISMATCH` (`ERROR`, detail member `obligation_key`, CV-15 blocks) when the update's non-null `obligation_key` differs from the first line's non-null `obligation_key`. Supervisor call on the shape the reviewer found undisposed: a null `obligation_key` on either side means no change, so an update that first supplies an obligation to an unreferenced line (an ERP feed completing its mapping late) is an accepted no-change update, the unreferenced attribution stands and the supplied key is ignored; a WARNING for a late-supplied attribution joins the post-rc catalogue item of (2). X11 (direct line then unreferenced same-amount update) is therefore an accepted status update of the direct line, E_b (L1, L2) = (10, 0). 04 table 15.4-B :5136 remediation: "send the status update with the original amount and obligation (or no `obligation_key`); record a re-attribution as a credit memo of the original line and a new invoice line". The S10-R-07 API 422 clause (amount and `obligation_key`, stored stream plus batch, CSV import alike) and the `_fold`/s01 ledger exclusion of status updates from `billed` (ledger.py:128-130; events.py:401-402) are post-rc platform items (lane l2).
    - **(4) ENGINE-mode expectations are split by mode.** ERP (POL-004): the update changes nothing (billed_cum 1,000.00, RL 1,000.00, position −8,000.00 with and without it). ENGINE: the same-amount noncancellable update is the S10-R-06 unconditional date (D-89 L7-6-Q-9): billed_cum 0 → 1,000.00 and, under (5), RL 0 → 1,000.00, position −8,000.00 in both states, revenue 8,000.00 in every row. The "RL 1,000 in both" expectation of `CLAUDE-RESPONSE-20260918.md` for the ENGINE no-update row was expressly subject to this ruling and is superseded by (5); the mode-independent assertions are revenue 8,000.00 and the changed-amount rejection (900.00 update → `INVOICE_STATUS_UPDATE_MISMATCH` in every mode).
    - **(5) Memo-only ENGINE line: ruled on consideration received or receivable, not from billed_cum 0 [J].** Basis [F]: 606-10-55-23 and 32-10 measure a refund liability on the consideration received or receivable that the entity does not expect to be entitled to; 606-10-45-4 makes a right receivable only when unconditional. Convention [J], moved here from the [F] sentence as the reviewer required: within the engine a `BILLING_RECORDED` flagged cancellable records that the right to that consideration is not yet unconditional (POL-123; S10-R-06; 606-10-55-284 to 55-286 support the measurement basis, not the classification of a flagged invoice for delivered units), so under the binding ALG-06 "billed or paid" proxy `units_billed_or_paid(ob, d)` = the amount of the obligation's counted lines whose S10-R-06 unconditional date ≤ d (ERP: the effective date, research 07 RC-02; ENGINE: the effective date of a noncancellable line, else the later of the line date and the earlier of a payment applied to its invoice and its first same-amount noncancellable status update, L2-4-Q-11; a line with no such date contributes nothing) ÷ p_ref, computed from stage 09's own AllocatedState with the POL-123/POL-004 resolution placed once in `stages/state.py` (`billing_mode_at`; stage 10 `classification._mode` calls the same function; D-88 L7-5-Q-3 forbids stage 10 helper imports, not a shared stage-neutral function). Only a same-amount update sets the date (the v3 `setdefault` without an amount test is refused). Consequences: revenue unchanged (ALG-06 step 2); in ENGINE a cancellable unpaid line no longer grosses up a refund liability against the contract asset (E_b 0, RL 0.00, position −9,000.00 → −8,000.00); paid 25 Jan: E_b 0 at 20 Jan, 10 at 31 Jan; paid 5 Jan before the invoice: 0 at 9 Jan, 10 at 31 Jan; a partial 400.00 applied receipt dates the whole line (inherited S10-R-06 semantics); every ERP figure and every approved key is unchanged. billed_cum 0 alone decides nothing: unapplied cash (A2; 606-10-55-23 would count it; candidate attribution S10-R-01 weights at the receipt date) and unbilled unconditional consideration (ALG-03; extending E_b to it would change ERP figures and EX-04-H "refund liability 0.00 (nothing is billed or paid)") stay outside the proxy for the rc as recorded limitations, not conclusions; L1-3-Q-13's "payments are not counted" is superseded to the extent a payment applied to an invoice dates that line. Balance-sheet only, on a shape no approved key exercises; flagged for the independent revenue-accountant G12 review.
    - **(6) Credit memos never enter the units-billed base.** ALG-06 step 3 removes returned units through Y; a base net of the refund credit memo would remove them twice: E 20, 10 units billed, 2 returned and refunded → E_b = min(18, 10 − 2) = 8, RL 800.00, P02 movement −200.00, position −8,000.00; a credit-memo-reduced base (6, 600.00) is refused; the pass-2 sentence "credit memos reduce the base as in S10-R-03" is not adopted.

D-91 registers `returns.units_billed.v1` (node `return_units_billed:<ob>:<period>`; one input per counted line: a direct line as `SourceRef("event", …, {member: amount, value})`, an unreferenced document as a `SourceRef` of the document's unreferenced total with params `keys|weights|key` so the S10-R-01 share is recomputed by `largest_remainder`, not replayed as a stored `share_minor`; params `p_ref`, `mode`, `as_of`, counts of skipped status updates and memo-only lines), `returns.refundable_units.v1` (node `return_refundable_units:<ob>:<period>`; inputs the `return_expected_units` and `return_units_billed` nodes; param `returned_units` Y; value max(0, min(E, U_b − Y))) and `rl.return.v2` (RETURN component; input 0 the E_b node; params `p_ref`, `as_of`, `minor_unit`; `FORMULA_OF_KIND[RETURN]` switches; v1 stays registered). Lineage tests: a trace copy with a direct line's cited value 1,000.00 → 2,000.00 re-evaluates U_b 20, E_b 20, RL 2,000.00; an unreferenced-document copy with the cited total changed re-evaluates the share. Trace node counts and hashes change for every returns key; no checkpoint value changes.

  - **C606-02 S08-R-07 amendment, incremental allocation of later changes, one apportionment per change on the untargeted pure-inception route, dated targeted quotas (rc; lane ENG-B2).** Inputs: REVIEW.md §C606-02, `2026-09-18-price-allocation-followup.md`, `2026-09-18-prototype-verification.md`, `2026-09-18-targeted-rpo-history-verification.md` (23 checks), `2026-09-18-c60602-d91-proposal-review.md`, `2026-09-18-c60602-d91-incorporation-check.md`, pass-1 c606-02-targeted-allocation, pass-2 c606-02-followup; frozen prototypes routing 680ff513… and dated-quota extension a8a1e365…; independent derivation `.run/l9/d91-c606-02/derive.py` and the reviewer's `.run/l9/d91-c606-02-review/rederive-output.json`.
    - **Classification: spec conflict, amendment first.** `routing._reapportion` implements S08-R-07 (ENGINE_SPEC:1546) literally: `largest_remainder(basis after, inception weights, keys)` over the whole price, which re-derives from raw SSP the quotas S05-R-14 (32-40), S05-R-11 (32-37) and S05-R-12 (32-34(c)) fixed at inception, contradicting S08-R-04, S08-R-06 ("x_exact = x + δ_p"), POLICIES §2.1.2 Uses (:425), DEV-056 and 606-10-32-43/44. Under §0 the conflict is a spec question: S08-R-07 is amended by this decision (ENGINE_SPEC rev 1.6) and the routing change is then a deterministic correction. Measured defect (Codex; pass 1 and 2 through public `compute()`): A/B SSP 100/100, fixed 100 (50/50), evidenced targeted VC 100 → A, A satisfied 10 January, untargeted 0 → 100 (1 Feb) → 0 (1 Mar) → 100 (1 Apr): main posts A 150 → 100 → 150 / B 150 → 100 → 150 with no February catch-up; the rule requires A 200 → 150 → 200 / B 100 → 50 → 100 and REVENUE FY2026-P01 +150.00, P02 +50.00, P03 −50.00, P04 +50.00 (x_A = 150 + U/2, x_B = 50 + U/2).
    - **S08-R-07 (rev 1.6).** On a pure-inception state for an untargeted element: (a) δ_p = (ΔTP ÷ 10^μ) × w_p ÷ Σw over the S08-R-04 weights and x′_p = x_p + δ_p, so every pool of the quota in force (relative, targeted, discount exception, residual, earlier changes) is carried unchanged; Σw = 0 raises `TOTAL_WEIGHT_ZERO` before dividing (CV-32; the code `largest_remainder` raises today; Table 0.8-A adds stage 08; the prototype's `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED` is not adopted); (b) when every x′_p ≥ 0 the posted allocations are one apportionment of the posted price over the exact quotas, a′ = `largest_remainder(basis after, [x′_p], keys)`, Δ_p = a′_p − a_p, so PROP:P1 and PROP:P2 hold after every change; a basis of 0 posts 0 to every obligation. Corrected claim (reviewer, Q5 interaction measured; Codex Round 4): a sequence whose cumulative ΔTP is 0 restores the pre-cycle carried state (exact quotas and posted amounts) when the weights and the other components are unchanged and the cycle-start carried posting satisfies a_0 = `largest_remainder(basis_0, [x_0])`, where a_0, x_0 and basis_0 are the state carried into the cycle, not the original inception (a canonical inception 70/140 carried to 70/140.03 by three deferred targeted +0.01 changes is restored to 70.01/140.02, not 70/140.03, by an untargeted +0.03/−0.03 cycle, so the first untargeted change may re-round a cent carried from the deferred targeted path, as it can for Q5) (a condition distinct from S05-INV-02); on a Q5-affected inception state (fixed 100.01, K 33.33 → [A,B]: stage 05 posts 66.68/66.66 for exact 66.67/66.67) the first untargeted change re-rounds the stage-05 cent inside its Δ_p (+0.01 → 66.68/66.67; −0.01 → 66.67/66.67), traced by v2, so Q5 is visible, not silent; (c) when any x′_p < 0 the incremental route is selected (Δ = `largest_remainder(ΔTP, w, keys)`); whether the change is appended or refused is decided separately by the S08-R-06 transition predicate below; (d) the `tp_share` nodes carry `estimate.route.inception.v2` (params `mode`, `keys`, `key`, `ssp_weights`, `quotas_after`, `basis_after`, `a_before`; `reapportion` = `largest_remainder(basis_after, quotas_after, keys)[key] − a_before`; `incremental` = `largest_remainder(ΔTP, ssp_weights, keys)[key]`); v1 stays registered. On an SSP-proportional state (b) equals the former text, so every existing re-apportionment (57 calls in 12 keys, pass 1; all proportional) is value-identical; only trace params change (12 keys: ALC-S4-EX35-CASEB, DISC-CATCH-UP-BY-CAUSE…, DISC-CHK-101…, JE-CHK-025-S3-EX24…, JE-CHK-026-CHK-100…, MOD-CHK-027-S6-EX8, MOD-CHK-115, ROY-JS-08…, VC-CHK-101-S3-EX23-CASEB, VC-CHK-110, VC-RB-01…, VC-S3-EX24; trace hashes differ, recorded in release evidence). Cumulative rounding figures: four +0.01 untargeted changes post 150.01/50.00, 150.01/50.01, 150.02/50.01, 150.02/50.02 (naive incremental 150.04/50.00); mixed +0.01, −0.01, +0.02, 0, +0.03 post 150.01/50.00, 150.00/49.99, 150.01/50.01, 150.00/50.00, 150.02/50.01; JPY (μ 0) and BHD (μ 3) sequences hold P1 and P2 at every step. Scope (reviewer, Codex): one apportionment is delivered on the untargeted pure-inception route only; targeted changes keep incremental shares within the targets (`routing._incremental`), so S05-INV-02 can fail by one minor unit after repeated targeted changes (SSP 100/200, fixed 210, three targeted +0.01: 70.00/140.03 against exact 70.01/140.02), identical to main. The supervisor approves that deferral explicitly: post-rc question L9-C606-02-Q4 (extending (b) to the targeted path moves ±1 minor unit in up to 10 targeted keys and needs a re-baseline D-number); the stage 05 `targeted.totals` double rounding is the separate post-rc finding L9-C606-02-Q5.
    - **Negativity test (ruled; the S08-R-06 transition predicate, published verbatim).** Routing and validation are separate: a negative resulting exact quota selects the incremental route (S08-R-07(c)); whether the change is refused or appended is decided by S08-R-06: raise `VC_ALLOCATION_NEGATIVE` (`ERROR`, no segment) when for any obligation p the exact quota moves from x_p ≥ 0 to x′_p < 0, for every routing path in `estimates.apply`. The predicate is on the exact quota (S08-R-06 tests the exact quota first, as S05-R-15 is implemented, targeted.py:187-202), which fixes two boundaries: an exact quota already negative at inception (S05-R-10 symmetric negative T, PROP:P3) that stays negative does not raise even when its posted amount moves from 0 to below 0 (the posting follows the exact quota); a newly negative sub-minor-unit exact quota raises even when both posted values are 0 (measured corner: SSP 10/90, fixed 0.01, targeted 5.00 → B, untargeted −0.02 gives x′_A = −1/1000 with posted 0 → 0; the prototype posts A 0 / B 4.99 with no finding, main 50/449; also x′_B = −1/200 for U = −100.01). No blanket "any negative allocation raises" rule exists. Wording (reviewer, Codex): the predicate is on each obligation's total exact quota, not an untargeted-pool budget: fixed 50/50, targeted 100 → A and 100 → B, U = −150 gives 75/75 and no error; the review fixture U = −150 gives x_A 75 / x_B −25 → finding on B (main silently posts 25/25 by re-weighting the targeted pool). 04 table 15.4-A `VC_ALLOCATION_NEGATIVE` condition text is aligned (an obligation's exact quota moved from at or above 0 to below 0 by a targeted allocation or a later change; editorial, no new code).
    - **Increment basis on exception states [J].** Discount exception (S05-R-11): reading (i), the increment is spread by the inception selected SSPs of all obligations (the bundle discount was fixed under 32-37; a later price change is not evidence about the bundle): FASB Example 34 Case A + 14.00 → 44.00 / 38.50 / 31.50 (main 32.57 / 44.79 / 36.64; reading (ii) 40.00 / 40.70 / 33.30). Residual (S05-R-12): reading (iii), the inception residual R is the obligation's 32-34(c) SSP estimate and 32-43 forbids later SSP changes, so R is its S08-R-04 weight: Example 34 Case B + 13.00 with weights 40/55/45/30 → 43.06 / 37.21 / 30.44 / 32.29 (main 40.86 / 56.18 / 45.96 / D 0.00; prototype weight 0: 43.71 / 38.11 / 31.18 / 30.00; (ii) 40.00 / 33.00 / 27.00 / 43.00). Stage 05 records `ObligationState.inception_weight` (selected SSP, or R for the residual candidate) and `routing.weight()` reads it on all three paths (`_incremental` within targets, `_route_32_45` pre-weights, S08-R-07). Reading (iii) is not in the frozen prototype (weight 0), so the committed routing differs from 680ff513… and Codex's rerun against committed code is mandatory; the lane's public-compute test on the `test_s05_exceptions` ex_34 fixtures supplies the figures. Corpus-neutral: 5 keys use exception approaches, none carries a later change. Flagged for authoritative research before G12 (Big-4 guidance on the locked residual estimate).
    - **Dated targeted quotas for S15-R-09 / POL-200 (code contradicts spec).** `estimates.apply` never updates `AllocatedState.targeted_vc_quotas` and `rpo._exemption_cap` reads the inception-only map, so after an evidenced targeted element rises 100 → 200 with both obligations wholly unsatisfied and POL-200 `APPLY`, disclosed RPO rises 100 → 200 where POL-200 and S15-R-09 leave it at 100 (allocations A 250 / B 50 are right). A latest-only map misstates the S15-R-12 opening (P02 opening 50 instead of 100): the opening is the previous lock snapshot (S15-R-12 text; the "day before the period starts" is the restated opening of rpo.py:34-35), and its exempt amount is the quota in force at that date under the S15-R-09/POL-200 amount clause. Ruling: stage 05 seeds and stage 08 appends `targeted_vc_quota_history[estimate][obligation]` ((date, Quota) in ENG-06 order) after every reallocating version of an evidenced `OBLIGATIONS` element whose segments were added; `targeted_vc_quotas` stays the latest map; POL-200 reads the quota in force at the measured date (d_v for the RPO row; each end of the rollforward); cap = min(remaining RPO, max(0, Σ a_posted in force)), a negative quota exempts nothing (50-14A(b)); the `rpo_excluded` node keeps `disc.rpo_exemption.v1` with additive params `quota_as_of` and `quota_nodes`. Codex's 23-check history (targeted 100 → 200 → 50 → 0 → 100 interleaved with untargeted 0 → 100 → 0, POL-200 APPLY; reviewer rerun `.run/l9/d91-c606-02-review/codex-23-rerun.jsonl` checks 23, failed 0) is the acceptance: month-end RPO 100, 100, 200, 200, 200, 200, 100, 100; EXEMPTIONS −100, −100, 0, +150, +50, −100, 0, 0; VC_ESTIMATE_CHANGES 0, +100, +100, −150, −50, +100, −100, 0; UNEXPLAINED 0, LATE_EVENTS 0 with and without opening snapshots; main fails 6 of 7 periods, latest-only 5 of 7.
    - **Evidence asymmetry (F5) and version guards.** `routing.route` routes every `allocation_target = OBLIGATIONS` element to its targets while `targeted.select` requires `allocation_criteria_evidence`; 11 corpus keys route unevidenced elements and pass; answer keys govern numbers (§0), so the code behaviour is kept for the rc and S08-R-04 states it; a `WARNING` for a targeted element without evidence and unification of the two stages are post-rc (a new code needs 04 T-15.4). Fail closed in the rc (CV-45 `EngineError("ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED")`, detail rule S08-R-04): a version whose `target_obligation_keys` differ from version 1 (`reason = target_set_changed`), and, supervisor call adopting the guard Codex and the reviewer required instead of the package's unimplemented claim, a version whose evidence state differs from version 1 (`reason = evidence_changed`; the predicate is the existing `targeted._evidenced`, `value is not None and value != ""`, targeted.py:328-330, shared and not a null-only check, in either direction including empty-string ↔ non-empty). Both guards run in `estimates.apply` before its zero-delta early return (estimates.py:231-232 at 908a5fa), not only in `routing.route`, and before any segment or quota-history mutation, so an unchanged-price version with a changed target set or evidence state is refused too; 0 corpus keys carry `allocation_criteria_evidence`, so both guards are corpus-neutral.

New key `VC-D91-01-TARGETED-THEN-UNTARGETED-CHANGE` (POL-200 APPLY; obligation checkpoints 200.00/100.00, 150.00/50.00, 200.00/100.00; revenue_cum A 200.00/150.00/200.00; REVENUE journal +150.00 / +50.00 / −50.00 / +50.00; rpo 100.00 / 50.00 / 100.00); no existing key carries `allocation_criteria_evidence` or POL-200 `APPLY`.   - **C606-03 Share-based consideration payable: revenue driver, formula lineage, rounding composition (rc; lane ENG-B3; supersedes lane note L2-2-Q-27; amends D-88 L7-6-Q-6).** Inputs: REVIEW.md C606-03, `2026-09-18-c60603-followup-verification.md`, `2026-09-18-c60603-rounding-supplement.md`, `2026-09-18-d91-c60603-proposal-review.md`, `2026-09-18-d91-c60603-acceptance-supplement.md` (exact numerator arithmetic), `2026-09-18-1722-monitor-crosscheck.md`, pass-1 c606-03-share-based-incentive, pass-2 c606-03-followup, prototypes proto.py (7cc9c6b2…) and proto_v2.py (f701c206…), oracle `research-harness/answer-keys/answer-keys-topics/specs_b3.py:107-137`.
    - **(1) Driver: code contradicts spec.** S04-R-17 (ENGINE_SPEC:1050), PT-10 (POLICIES:1341) and T-CON-13 (04:3265) define the cumulative reduction as grant-date fair value × (1 when vesting is probable, else 0) × cumulative related revenue ÷ expected related revenue, capped; `customer_consideration._share_based` (:430-457) measures related purchases invoiced from the grant date instead; the lane note L2-2-Q-27 was never ruled. Reproduced: 400,000 invoiced without delivery posts Dr REVENUE 20,000.00 / Cr BILLING_CLEARING (EQUITY) with contract revenue_cum −20,000.00; 400,000 delivered without an invoice posts nothing. Ruling: R(t) is the cumulative revenue recognised for the related obligations through the contracting entity's period end t, gross of consideration payable and of this reduction (S04-R-02), read as the POSTED stage 09 `revenue_cum` node value at the latest performing-entity period end ≤ t, as stage 13 `_relief_targets` maps relief (S10-R-09), less the posted stage 10 `concession_created_cum` of those obligations at t (JET-05c) (erratum, count-once numerator, ruled on L9-ENG-B3-Q-1: the literal "less posted `concession_created_cum`" double-counted a concession the stage 06 S06-R-09 / stage 08 D-88 L7-5-Q-4 (iii) producer had already applied to the obligation's recognition segment, so the posted `revenue_cum` node already carried it. Operative rule, ENGINE_SPEC_B S10-R-26 rev 1.8: R(t) = Σ over the related obligations of the posted `revenue_cum` of the selected node (latest performing-entity period end c ≤ t) − the posted concession portions of those obligations dated after c and on or before t, read per dated portion from `AllocatedState.concession_history` (stage 06 −receiver.exact / −receiver.posted under CREDIT_OR_REFUND; stage 08 −share.exact / −share.delta for a targeted DISCOUNT / SLA_CREDIT on a complete obligation without an explicit refund-liability target; eligibility unchanged, no reversal behaviour) and each cited with sign − through its own `concession_portion@<event>:<obligation>:<period>` node (param `aggregate_node` names the aggregate `concession_created_cum` node, whose id is never duplicated as an input); a portion dated on or before c is embedded in the selected node and is neither subtracted nor cited (element param `concessions_embedded`; `revenue_cutoff` lists the selected nodes' cutoffs); a SEPARATE-marked portion (no 1.0-rc producer) is always subtracted; without a revenue node every portion at t is subtracted (signed, no floor). Conservation by `check_history`, once per measure: per obligation Σ portion.exact = quota.x_exact and Σ portion.posted = quota.a_posted against the FINAL CONCESSION refund quota, never re-rounded; every portion names a producing event of the state, is dated by it and names producer 06 or 08; a quota without history, a history without a quota, a history that does not conserve the quota, or a non-empty quota (exact ≠ 0 or posted ≠ 0) with absent history fails closed (`ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED`, rule S10-R-26); the empty-quota shortcut applies only when exact and posted are both 0. There is NO temporal comparison with the aggregate `concession_created_cum` node: its date comes from `refund_liability._concession_event`'s preferred settled modification and is incompatible across producers (b260878's lower bound rejected the valid mixed-producer history at P07). The aggregate refund component, its node, JET-05c / JET-04b netting and credit-memo consumption are unchanged; lane commits b2029bb, 220e016, 14479e4, 3ba0ec9, cf5b2f9, b260878, 4cb7070; Codex blockers 554c7aa / 14479e4 / cf5b2f9 / b260878). Posted, not "exact where present" (reviewer, Codex-confirmed): `reevaluate` resolves node-id inputs to posted values (trace.py:204-231), so an exact numerator and a node-input formula disagree at the threshold: a DAILY obligation of 14,600,007.28 at day 1 of 365 with F 12,500.00 / E 50,000.00 has exact related revenue 365000182/9125 (40,000.019945…), posted 40,000.02; the element is 10,000.01 on the posted basis (10,000.005 rounds half up) and 10,000.00 on the exact basis; D-91 rules the posted basis, so the stored value and the replay agree at 10,000.01, and the case joins the regression. Billing documents never move R(t); returns, VC and price-concession changes (stage 08 → allocation → revenue_cum) and post-satisfaction concessions lower it: Codex return case equity Dr 5,000.00 in July (cumulative 15,000.00), net revenue 285,000.00; verify case C −2,500.00 at FY2026-P09 (17,500.00), +30,000.00 at FY2027-P06, revenue_cum 902,500.00; concession 50,000.00 on L1-SUPPLY at 31 Aug: 17,500.00 / −2,500.00 at P08, 47,500.00 at FY2027-P06, contract_version.revenue_cum 902,500.00 (erratum, L9-ENG-B3-Q-1: the obligation's `revenue_cum` 950,000.00 already carries the 50,000.00 concession, less the 47,500.00 reduction; the hand-derived 952,500.00 assumed a gross 1,000,000.00 and is reproduced by neither reading; derivation `.run/l9/d91-c606-03-b3/derive-output.json` `concession` and the measured trace, worktree l3). Acceptance figures under the count-once rule (lane record `docs/reviews/loop/sprint/L9-ENG-B3.md`; every figure re-derived with Fraction arithmetic in `derive-output.json` before the tests): the Codex fully delivered same-calendar fixture (400,000.00 delivered and billed, F 50,000 / E 1,000,000, −50,000.00 CREDIT_OR_REFUND at 31 Aug) gives related revenue 350,000.00, cumulative 17,500.00, August movement −2,500.00 and net revenue 332,500.00 (main before the fix 300,000.00 / 15,000.00 / −5,000.00 / 335,000.00); differing calendars (US02 4-4-5 performing close 29 Aug, concession 30 Aug, contracting close 31 Aug; input sha a498d44f…) 17,500.00 at 31 Aug, 30 Sep and 31 Oct, the 30 Aug portion cited at 31 Aug and 30 Sep and embedded at 31 Oct (main 17,500 / 17,500 / 15,000; b2029bb's blanket marker 20,000 / 20,000 / 17,500); two concessions 15 Aug 20,000.00 + 30 Aug 30,000.00: 17,500.00 at all three closes citing only the 30 Aug portion (the 29 Aug node 380,000.00 embeds the 15 Aug portion; 14479e4's aggregate dated by its latest event gave 16,500.00); cross-month stage 06 15 Jul 20,000.00 + stage 06 30 Aug 30,000.00: 19,000.00 at 31 Jul (the 4 Jul node 400,000.00 minus the July portion; `revenue_cutoff` 2026-07-04) then 17,500.00 at 31 Aug, 30 Sep and 31 Oct (equity P07 +19,000.00, P08 −1,500.00); mixed stage 06 15 Jul 20,000.00 + stage 08 30 Aug targeted DISCOUNT 30,000.00 (input sha 1298cb3a…): 19,000.00 then 17,500.00, the July portion node citing the `CONTRACT_AMENDED` event (producer 06) and the August one the `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` event (producer 08). CHK-120 figures unchanged (20,000.00 / 50,000.00; revenue_cum 380,000.00 / 950,000.00). The stale S04-R-17 clause ("until 04 adds it, no share-based target is produced") is removed.
    - **(2) Related-revenue window [J]: LATER_OF with catch-up at grant.** POL-246 `LATER_OF_RELATED_REVENUE_AND_GRANT` (606-10-32-27 via 32-25A is the timing authority; 55-88A to 55-88C are ASU 2025-04 implementation guidance) and the oracle (0 before the grant date, all cumulative related revenue at t otherwise). Grant 1 Jul 2026 after 400,000 delivered 30 Jun: 20,000.00 at FY2026-P07, 30,000.00 at FY2027-P06, revenue_cum 950,000.00; FROM_GRANT (30,000.00 / 970,000.00) is rejected (20,000.00 of a vested fair value would never reduce revenue while E stays 1,000,000.00). A mid-period grant counts the revenue of its period at that period end (15 Jul grant after a 10 Jul delivery: 20,000.00 at FY2026-P07). No window parameter in the rc; a preparer-set `related_revenue_start` (T-CON-13, optional) is post-rc; until then PT-10 states that `expected_total_amount` is measured on the same all-related-revenue population as the numerator (reviewer).
    - **(3) Related-obligation scope [J]; element-only awards stay supported (supervisor call overriding the synthesis).** Scope = the union of `related_obligation_keys` over the contract's promises with `share_based = true` (empty list = every obligation, the convention of L2-2-Q-23 and the oracle); when the contract has no share-based promise, the element's `target_obligation_keys` / `obligation_key`, else every obligation. The synthesis ruled an element without a share-based promise an ERROR (`NO_PROMISE`); the pass-2 accounting verifier, Codex (`2026-09-18-1722-monitor-crosscheck.md`; acceptance supplement §3) and main (customer_consideration.py:188-197 proceeds for element-only contracts) hold element-only awards supported by existing rules, and the oracle (specs_b3.py:118) skips them silently, so engine and oracle already disagree. Ruling: element-only awards remain supported and measured; the missing S04-R-14 −ΣR memo is a presentation inconsistency (consideration_payable_amount 0 while revenue is reduced) recorded as a post-rc WARNING candidate (`CPC_PROMISE_MISSING`, 04 T-15.4 backlog), and the oracle is amended to measure element-only contracts instead of skipping them (no existing key is element-only). `CPC_RELATED_SCOPE_INVALID` (`ERROR`; 04 table 15.4-C rev 1.11; PRD IMP-107; reasons `MISMATCH` | `UNKNOWN_OBLIGATION`) is raised when element keys, when set, do not EQUAL the promise scope (equality, not subset: the ruling, plan and 04 row use one word) or a scope key names no obligation of the contract (verify case R, a silent zero today). Stage 04 keeps `_share_based_keys` in `starts` so the EMOD-22 series opens at the earlier of the promise date and the first pin.
    - **(4) Placement: a stage 10 tail consumer, no fixed point.** Revenue exists first in `RecognitionState.revenue_targets` (ENGINE_SPEC_B §9.1) and JET-05c concessions in `BalanceState.concessions` (stage 10), so the measurement runs last in stage 10 (`s10_billing_balances/customer_consideration.py`), publishing `BalanceState.customer_consideration`: the stage 04 `consideration_payable`, `incentive_release_ordinary_cum` and `customer_incentive_asset` rows plus the stage 10 `share_based_reduction_element_cum`, `share_based_reduction_cum` and `incentive_release_cum` rows. Nothing in stages 04 to 10 reads the release (position.py:177-178); the readers are stage 12 balances.py, stage 13 `costs_view`, stage 14 targets.py and the engine root, all after stage 10; the reduction is outside `allocation_basis` (S04-R-02) and outside the net position (REQ-BIL-007), so one ordered pass is exact. A stage 09 consumer is rejected (cannot see JET-05c); a stage 13 pass is rejected because stage 13 is a view without a STAGES entry and owns no targets. `RecognitionState.allocated` stays unchanged (ENGINE_SPEC_B §0.4 :91 and §9.1 :249, rev 1.2, frozen by D-77; the "D-77 decision 6" citation of the package is wrong); D-88 L7-6-Q-6 is amended to read the set through `PartInputs.customer_consideration`.
    - **(5) Rounding composition [J]: per part, per element; T = P + E; zero residue.** Main rounds T = round(O + S), E = round(S) and posts the ordinary part as P = T − E (targets.py:834-846; balances.py:333), so the ordinary asset line depends on the share measure (verify case A: spurious ±0.01 in P02, P03, P05). Ruling: P(t) = round_half_up(O(t)) is the JET-14 release part (Dr REVENUE / Cr CUSTOMER_INCENTIVE_ASSET), posted from the ordinary node; E_e(t) = round_half_up(S_e(t)) per element and E(t) = Σ E_e(t) is the JET-14 share-based part (Dr REVENUE / Cr BILLING_CLEARING EQUITY); T(t) = P(t) + E(t), a sum of posted parts with no further rounding, is `incentive_release_cum`, the amount netted from `contract_version.revenue_cum`; stage 12 Rel(t) = P(t); `customer_incentive_asset`(t) = posted `consideration_payable`(t) − P(t) [J: a derived identity replacing an independently rounded measure; S14-R-11 no residue; T-CON-09 ties] (R 0.01, committed 2.00, invoice 1.00: release 0.01, asset 0.00 where today's round(R − O) gives 0.01 and asset + release exceed R). The mixed half-cent case (each exact 0.005): P 0.01 + E 0.01 = T 0.02, net revenue 0.98, asset 0.00, 0 trace mismatches (main T 0.01 / P 0.00 / net 0.99; the frozen proto.py stored 0.01 and replayed 0.02, 40 of 611 nodes). This supersedes the provisional 0.01 / 0.99 expectation of `CLAUDE-RESPONSE-20260918.md`, as the rounding supplement invited; the monetary change follows from S04-R-16, S04-R-17, CV-35, S14-R-01 and D-16 read per part, not from the trace defect. Per-element rounding is ruled explicitly as policy [J]: each `SHARE_BASED_CONSIDERATION` element is a separately measured award (its own grant-date fair value, expected related revenue and vesting conclusion, PT-10 and T-CON-13), so each element target is rounded once (CV-35) and the share-based part is the sum of the posted, individually rounded element targets with no rounding of their aggregate; it exceeds S14-R-01's per-part grain by at most one minor unit per additional element, and the trace architecture does not compel this grain: two elements each exact 0.005 post E = 0.02, not 0.01; a two-element half-cent regression is required. Case A (DAILY SaaS 1,200,000; share F 50,000 / E 1,200,000; ordinary R 10,000 over committed 900,000; quarterly invoices): ordinary release 3,333.33 / 6,666.67 / 10,000.00 at P01/P04/P07 with asset movements +6,666.67, −3,333.34, −3,333.33 and no ±0.01 elsewhere; monthly share credits 4,246.58, 3,835.61, 4,246.58, 4,109.59, 4,246.57, 4,109.59, 4,246.58, 4,246.57, 4,109.59, 4,246.58, 4,109.58, 4,246.58 (sum 50,000.00); T at P02 11,415.52 and P04 23,105.03 (main 11,415.53 / 23,105.02) (erratum, L9-ENG-B3-Q-2: the case A `contract_version.revenue_cum` 849,178.08 (900,821.92 − 41,643.84 − 10,000.00) holds with invoices dated the 1st of each quarter, d_v 1 Oct, the `adv.py saas_mixed` dating the engine test uses; with the period-end invoices of the package and of the keys CPC-CHK-120-OVER-TIME-QUARTERLY and -MIXED-ORDINARY, d_v is 31 Oct and the figure is 947,808.21 (999,452.05 − 51,643.84); the keys assert the period-end dating, so every checkpoint that asserts a version field has d_v = `as_of`; both figures recorded, lane record L9-ENG-B3.md Q-2). No corpus key mixes an ordinary promise with a share-based element, so no expected value changes.
    - **(6) Formula lineage.** Both prototypes replay the share node from a precomputed estimate `SourceRef` (related_revenue 400,000 → 0 leaves 20,000 unchanged). Ruling: registered `tp.cpc_share_based.v1` (declared in stage 10 FORMULA_IDS with `tp.cpc_release.v1`): inputs[0] the award source (`SourceRef("estimate_version", …, {value: F})`), then the related obligations' posted `revenue_cum` node ids and their `concession_created_cum` node ids (params `signs` +…/−…) (erratum, count-once: the concession inputs are the `concession_portion@<event>:<obligation>:<period>` nodes of the portions the selected node does not yet carry, never the aggregate `concession_created_cum` node; additive params `revenue_cutoff`, `concessions_embedded`; S10-R-26 rev 1.8; lane commits cf5b2f9, 4cb7070), params `expected`, `vesting_probable`, `grant_date`, `period_end`, `timing`, `minor_unit`; R the signed sum of inputs[1:] per `signs` with no floor; value 0 while not probable or t < grant; E ≤ 0 with R = 0 gives 0 before any division (no 0 ÷ 0), E ≤ 0 with R ≠ 0 raises `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT`; else round_half_up(min(F, F × R ÷ E)); element node `share_based_reduction_element_cum:<contract>/<element>:<period>`; `share_based_reduction_cum` and `incentive_release_cum` are `tp.cpc_release.v1` signed sums of posted nodes; stage 04 publishes `incentive_release_ordinary_cum` and `customer_incentive_asset` as the signed sum (+ payable, − ordinary). Acceptance on every case: `reevaluate(book.trace)` equals every stored node; the element node's only `SourceRef` is the award source and its inputs include the revenue nodes; when one cited node is zeroed the replay equals the independently recalculated result from all remaining signed inputs (concession scenario F 50,000 / E 1,000,000 / revenue 400,000 / concession 50,000: zeroing the revenue node gives R = −50,000 and the element −2,500.00 under the written signed formula, not 0) (erratum, count-once: in the same-calendar concession scenario the element cites the revenue node only, so zeroing it replays 0.00; the signed-lineage acceptance is carried by the differing-calendar fixture, where zeroing the cited 30 Aug portion node replays 20,000.00 from the 29 Aug revenue node, and by the two-concession fixture, 19,000.00 from the 380,000.00 node; lane commits 14479e4, cf5b2f9); the 0.00 replay and the release equal to the ordinary alone are asserted only for traces whose related revenue and concession inputs are all zero; no floor policy is added. The lane ADDS a fail-first formula-ownership test (every emitted node's `formula_id` ∈ the emitting stage's FORMULA_IDS; `tp.cpc_release.v1` allowed in 04 and 10): no such lint exists today (stages/__init__.py:61-64 and test_stage_registry.py check registry membership only).
    - **(7) Guards.** E ≤ 0 with R(t) = 0 gives 0 before any division (no 0 ÷ 0); E ≤ 0 with R(t) ≠ 0 raises `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT` (CV-32; detail formula_id and subject; main returns 0 silently when no invoice exists); missing `grant_date_fair_value` or `expected_total_amount` stays CV-45. The foreign-currency share-based part keeps failing closed (D-88 L7-6-Q-6; EUR contract in a USD entity with the element → `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED`, would-be 2,000.00 EUR refused).
    - **(8) Netting basis.** `contract_version.revenue_cum` nets the posted `incentive_release_cum` of the contracting entity's period holding d_v, as the code (engine `__init__.py:433-457`) and the oracle (specs_b3.py:135 month_end) do; written into S04-R-02 for the rc. The rc test asserting 900,821.92 − 41,643.84 = 859,178.08 at d_v 1 Oct 2026 is tagged period-end-basis-dependent (the d_v-consistent 863,287.67 is a post-rc question for oracle and engine together; d_v 1 Oct presumes invoices dated the 1st of each quarter, see the (5) erratum on case A dating).
    - **(9) ASU 2025-04 is configuration.** POL-246 is FORCED and PT-10 assumes 55-88C for every tenant although ASU-23 is effective for annual periods beginning after 15 Dec 2026 (POLICIES:1214); the engine reads only the boolean `vesting_probable`, so CHK-120 amounts are unchanged under either basis. Rc: universal application (as ASU 2025-07 under S04-R-18; D-26) with PT-10 requiring the estimate `rationale` (T-CON-12) to state the basis of `vesting_probable` (Topic 718 without the 606 constraint under ASU 2025-04; the constraint conclusion before adoption). Post-rc: one project-wide adoption profile (proposed POL-247 `standards.adoption_profile`, tenant/book, CFG) with per-ASU members and effective-date defaults; `expected_forfeiture_ratio` stays evidence-only; a PT-10-only switch is rejected.
    - **(10) Recorded open.** S04-R-16 keeps the ordinary incentive-asset release on invoiced related purchases (under advance billing it releases before related revenue; FASB Example 32 reduces price as revenue is recognised): post-rc rule question L9-CPC-Q-1, not folded in.
    - **Tests, keys, owner.** New `test_s10_share_based.py` (full compute): matched / advance / unbilled (20,000.00 / 0 / 20,000.00); billing-timing invariance S1–S3; over-time quarterly-in-advance and single-in-arrears with identical monthly credits; unrelated obligation excluded (20,000.00 / 780,000.00); probable → not probable → probable (+20,000 / −20,000 / +20,000 / +30,000); E 1M → 2M (−10,000 / +15,000; 975,000.00); grant after performance and mid-period grant; fractional cap USD/JPY/BHD (4,572.48 / 16,918.11 / 11,842.74 = 33,333.33; JPY 4,572 / 16,918 / 11,843; BHD 4,572.481 / 16,918.111 / 11,842.741); return and concession lower R(t); two elements (24,000.00 / 36,000.00; 940,000.00) and the two-element half-cent (0.02); scope validation (UNKNOWN_OBLIGATION, MISMATCH; element-only accepted; empty list = every obligation, S6 world 40,000.00); expected 0 guard; the posted-basis threshold case (10,000.01); trace identity and lineage sensitivity on every case; same-date ordering. `test_s14_customer_consideration.py`: release part cites the ordinary node only; mixed half-cent parts compose (0.01 / 0.01 / 0.02 / 0.98 / 0.00); mixed over-time case A. `test_s04_specialist.py`: the stage 04 series renamed with figures unchanged (CHK-133 200,000.00 / 1,300,000.00 / 1,500,000.00; after-revenue 100,000.00 at P03; before-revenue 40,000.00 / 60,000.00; CHK-120 ordinary 0, memo −50,000.00; tie world release 0.01, asset 0.00). `test_s13_payable_flows.py`: foreign share-based part fails closed; L8-E tests unchanged.   - **C606-04 S15-R-12 RPO rollforward basis and movement classification (rc; lane ENG-B2).** Inputs: REVIEW.md C606-04, `2026-09-18-refund-rpo-followup-review.md`, `2026-09-18-rpo-prototype-verification.md`, pass-1 c606-04-rpo-rollforward, pass-2 c606-04-followup; prototypes proto.py (f66f6e09…, Codex-frozen NET), proto_pub.py (92c2ce9c…, published-basis ρ), rpo_patch.py (b32af94f…, GROSS, rejected).
    - **Basis (code contradicts spec; supervisor call on the unratified lane simplification).** The RPO of an obligation at d in the S15-R-12 rollforward is the S15-R-08 measurement: A(d) − R(d), A(d) = `a_posted` of the FIXED segment in force less the S09-R-23 reduction ρ(d) = round(r × (Y + E)) of a returnable obligation under POL-053 `REDUCE_CONTRACT_QUANTITY` (S04-R-08b: the segments keep the gross allocation), R(d) the stage 09 `revenue_cum` target. `rpo._rpo_at` (rpo.py:422-434) measures the gross basis, the deliberate lane simplification L3-2-Q-32 (L3-2.md:358-363) accepted at the EDS-2 tick and never ratified by a D-number; its A(d) definition and its "would show the difference as UNEXPLAINED" statement are withdrawn (opening, movements and closing shared the gross basis, so V9 tied: 275 mismatching cells at 908a5fa over 9 keys, all with UNEXPLAINED 0). No answer-key expected value changes (`rpo_amount` comes from `rpo.build`, already on the S09-R-23 basis; RET-CHK-029:245 pins allocated_amount 9,700.00 = revenue_cum 9,700.00 in February, so RPO 0 is the only reading consistent with the key).
    - **Measurement points.** ρ at the opening, at the closing and between boundaries is read from the stage 09 published performing-entity period-end `return_states` at the last performing-entity period end ≤ d (the rule `_revenue_points` applies to R(d); C-04; S12-R-17), the proto_pub.py measurement; dated recomputation through `returns.return_state` only at a FIXED boundary inside the period. The Codex-frozen proto.py recomputes ρ at contracting-entity period ends and fails V9 by ±200.00 in the cross-calendar case J2 (UK01 periods to 28 Jan / 25 Feb / 31 Mar; RETURN_RECORDED 5 units 27 Feb: US01 P03 VC_ESTIMATE_CHANGES −200.00, REVENUE +200.00, UNEXPLAINED 0 under the published basis; ±200.00 under the dated variants). Limitation recorded: at a contracting-entity period end that is not a performing-entity period end the closing equals the stage 09 dated measure only when no event falls between the two ends (case K, 600,000 against 500,000 minor, pre-existing; post-rc question).
    - **Movement classification [J]: NET.** A segment's cause line takes the movement of the returns-adjusted allocation at its boundary, so a new contract enters NEW_CONTRACTS at the constrained transaction price allocated (04 DB-17 V1; RET-CHK-029 9,700.00) and a MODIFICATION / TP_CHANGE / ESTIMATE_CHANGE segment carries the whole jump of A including the change of ρ that a new r produces. Corrected clause (reviewer): E is capped at units kept (formulas.py:290-291), so when booking precedes the first transfer ρ at the inception boundary is 0, NEW_CONTRACTS = a_posted (10,000.00) and the first-transfer reduction (−300.00) is a between-boundary movement on VC_ESTIMATE_CHANGES; all 7 changed keys have inception = delivery = pin date, so a constructed booking-before-delivery fixture (booking in P01, delivery in P02) is required. The between-boundary movement of ρ (RETURN_RATE revisions, ALG-06 step 1; returns beyond E, step 6; window expiry, S09-R-26, E = 0 on a measurement dated on the window end, D-87 L6-5-Q-25) is a change in the estimate of variable consideration (606-10-55-25; 32-6; 32-14; 32-42 to 32-44; ALG-06 §2.7.1 [J]) presented on VC_ESTIMATE_CHANGES with sign −Δρ; actual returns within E move no line. REVENUE stays −(R(end) − R(start − 1)) on the stage 09 targets (S14-INV-06). Rejected: GROSS (rpo_patch.py; disagrees with DB-17 V1 and with the platform RPT-07 builder, builders/rpo.py:651-660, which already nets stored `allocated_amount` deltas, so engine and platform share one basis when RPT-07 is wired, L6-3-Q-33); a dedicated RETURNS row (RPT-07 has none; post-rc under D-88 L6-3-Q-33). Non-included obligations (supervisor call the reviewer required): S15-R-12 counts revenue "on included obligations", so suppression applies strictly after the termination boundary: historical periods, the termination period's opening and pre-boundary movements and the CANCELLATIONS removal at the boundary are retained (a final-CANCELLED-status filter must not erase history or its cancellation), and after the boundary the obligation contributes nothing to any line (its later stage 09 movements, such as a window expiry after termination, stay outside RPO); acceptance: opening + classified movements = 0 closing in the termination period, then zero contribution in later periods despite return-window expiry; the proto_pub behaviour of carrying REVENUE −100.00 / VC +100.00 on a cancelled obligation is not adopted. The reviewer's fixtures refute the package's "every termination variant raises": `RET-CHK-029-VERIFY-TERM-40.yaml` (TERMINATION/REMOVE 20 Feb, remaining goods distinct) and `PRICE_MOD_PIN_CCU` (MODIFICATION 15 Feb, 12,000.00, cumulative catch-up) compute through stage 15; both join the regression: the modification case as the NET MODIFICATIONS boundary case (r 100 → 120; ρ 300.00 → 600.00 at the boundary; expiry ρ 600.00 → 240.00; amounts derived by the lane), the termination case evidencing reachability and classification only until the lane derives its monetary expectations independently or the stage 09 `unit_rate` divisor sibling is resolved (its 9,250.00 January revenue on 40 units is contaminated by that sibling; RET/MOD lane); it is not called financially verified before then.
    - **Reconciliation invariant S15-INV-08 (S15-INV-03 exists; the table ends at S15-INV-07).** For every contracting entity e and period P, over the obligations whose performing-entity period end coincides with end(P) on both sides: Σ CLOSING(P) of e's rows = Σ RPO after exemptions from stage 09 obligation measures dated at end(P) with S15-R-09 read at P; the published rollforward of the period holding d_v closes at `contract_version.rpo_amount` per entity when d_v is that period's end (ENT-PER-ENTITY-NETTING: Σ entities = group 13,444.45; not a defect). Stated rounding-safe (closing = dated stage 09 measure, not r × undelivered).
    - **LATE_EVENTS.** Restated opening − snapshot: a correct prior snapshot yields 0 (main fabricates +300.00 from a correct 6,000.00 opening); a genuine late delivery is the whole line (RET-CHK-029 at 40 units with 10 units effective 20 Jan recorded 20 Feb: opening 6,000.00, LATE_EVENTS −1,000.00, closing 5,000.00 = `rpo_amount`; main −700.00 / 5,300.00). No engine or platform caller passes `opening` today, so nothing migrates at rc; when RPT-07 is wired, snapshots are cut on this basis or the first post-fix period carries a one-time LATE_EVENTS of −ρ (demonstrated: main P01 CLOSING 630,000 → patched P02 LATE_EVENTS −30,000 minor).
