  - **C606-05 Stage 04 financing: an S10-R-07 status update counted as a second S04-R-10a payment point (rc; lane ENG-B1).** Inputs: `erev-606-financing-followup-review.md` and probe, `2026-09-18-financing-metadata-verification.md` (frozen patch ed21781d…), `2026-09-18-financing-date-basis-crosscheck.md`, `2026-09-18-d91-c60605-proposal-review.md`, pass-2 c606-05-financing-status. Classification: the identity correction is code contradicts spec; 05g is a supervisor call; 05e is a convention shared with C606-01 (2).
    - **Finding and rule.** `financing._judged_points` (financing.py:565-594) sums every raw `BILLING_RECORDED` per effective date with no identity rule, so a status update becomes a second payment point, against 04:5588 ("a status update that adds no billing"), S10-R-07 and D-87 L5-3-Q-8 ("documents"). Measured on SFC-S3-EX29 with a matching noncancellable update dated 20 January 2026: `financing_interest_cum` FY2026-P01 20.00 → 40.00, `financed_balance` 4,020.00 → 8,040.00, FY2027-P12 508.64 → 1,017.28, `cash_selling_price` 4,508.64 → 9,017.28, revenue 450,864 → 501,728 minor, interest-node param `payments` "0:4000" → "0:4000,0:4000", while `billed_cum` stays 400,000 minor and `reevaluate(trace)` is True in both worlds (trace self-consistency does not establish event semantics). New ENGINE_SPEC S04-R-10a (the D-87 L5-3-Q-8 rule, today only in DECISIONS:495 and L7-5.md): when no payment schedule is in force and the reviewed `SFC_ASSESSMENT` has `significant = true` and `exception_32_17 = NONE`, the payment points are (a) the contract's cash `PAYMENT_RECEIVED` receipts preceding `before`, summed per the receipt event's ENG-06 `effective_date` (not the payload `receipt_date`); else (b) the contract's billing lines recognised under S10-R-07 over `CanonicalBundle.events` (state.py:399; `st.identified.canonical.events`) at contract scope with identity (contract, `invoice_number`, `line_external_id`): the first-seen event of an identity is the line and enters at the line event's `effective_date` (not `issue_date` or `due_date`) for its amount; a repeat whose `is_cancellable` is not true (`false`, "false" or absent) is a status update and adds no point; a repeat flagged `true` is another line (stage-10 parity, 05e); `CREDIT_MEMO_RECORDED` amounts net by effective date; dates netting to 0 are dropped. A different-amount update is stage 10's `INVOICE_STATUS_UPDATE_MISMATCH` (CV-15 stops the computation); stage 04 neither re-validates nor raises and keeps the first amount. The payload `obligation_key` is not part of the identity. Stage 04 never deduplicates a record only because it shares an identifier. Check figures: the update dated after the line, before the line (the 5 January event is then the line; the single point is dated 5 January with the same 24 month ends), naming no obligation, or following a cancellable original keeps 20.00 / 4,020.00 / 4,246.71 / 4,508.64, adjustment 508.64; a 1,000.00 credit memo nets the point to 3,000.00: 15.00 / 3,015.00 / 381.48, cash selling price 3,381.48 (the resulting transaction price 4,381.48 is S04-R-12 mechanics, an unsupported input recorded for a post-rc ruling and never asserted). Parity figures for a repeated cancellable identity: 40.00 / 8,040.00 / 1,017.28 / 9,017.28. Trace: formula ids unchanged; the gap node gains params `payment_points` and `status_updates_ignored`; the judged branch passes the recognised line event keys so the CSP node's per-point `contract_event` SourceRef names the line event (today `schedule.source_event_key` is None on that branch). Corpus: 232 active keys identical baseline and patched (in-process); no S04-R-10a key carries `is_cancellable` or a repeated identity, so the regression file is the guard: 5 failed / 4 passed on main (every failure the `payments` param), 9 passed with the patch, re-run at 1dda2d1.
    - **05h other consumers (post-rc).** `taxes.excluded` (:70-81) and `taxes.collected_tax` (:155-170) drop the cancellable condition and lack contract scope (identical identifiers on two member contracts would merge); `specialist.invoices` (:127-153) already filters on `event.contract_key` (:135) and needs only the cancellable condition (the package's overstatement corrected). Aligned through the shared helper post-rc; `specialist.invoices` is coordinated with ENG-B3 (its callers are the C606-03 driver). No rc key reaches either (234 files, 514 `BILLING_RECORDED` events, 0 repeated identities). Landed by lane ENG-B4 (790a93e; D-97 (32)).
    - **05g ENGINE-mode payment-point eligibility and date basis [J]: ruled as an explicit project proxy, implemented post-rc.** Measured (POL-004 override on SFC-S3-EX29; identical at baseline and patched): a cancellable 1 January line made noncancellable on 20 February gives stage 10 `billed_cum` nodes FY2026-P01 0.00 / P02 4,000.00 while stage 04 dates the point 1 January (P01 interest 20.00, P02 40.10); a cancellable line never made unconditional and never paid gives `billed_cum` 0.00 in every period yet 508.64 of accretion, cash selling price 4,508.64 and Cr CONTRACT_LIABILITY 50,864 minor on a liability that does not exist. Ruling: in ENGINE mode (POL-004 `ENGINE`, POL-123 `UNCONDITIONAL_INVOICES_ONLY`) a billing payment point is dated its S10-R-06 unconditional date (a noncancellable line: its effective date; a cancellable line: the effective date of its first same-amount noncancellable update, or the applied receipt, which then already governs under (a)); a line with no unconditional date at `at` is memo-only and contributes no point. Basis, as Codex's primary-text review requires: 606-10-32-15/16 assess financing on agreed or expected payment timing and the cash selling price, and 606-10-32-20 limits interest to a recognised contract liability, receivable or contract asset; neither equates payment timing with the unconditional date, so the unconditional date is the supervisor's adopted payment-timing proxy for an engine that holds no expected-payment date outside an explicit schedule (API-S-PaymentPoint `date` is an expected payment date and keeps precedence), not a GAAP derivation; 32-20 alone supports withholding interest on a memo-only line, and the proxy supplies the timing. Candidate figures under the proxy: the 20 February update dates the point 20 February, 23 month ends, P01 interest 0.00, P02 20.00, cash selling price 4,000 × 1.005^23 = 4,486.21, adjustment 486.21, revenue 4,486.21. The memo-only line, and any case with no eligible point under a `significant = true` judgement, raises `SFC_REVIEW_REQUIRED` (existing code) and posts nothing, replacing the silent no-schedule path of financing.py:226-232; "adjustment 0 / revenue 4,000.00 / no interest" is the counterfactual output of that rejected silent-skip path and is not a successful-compute acceptance figure. The deterministic identity fix (rc, ENG-B1) stays separate from this payment-date and eligibility implementation (post-rc). The whole-contract receipts-first fallback of D-87 stands (a receipt replaces the invoice-derived points; receipts and invoice points are never combined), and the acceptance rows Codex specified are carried: explicit schedule plus a differently dated receipt (schedule priority; no second point from the update); partial receipt plus unpaid remainder (applied and unapplied variants; the remainder is not counted twice nor lost); historical cutoff and replay; the ENGINE Jan 1 / Feb 20 case asserting the period-dated `billed_cum` node (never the contract-version column) and postings by `PostingIntent.posting_period_key` (the engineering probe read a nonexistent `period_key`, so its January posting zeros are an extraction artefact and not expectations). ERP mode unchanged. Reachability: 0 active SFC keys in ENGINE mode; 0 overlap between the 9 active `is_cancellable` keys and the 10 SFC keys. Flagged for G12.
    - **Tests, owner.** `test_s04_financing_judged.py`: status update adds no payment point [after | naming no obligation | dated before the line | cancellable original then update]; absent or "false" repeat; two updates; voided first line makes the update the line (S01-R-12); changed amount rejected by stage 10 (passes on main and after); another line is another point (parity-labelled: repeated cancellable; different `line_external_id`); receipts keep priority; credit memo nets and the update adds nothing (transaction price not asserted); end-of-2026 246.71 / 4,246.71; post-rc 05g tests under the ruling above. New kernel test `test_billing_identity.py` (matrix and four-consumer parity; taxes/specialist rows xfail until 05h lands). Owner: engine lane ENG-B1 (with C606-01, so the helper lands once). Documents: ENGINE_SPEC rev 1.6 (new §4.3 row S04-R-10a after :1042); ENGINE_SPEC_B rev 1.7 (S10-R-07 :789 states contract scope, the absent-flag reading and the bound consumers); 04 rev 1.11 (§16.3 :5588 note); D-87 L5-3-Q-8 read as amended; dev-guide rev 1.7 (kernel tree entry for `erev_engine/billing_identity.py`).
  - **One identity rule, one home (cross-topic).** S10-R-07 is implemented four ways with three semantics (stage 10; stage 09 `returns._billed`; stage 04 taxes and specialist; stage 04 financing, none). The C606-01 package placed the helper in `stages/state.py`; the C606-05 package in a kernel module. Ruling: the identity is the pure kernel module `erev_engine/billing_identity.py` (standard library only, DG-ARC-02; stage-neutral, so D-88 L7-5-Q-3 "not through s10 helpers" is satisfied; its own unit test as `test_dates.py` and `test_money.py` have): `line_identity`, `is_cancellable`, `iter_billing_lines(events, *, contract_key, before)` yielding lines, status updates (with the first line) and amount or obligation mismatches; no amount validation (stage 10 owns the finding). The POL-123/POL-004 resolution is one function `billing_mode_at(policies, entity, on)` in `stages/state.py` beside `PolicyResolver`, called by stage 10 `classification._mode` and stage 09. Rc consumers: financing, returns, and classification (parity-guarded: byte-identical documents, lines and findings for the corpus); a parity test asserts the consumers agree on the matrix (line; noncancellable repeat with `false`, "false", absent; cancellable repeat with `true`, "true"; different line id; identical identifiers on another contract). Post-rc: taxes and specialist.
  - **Contract and obligation gaps L1-2-Q-9, L5-5-Q-5, GAPS-A1 to A3 and L2-2-Q-4 (post-rc with one complete disposition; rc guard adopted, lane ENG-B3).** Inputs: `contract-obligations-followup-20260918.md` and crosscheck, pass-1 c606-gaps, pass-2 gaps-followup (GAPS-A1, A2, A3, B1), prototypes `gap_a_patch.py` and `gap_b_patch.py` (232 active keys identical), exact checks `.run/l9/d91-gaps/exact-checks.txt`.
    - **(i) Premise.** (A1) S02-R-07 (ENGINE_SPEC:843) is evaluated "at every period end through the horizon" but `_fully_performed` (s02 `__init__.py:221-248`) tests ledger measures only and (a) is evaluated inside the event loop only (:328-332), so a completed, fully paid, nonrefundable TIME_ELAPSED service keeps a 1,200.00 deposit liability and 0 revenue (Codex `time_service_completed_horizon_only_full_compute`): code contradicts spec plus an incomplete enumeration of "fully transferred". (A2) Every 25-7 recognition publishes `deposit_to_revenue_cum` and a 0 balance but posts no Dr DEPOSIT_LIABILITY / Cr REVENUE, because s13 `_DEPOSIT_PARTS_BOUND` (:110-112) and s14 `_DEPOSIT_PARTS` (:92-98) exclude the measure (Codex `pit_goods_delivery_positive_control_full_compute`: FY2026-P12 balance 0, journal only the February receipt): the journal is required by POLICIES:573, ENGINE_SPEC_B:1844 and S15-R-16; the attribution weights are the judgment. (A3, new) after a 25-7 release, `CRITERIA_MET` under POL-013 `CATCH_UP_AT_TRANSITION` recognises C(d) again with reason STEP1_MET (T9: 240,000 minor of revenue on a 1,200.00 contract once the part is bound). (B1) S03-R-10 (:937) branches on "FUNCTIONAL meeting 55-62 (outcome)" while Table 0.4-A (:392) and 04 T-CON-19 (:3441) define no such member; `licences.py:63-64` is SYMBOLIC-only; `_Questionnaire` is `extra="allow"`, so a stored `meets_55_62` is silently ignored (a qualifying functional-IP licence recognises 1,200,000 minor on delivery instead of 100,000 a month).
    - **(ii) Time-elapsed transfer test [J].** An `OVER_TIME` / `TIME_ELAPSED` obligation with a resolved term is fully transferred from its term end date inclusive (the S02-R-06 truncated end where truncated); the transfer date is the term end, not the ALG-11 f = 1 date (D-79: MID_MONTH reaches f = 1 on 2026-11-30 for a term ending 2026-11-15 or 2026-11-20, f(end) = 10/11, and on 2026-10-31 for a term ending 2026-11-10, before the service ends); an unresolved term is never transferred by time; other measures keep the ledger test; a mixed contract releases at max(delivery, service end) (T5 2026-12-31). Receipt test: cumulative receipts ≥ the stated consideration, meaning the S02-R-06 enforceable consideration where the term is truncated (reviewer: the receipt test today sums payload `total_price`; ENA-2b reads `terms[c].truncated_lines`); "substantially all" is 100 percent, no tolerance [J]; a stage 09 hold at the end date does not defer the evaluation [J].
    - **(iii) Evaluation dates, fold order, amount class.** Stage 02 evaluates (a) at T = max(term end) as a dated point ordered after every event of T (L1-2-Q-7; node `deposit_to_revenue@<ISO date>:…`), inside the loop and after the stream when T ≤ the horizon end; the recognition is dated the first date the conditions hold (T1 2026-12-31; T8 2026-11-15 in FY2026-P11; T4 completing receipt 2027-01-15; T4b nothing). A post-fold second pass is refused. The deposit fold moves after stage 03 in `s13_books._run_book` (`run_deposits(ctx, identified, pob, tb)`; the refined `IdentifiedState` replaces `published["02"]` before stage 04). Amount class: Table 14-A "EVENT; re-evaluated in CLOSE_RELEASE" stands: a recognition at an event is `EVENT` at compute; a recognition at a dated evaluation without an event ((a) at T, (c) at `event_c_met_on`) is a `TIME` amount of the `CLOSE_RELEASE` pass (S14-R-05; 05 RCP-08), posted by the close run of its period or at compute into the first open period with origin when closed (this closes A11: a COMMAND compute at known_at 2026-06-30 posts no December release). Reconciling the `CLAUDE-RESPONSE` commitment "bound under the CLOSE_RELEASE amount class": that class applies to the re-evaluated dated recognitions; event recognitions keep EVENT. Stage 02 publishes `deposit_to_revenue_time_cum` (sixth EMOD-12 measure, S02-R-08).

Node ownership (reviewer): stage 09 emits the `deposit_revenue_share:<obligation>:<period>` nodes (formula `step1.deposit_revenue_share.v1`; it holds `IdentifiedState.recognitions` and the AllocatedState) and stage 14 cites them, so no forward reference from stage 09 to a stage 14 node exists. Foreign currency: the release takes the stage 12 `fx_deposit_to_revenue_functional` amount (S12-R-20) split by S14-R-10 over the transaction shares. Corrected FX2 figures (reviewer, from FX-CHK-084-B's rates 1.1000 receipt, 1.1200 January closing, 1.1300 settlement): release 100,000 txn / 113,000 functional; JET-10d 2,000 at the January close (110,000 → 112,000) and 1,000 at settlement (112,000 → 113,000), total 3,000; two-obligation split [75,333, 37,667]; the package's "2,000 = 113,000 − 111,000" used the January average rate and is withdrawn.

The independent revenue-accountant review covers (1) as AD-28 and (5) as AD-29 (`docs/accounting/reviews/G12-REVIEW-PACKAGE-2026-09-19.md`).
  - **(1) Same-date estimate versions recorded before the amendment belong to the modification (spec amendment).** An `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` version (EAC or VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION) whose effective date equals the effective date d of a `CONTRACT_AMENDED` of the same contract, recorded in the same date's event batch with a lower `record_seq`, is read as the modification's updated estimate: S06-R-15 / S06-R-17 measure C_before at the boundary WITHOUT it and the class D / class N treatment WITH it, so no separate `CATCH_UP` (S09-R-35) or `TP_CHANGE` cause arises on d and the combined effect carries the `MODIFICATION` cause; S09-R-04's "lower record_seq is measured in the previous segment" gains this exception for same-date versions of the modified contract. Precisely: the PRE-boundary state — C_before, the pool and the S06-R-11 remaining-service weights — is measured with the estimates in force BEFORE the same-date version (D18: EAC 60,000, f = 30,000 ÷ 60,000 = 0.5, weights 62,500 : 30,000, shares 47,297.30 / 22,702.70); the version then applies AT the boundary, so post-change recognition uses the updated estimate (D18: EAC 75,000, f = 0.4 going forward). This is a measurement rule with monetary consequences where weights depend on progress — D18: contract revenue 70,918.92 (engine today 70,880.00), B 77,297.30 / C 22,702.70 (today 77,200.00 / 22,800.00) — not only a cause relabel (Codex correction, production-20260919-1720). Basis: the BUILD_SPEC's own D18 test and EX-09-B narrate the amendment carrying the EAC update; 606-10-25-12/13 treat the updated estimates of a modification as part of its cumulative catch-up; a same-date send order must not change cause attribution or the S06-R-11 pool weights (D18: f = 0.5, not 0.4). Figures: MOD-CHK-027 FY2027-P02 `catch_up_modification_cum` 91,463.41 (engine today: CATCH_UP (87,804.88) + TP_CHANGE 102,439.03 + MODIFICATION 76,829.26); MOD-CHK-042-D18 shares 47,297.30 / 22,702.70 (today 53,200.00 / 22,800.00); MOD-CHK-042-INCEPTION 2,000.00 combined; MOD-CHK-115 MODIFICATION (15,555.56); MOD-S6-COMBINED-MOD-OWN (5,142.86). Totals are unchanged for MOD-CHK-027, MOD-CHK-042-INCEPTION, MOD-CHK-115 and MOD-S6-COMBINED-MOD-OWN (cause split only); MOD-CHK-042-D18 changes allocation and revenue as stated. A version recorded on d AFTER the amendment (higher seq) keeps today's reading (it is the modification's EAC by S06-R-15 already).   - **(1a) Membership limits for same-date versions (Codex 1726).** The exception applies only to a version of an estimate that the modification's amendment lines change or that a class D / class N re-measurement of the modified obligation reads (EAC of an amended cost-to-cost obligation; a VC element re-allocated by the amendment); a same-date version of an unrelated estimate keeps S09-R-04's ordering; with several `CONTRACT_AMENDED` on one date, a version belongs to the first amendment after it in `record_seq` (the next boundary), never to an earlier one. The lane states the membership test in code and tests both boundaries (unrelated estimate; two same-day amendments).

No expected value changes; FS-03B is expected to turn green under its own 2:1 — the analysis's arithmetic (110.00 per seat-month × 100 seats × 6 months = 66,000.00 → weights 66,000 : 33,000 → shares 57,200.00 / 28,600.00 = the key), to be measured by lane ENG-C1b once the series branch exists; the e467e25 probe measured `mod_weight` L1-SUB 132,000 (class D, remaining_quantity 1, unit_price 120,000, no ρ) against 33,000 because series is excluded from `segments.eligible()` and no series branch exists in `weights.py` / `inception_basis.py` (20 mismatches, fs03b-report.json and fs03b-verify-report.json). The "completing receipt's event" branch applies only to a completing receipt after T, which stays `EVENT` at compute (Codex's $0.01 July 1 case → EVENT 1,200 on July 1). Total release is 1,200 under either reading; only the amount class and the pass differ. Conditions: the E6 docstring records both readings with the S02-R-07 sentence and D-91 (iii) (the lane labels the ruled reading "reading B"); sibling tests pin after-T EVENT, goods-only Jun 30 EVENT and mixed Jun 29 / Jun 30 EVENT; S02-R-07 gains one clause ("a receipt dated T completes the receipts before the dated point; the recognition is at T as TIME"), revision-logged. Not an accounting change (dating and amount identical); platform pass semantics.

- **D-95 Hosted recovery provider: Cloud SQL, `BYPASSRLS` and the backup of record; `NATIVE` and `MANAGED` recovery paths (2026-09-19).** Inputs: lane P6's record `docs/reviews/loop/prod/P6-recovery.md` §5.3, §7b, §7c and §7g on sprint/l8 (914d263; the hosted-backup question raised by the supervisor from lane P3b's Terraform `google_sql_user erev_backup`), the PostgreSQL `CREATE ROLE` / `ALTER ROLE` and `ALTER TABLE … FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` references and the Cloud SQL for PostgreSQL user documentation as read by the lane (documented rules only; no live cloud call), the 05 rev 1.5 OPR-15 amendment (07bac7a, 8f89f21) and 04 DB-14. Supervisor ruling under the delegated authority; a platform ruling, not an accounting interpretation; none of it is attributed to Ray.
  - **(1) Premise (unestablished, not impossible).** Specifying `BYPASSRLS` requires a superuser or an actor already holding `BYPASSRLS`; Cloud SQL customers have no superuser, `cloudsqlsuperuser` carries `CREATEROLE`/`CREATEDB`/`LOGIN` only and membership does not inherit the attribute, so no documented customer path grants `BYPASSRLS` — unestablished, so the design must not depend on it (never "impossible" or "superuser-only"); lane P6 measured `pg_dump` as `erev_owner` refused under `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` ("query would be affected by row-level security policy"), and `--enable-row-security` would dump only the rows the policies show, silently. The lane record's stronger phrasing ("no Cloud SQL role exists or can be created that takes a complete logical dump") is not adopted; the ruling rests on the absence of a documented customer path. The rev 1.5 sentence "Hosted: the `erev_backup` Cloud SQL user is a Terraform variable (lane P3b) and RB-11 documents the provisioning" is withdrawn.
  - **(2) Hosted backup of record.** In hosted production the backup is the physical one of OPR-08 — Cloud SQL automated backups and point-in-time recovery, which do not pass through row-level security — and `pg_dump` cannot be relied on for a complete logical dump there. OPR-08 gains that sentence.
  - **(3) Hosted restore verification runs under the application roles.** OPR-11 step (3) on the clone: `erev migrate` as `erev_owner` (schema identity), then `erev verify --all-tenants` and `erev doctor` as `erev_app`, the identity that also runs `erev restore-applied` in step (6); no `BYPASSRLS` role is needed. The chain, seal and digest comparisons of the step are unchanged.
  - **(4) Hosted restore drill.** OPR-12 quarterly: an automated restore of the latest production backup, or a PITR instant, into a fresh instance named by the Terraform variable `restore_test_instance` (UI-P6-2) in the isolated restore-test project, verified per (3). The restore test record binds {backup id or PITR instant, project, instance, operation status, engine/schema/release identity, recovery interval, file generations and key references, tenant inventory, audit anchors, restore duration, verification result, recovery age}. Recovery-point identity, expected-document validation and `restore_measurements` are those of the NATIVE drill (lane P6 §7d–§7f); the restore point is the PITR instant or the backup's snapshot time. RB-06 names the instance and the recovery age.
  - **(5) Logical backup and the restore-target admin are self-hosted identities.** OPR-15 is local and self-hosted only: `make backup` as `erev_backup` (`LOGIN NOSUPERUSER NOCREATEDB NOCREATEROLE NOREPLICATION BYPASSRLS`; `GRANT pg_read_all_data`) and `make restore-verify` as the restore-target admin exist only where a superuser can grant `BYPASSRLS` — self-hosted PostgreSQL and the compose drill, which proved both as non-superuser roles — never on Cloud SQL. Hosted production has no `pg_dump` path (OPR-08 backup of record; OPR-12 drill). OPR-17 states that `erev_backup` and the restore-target admin are self-hosted identities; RB-04 and RB-11 mark both roles self-hosted-only with the superuser-run provisioning statements. P3b's `google_sql_user erev_backup` variable is unused under this ruling and is dropped or documented as unused by the Terraform lane (UI-P6-2).
  - **(6) Setting.** `EREV_RECOVERY_PROVIDER ∈ {NATIVE, MANAGED}` (05 CFG-30), defaulting from `EREV_KEY_PROVIDER` (`local` → `NATIVE`, `gcp` → `MANAGED`); an explicit value that contradicts the key provider, or a value outside the two, is a configuration error. `NATIVE` requires `EREV_BACKUP_URL` and `EREV_RESTORE_ADMIN_URL` under `EREV_ENV=production` (`check_env --db`; their absence is noted in every other environment); `MANAGED` refuses a set value of either and notes the managed-drill inputs as unknown; `make backup` and `make restore-verify` refuse `MANAGED`. 04 has no settings table for the key provider, so the setting is recorded in 05 only. Lane P6 holds the candidate implementation on sprint/l8 (`recovery_preflight.resolve_recovery_provider`, `MANAGED_NATIVE_REFUSAL`; `test_check_env.py`; 47a2431) — committed (lane), not merged.
  - **(7) Rejected alternative.** A migration-defined per-table policy `FOR SELECT TO erev_backup USING (true)` would let `pg_dump --enable-row-security` dump every row on Cloud SQL without `BYPASSRLS`, at the cost of a DB-14 lint amendment, an Alembic revision on every tenant table and the silent-partial-dump risk of `--enable-row-security` (a row a policy hides is lost without an error). Not adopted; it may be re-proposed under a new ruling only if UI-P6-1 closes negatively and a logical export of hosted production is still required.
  - **(8) Unknown inputs opened.** UI-P6-1: whether Cloud SQL's managed export (`gcloud sql export sql`) runs with an identity that bypasses `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` — settled by one staging test against a FORCE-RLS table with a row-count comparison against the source; not a backup path until then. UI-P6-2: the `restore_test_instance` variable, the drill project and the hosted drill wiring (lanes P3b and P5). The MANAGED path is mock-tested now; hosted evidence is pending both.
  - **Status vocabulary.** Ruled here; implemented and measured by lane P6 on sprint/l8 (NATIVE: the compose drill; MANAGED: unit tests and the mock drill); not "fixed" until merged and gate-measured; a real clone into `restore_test_instance` is a P3b/P5 deliverable after UI-P6-2 and stays open until recorded. Nothing here is acceptance.
  - **Documents (this commit; rule changes carry revision rows).** 05 rev 1.6: OPR-08, OPR-11 (3), OPR-12, OPR-15, OPR-17, CFG-30 (new), RB-04, RB-06, RB-11; `docs/reviews/loop/prod/unknown-inputs.md` UI-P6-1 and UI-P6-2 (README and PLAN-SUMMARY index lines); readiness-matrix rows DEP-5, DEP-6, G10 and G11 cite D-95 and split NATIVE (self-hosted; lane P6 candidate) from MANAGED (hosted; mock now, hosted evidence pending); the UI-10 bullet and the P6 lane brief name `restore_test_instance`. Lane P6's dev-guide, runbook, `.env.example`, script and test changes arrive with the P6 merge, not with this commit.
- **D-96 SOP-3 release replay data contracts (2026-09-19).** Inputs: Codex `PRODUCTION-SOP3-REPLAY-ACCEPTANCE-MAP-447a2a7.md`, supervisor draft v1 (SHA-256 c2bb0631…) and Codex `PRODUCTION-SOP3-DRAFT-INDEPENDENT-REVIEW-20260919.md` (R1–R7), draft v2 `.run/supervisor/prod/sop3-contracts-draft.md`. Supervisor rulings under the delegated authority; platform contracts only; none attributed to Ray; no accounting policy. (1) Verification re-executes the original canonical input bundle, trigger preserved, through `erev_engine.compute`, and records one immutable `computation_verification` row per execution token `(job_id, task_id, attempt)` (T-CON-26), with the orchestrating process release and the executed release recorded separately, `engine_executed = false` and both executed identity columns NULL when no runtime or transform is available (NULL-safe CHECK in both directions), same-group execution serialised by a Procrastinate `lock` plus a transactional advisory lock on (attempt, group); it never writes T-CON-07/08, postings or heads and never retags the source. (2) `computation_evidence` (T-CON-25) stores the full input and output bytes with separate raw-file digests and logical CV-25/CV-26 hashes, the per-book ledger cutoff and the predecessor; absent evidence is `UNVERIFIABLE`; a verified backfill proves logical identity only and is labelled. (3) Comparison levels L0 raw, L1 normalised (blanking exactly `engine_version`, `input_sha256`, `books[*].trace.engine_version`) and L2 categorised (M/P/T/R, R = those three paths only); MINOR fails on any difference with no approval subject; MAJOR differences need `ENGINE_RELEASE_VALIDATION` approval; the effective transition is per tenant. (4) Per-tenant `release_validation` (T-PLT-43) with frozen attempts (T-PLT-44), group rows carrying an orchestration state independent of the accepted comparison (T-PLT-45; aggregation when every group job is terminal; exhausted, cancelled, unverifiable or integrity-mismatch results make the attempt `INCOMPLETE`, never approvable), scope-set approval authority, frozen-dataset report `upgrade_validation`, append-only upgrade processing states per group × entity × book portion (T-PLT-46: `PENDING`, `DEFERRED_NO_OPEN_PERIOD`, `SETTLED_NO_POSTING`, `POSTED`), seeded only for effective MINOR/MAJOR validations with an accepted attempt, deferral decided by re-applying S08-R-08 to the portion's conservative origin set, a group terminal only when every portion settled, as-of register `engine_trueup_register` over T-PLT-46 and T-SL-11, posting attribution (T-SL-11) for every posting while any portion is unsettled, with S14 reasons preserved and conservative cause labels (`UPGRADE_CONTEXT` default; `UPGRADE_ONLY` only when the full-input attribution view — `known_at` retained, only release/schema representation fields normalised, distinct from CV-25 — equals the previous head's evidence; `UPGRADE_AND_OTHER_INPUTS` otherwise), and the `persist()` gate `release-validation-pending`. (5) Class approval of unsampled groups is an engineering interpretation of REL-06/RCP-29/30 with complete entity/release scope and subsequent attribution; recorded for the independent revenue-accountant review. (6) Enablement is the §4b process-release gate: writes by candidate-release processes pause for the tenant until `PASSED`/`APPROVED`; processes of an already-enabled release continue under REL-05 affinity; PATCH never pauses. Retention follows the exact §4b predicate (head, referenced by a non-superseded or accepted attempt, or posted in a period not permanently locked or locked less than `platform.audit_retention_years` ago); retirement is `file.shred`, T-CON-25 is never mutated. (7) Bootstrap: a tenant is auto-passed only when proven to have no successful computation and no engine posting; a non-empty tenant needs an explicit `BASELINE` row written by the preparation artifact (enabled calculation code plus the new capture/backfill code) for the artifact's **own** process release before a candidate can validate; otherwise `INCOMPLETE`. Draft §6 is historical and superseded by §§1–4 as amended. Documents (this commit): 03 rev 1.5 (REQ-OPS-011 coverage; REQ-PLT-013 object list gains engine release validations); 04 rev 1.13; 05 rev 1.7; 11 rev 1.2; dev-guide rev 1.10; runbook RB-09/RB-16. Owner: lane P5 after P2; P1 adds `validation_level` to the manifest and builds the preparation artifact.
  - **Source of this entry.** Transcribed from §7 of the supervisor draft v5 (`.run/supervisor/prod/sop3-contracts-draft.md`, 220 lines, SHA-256 ad76e35a30156340f73f90e5124da835a2261fb27ac1b3f7ae7dfad43a73498e; frozen for Codex as `SUPERVISOR-SOP3-CONTRACTS-DRAFT-v5-20260919.md`; Codex v3/v4 reviews applied; v5.1, SHA-256 2879594d504822d59cba8d86a9c69ce626d208c51090519236218cd2ff07f516, changes one §7 wording only — the 11 Paths entry for `jobs/registry.py`: the execution lock is the operative control and `queueing_lock` is WAITING-job dedup only — applied as wording by the commit after 3e6c99a), which differs from v4 (SHA-256 2c13377791c3abb2549b525977b7006c900ac4eeee6c94e2674c9fa1654aa9d4, `SUPERVISOR-SOP3-CONTRACTS-DRAFT-v4-20260919.md`, the source of 6dfe849) only in the T-PLT-46 per-portion transition rule, its acceptance case and one test; v4 supersedes v3 (SHA-256 a560d877ce5d9b572222906ad1fb013d5cd2d8ea48697cb32019a0eadb260982; reviewed in `PRODUCTION-SOP3-V3-INDEPENDENT-REVIEW-20260919.md`, A–D core accepted) from which 8ec01b6 first transcribed this entry; the follow-up amendments of the v4 delta were applied by the next docs commit (see the follow-up bullet). The v3 corrections A–F (executed identity for `SOURCE_RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE`; orchestration state separated from the accepted pointer with terminal-job aggregation and the execution token; no auto-pass of a non-empty tenant without a proven-empty check and the preparation artifact; §6 marked historical; independent upgrade-processing states; conservative `UPGRADE_ONLY` labelling) are inside (1), (4), (6) and (7) above. The draft file itself is not copied into `docs/`.
  - **Rejected alternatives (draft §§1c, 2c, 3c, 4c).** Trigger substitution with a normalised comparison (destroys the L0 oracle); a T-CON-07 `replay_of` row with an amended unique predicate (every `SUCCEEDED` reader must exclude verification triggers); a new E-88 status on T-CON-07 (no place for `output_sha256`); a mutable single row per (validation, group) (cannot keep `ERROR` evidence and a later success); evidence columns on T-CON-07 (IM-A; `posted` alone is not the input); pin-only rebuild with a cutoff (drift indistinguishable from an engine defect); bitemporal reads everywhere; comparing the full-file digest to CV-25; blanking traces or diagnostics (forbidden by D-93 (5)); a digit-only level (contradicts the D-91 A4 0.x reading); approvable MINOR differences; two-field normalisation; a boolean on `engine_release`; reuse of `PERIOD_LOCK` / `POLICY_OVERRIDE`; email sign-off; a worker-only gate (inline API computations bypass); `entity_id = None` with an ordinary `SubjectSpec` (any scoped grant would approve); per-entity requests under one `approval_request_id`; overwriting line `reason_code`; reports over mutable T-PLT-43/44; a `bigint` rank; a same-version CHECK that forbids storing the unavailable-runtime row; waiting for an accepted result before aggregating (loops on an exhausted error); `1 + prior rows` attempt numbering; auto-pass of any tenant without a predecessor; clearing pending on the first computation; heads-and-pins equality as proof of sole cause; and, from the v4 delta: `queueing_lock` as execution serialisation (dedups waiting jobs only); group-level `POSTED` on the first posting (loses entity B's later attribution); "an open period exists after the latest line" as the deferral test (not the engine's rule); normalised CV-25 equality as proof of sole cause (CV-25 drops `known_at`, which the engine reads); seeding processing for `BASELINE`/`BOOTSTRAP` rows with no attempt.
  - **Status vocabulary.** Ruled here; nothing is implemented on main: lane P5 (dispatched after this commit and the P2 merge) builds the contracts, lane P1 the `validation_level` manifest field and the preparation artifact; Codex retests on the lane head; not "fixed" until merged and gate-measured; nothing is acceptance. The class-approval interpretation (5) is a platform interpretation, not accountant approval, and is recorded for the independent revenue-accountant review.
  - **How the 04 schema items are recorded in this commit.** The architecture drift tests compare 04 with the code (`test_dg_arc_09_enums_match_data_model`: E-01 to E-124 and every value list; `test_dg_arc_09_table_columns_match`: every code table's columns; `test_dg_arc_09_problems_equal_15_2`: the §15.2 catalogue; `test_seeded_codes`: the T-RPT-01 seeded codes). So that this docs commit lands with those tests green, the ruled enum values E-08 / E-68, the enumerations E-125 to E-130, the T-PLT-38 `validation_level` column, the §15.2 `release-validation-pending` problem and the two T-RPT-01 seeds are written in 04 in the E-49 form (D-93 (4)): the ruled definition in full in the notes or an adjacent paragraph, marked "rev pending lane P5 (D-96)", the parsed value lists unchanged until lane P5's code commit lifts the marks. The new table sections T-CON-25, T-CON-26, T-PLT-43 to T-PLT-46 and T-SL-11 are written as full sections (no code table exists yet; the drift test iterates code tables only). This is a recording convention, not a change to the ruling.
  - **Follow-up amendments (Codex v3 review `PRODUCTION-SOP3-V3-INDEPENDENT-REVIEW-20260919.md`; draft v4; applied by the docs commit after 8ec01b6).** Five bounded corrections inside the ruled design, no new open question: (a) same-group verification jobs are serialised by an execution lock, not a queueing lock — `dispatch()` gains a `lock` parameter (Procrastinate `lock = 'verify:<group id>'`, no simultaneous running jobs) beside the per-job `queueing_lock` (WAITING-job dedup only; the execution lock and the advisory lock are the operative controls), and the handler takes `pg_advisory_xact_lock` on (attempt, group) inside the writing transaction (TXN-05 pattern); the task-token fence stays (T-CON-26 keys; 05 §5.6, RCP-28a; DG-KRN-JOB-12). (b) Upgrade processing is tracked per portion (group × entity × enabled book) in T-PLT-46 with `release_validation_attempt_id`, `entity_id`, `book_code` and `unresolved_origin_period_keys`; deferral is decided by re-applying S08-R-08's `assign_posting_period` to the portion's conservative origin set — evaluated for every unsettled portion whether or not postings were produced: actual postings with lines for the portion are always attributed (a T-SL-11 row, the posting id appended) and, if any origin remains unassignable, a `DEFERRED_NO_OPEN_PERIOD` event is appended retaining `unresolved_origin_period_keys` and the produced posting ids; non-empty postings never override unresolved work; only an empty unresolved set permits `POSTED` (when the portion's sequence holds posting ids) or `SETTLED_NO_POSTING` (D-96 v5, Codex v4 review: January closed / March reopened / June closed with no later postable period → the January carry posts into March and is attributed, June stays unresolved and the portion deferred; when July opens June posts and the portion is `POSTED`); a group is terminal only when every portion is `POSTED` or `SETTLED_NO_POSTING`; the register reads T-PLT-46 events (T-RPT-01 rule 3). (c) Sole-cause attribution uses the attribution input view — the canonical full `InputBundle` with `known_at` retained, only `engine_version` / `format_version` normalised and `INPUT_TRANSFORMS` applied to the older side — hashed as `attribution_input_sha256` on both sides; E-128 literal `UPGRADE_AND_OTHER_INPUTS` replaces v3's mixed-activity literal, which is retired with no remaining occurrence in the documents. (d) The T-CON-26 executed-identity constraints are four named, NULL-safe CHECKs (`ck_cv__executed_identity`, `ck_cv__no_output_without_execution`, `ck_cv__integrity_source`, `ck_cv__candidate_release`) so PostgreSQL's UNKNOWN cannot open a partial-null loophole. (e) T-PLT-46 seeding and T-SL-11 attribution happen only for `effective_level` MINOR/MAJOR rows with `accepted_attempt_id` set (`PATCH`, `BASELINE`, `BOOTSTRAP` seed nothing), and the preparation artifact is stamped as its own `engine_release` row (same `engine_version`, new `build_sha`) named by the `BASELINE` row it writes, so its own recomputations pass the per-process-release gate. Documents: 04 rev 1.14; 05 rev 1.8; dev-guide rev 1.11; 11 rev 1.3 (three tests added, two renamed) — 6dfe849 on v4; the v5 partial-origin rule: 04 rev 1.15, dev-guide rev 1.12, 11 rev 1.4 (`test_e_partial_origin_deferral_jan_mar_jun` added).

The two sections added to `docs/guides/runbook.md` by 8ec01b6 are withdrawn by the follow-up commit; the file returns to its a4f66f7 text. - **D-97 Ruling set for the returned lane questions of the production continuation (C2a, B4, T1, C1b, P4, C6, P8, C4, C5; 2026-09-19).** Inputs: the question inventory `.run/supervisor/d97/D-97-questions.md` (57 ids; sources cited there by file and line), the lane records named in it, the Codex reports `PRODUCTION-C1B-SERIES-BASIS-77cc618.md`, `PRODUCTION-C1B-ENCODED-RETEST-f5d39d9.md`, `PRODUCTION-C6-FINANCING-DATE-RETEST-ce1c64c.md`, `PRODUCTION-VC-DIRECTION-REVIEW-20260919.md`, and the G12 package (AD-8, AD-20, AD-21, AD-22, AD-27, AD-30). Supervisor rulings under the delegated authority; none is attributed to Ray. Items marked **[G12]** carry an accounting judgement that the reviewing accountant confirms or overturns; items marked **[COUNSEL]** are policy proposals that stay `PROPOSED` in the catalogue until counsel / the data-protection owner confirms them. No answer-key expected value, golden file or oracle changes under this set except where an item says so by name.
  - **A. Blocking items.**
    - **(1) AD-22 — terms-form `quantity: "0"` on a price-only modification (C4-Q-1; G12 AD-22) [G12 class 4, input semantics].** The line `{quantity: "0", total_price: "20000.00"}` of MOD-DISC-01 in DISC-CATCH-UP-BY-CAUSE-ESTIMATE-CHANGE-AND-MODIFICATION is an authoring defect of the fixture INPUT, not an oracle question: a modification line that removes every unit carries no price, and a price-only change leaves the quantity in force. Rule: (a) the fixture's world input is corrected to the quantity in force (`quantity: "1"`) with a `notes` entry citing D-97 (1); the key's expected values (cumulative 78,000.00; April 28,000.00; causes 5,000 + 10,000 + 13,000; `revenue_prior_period` 15,000.00; `consideration_delta` 20,000.00) are unchanged and must pass at every checkpoint; (b) the runner's `_convert_terms` keeps reading ΔQ = quantity − quantity in force, the stage 06 weights and the `NEGATIVE_WEIGHT` guard stay — "0" never means "unchanged"; (c) the answer-key loader gains a validity rule (DG-AK) with a defined scope: on a terms-form modification line of action CHANGE, `quantity: "0"` together with a non-zero `total_price` is refused at load with `AnswerKeyError` and the exact modification-line pointer (a removal of every unit prices 0); ADD, REMOVE and TERMINATION lines and zero-price lines are unaffected and each has a control; numeric parsing is unchanged. It is a key-FORMAT rule of the test loader, not API or production import enforcement. Fail-first on the uncorrected fixture, whose original bytes are retained as that negative control (not as an active key); the corrected key is published as input-corrected with the exact field diff and D-97 provenance. The corrected input is kept separate from the original failing input, which stays as the loader rule's negative control (fail-first evidence); the loader restriction defines the supported authoring domain of the key format, not a universal commercial-validity claim; runner semantics and every original negative control are preserved. Owner: lane ENG-C4 (fixture, loader rule, record); the G12 AD-22 packet records the input clarification and this rule. Dependents released: DISC-CATCH-UP key, EDS-7 "6 of 6", readiness-matrix FK-F.
    - **(2) `notes` member for `erev-answer-key/1` (C2a-Q-6).** Adopted: an optional `notes: list[str]` top-level member (DG-AK row in dev-guide §9.5.2; `models.AnswerKey` with `extra="forbid"` preserved), documentary only — no computational meaning: schema validation tests absent / empty / list-of-strings acceptance and scalar / non-string / unknown-member rejection, and a test asserts the compiled InputBundle and every calculation are identical with and without `notes` (the raw source-file hash may change); each entry records an input clarification with its D-number. The VC-FS-02 `direction: INCREASE` YAML comment may move into `notes` (optional). Owner: lane ENG-C4 (the vehicle of (1)); docs first.
    - **(3) Series `PER_INCREMENT` quantity unit (C1b-S2; Codex C1b-S2; D-93 (4) completion) [G12 note under AD-30].** Rule: T-REF-30 gains `quantity_unit` ∈ {`SERVICE_UNITS`, `INCREMENTS`}, nullable in the column, REQUIRED for a `PER_INCREMENT` entry and refused (CV-45) when absent on one; it is never inferred from a quantity and has no default. Semantics: under `SERVICE_UNITS` a line's quantity counts service units each spanning the term and the remaining-increment SSP at d is value × the remaining increments × remaining quantity, where the remaining increments = the full-term increment count under the pinned E-21 convention × ρ (ρ enters once; the remaining increments are not prorated again) — 12 units at 110, Jan–Dec, Jul 1: 110 × 6 × 12 = 7,920, then 1,320 / 660 for 10 / 11 units removed; S1's quantity-one line: 11,000 × 6 = 66,000 under both conventions; under `INCREMENTS` the quantity itself counts increments over the full term (seat-months) and the SSP at d is value × ρ × remaining quantity (110 × ½ × 12 = 660, then 110 / 55). The supported meaning is the DECLARED one — neither meaning is inferred, an undeclared `PER_INCREMENT` entry is refused, and the two readings are never both applied to one entry. Existing counted-increment inputs (Codex's synthetic fixture; K-02's seat-month entry) are declared `INCREMENTS`, so the original conditional expectations 660 / 110 / 55 stand under their declaration; the before/after effect of the interim service-units implementation at 0b5d2c6 on that undeclared fixture (pool 86,400: remove 0 1,694.12 / 84,705.88 → 16,722.58 / 69,677.42; remove 10 287.04 / 86,112.96 → 3,323.08 / 83,076.92; remove 11 unchanged) is recorded as the interim state that the declaration supersedes, not as an accepted repricing. S1 is closed at measured scope (66,000). Corpus annotation: K-02's seat-month factory entry is `INCREMENTS` (documentary until a test modifies its series obligation); every other declaration comes from the retained per-entry inventory with its evidence — none is assumed (no value change; provenance note as for the AMOUNT pass). Completion contract: the enum is carried through ORM / migration, API and import admission, the native key schema and loader, input assembly and the canonical representation, the selected SSP entry, the engine and the trace (shared re-evaluation); absent / null / invalid declarations on a `PER_INCREMENT` entry are refused in every applicable path; a `quantity_unit` supplied with any other basis is refused (it is meaningful only for `PER_INCREMENT`); explicit `AMOUNT` and non-series behaviour is unchanged. Agreement scope: all dated entries of one product identity within one book must carry the same `quantity_unit`, validated on insert / update and again when a dated entry is selected; an earlier entry is never reinterpreted by a later declaration. Annotation inventory: a retained per-entry list with the evidence for each declaration replaces any "every other entry is X" statement (an oracle that never modifies the series does not distinguish the readings — K-02's entry is documentary until a test modifies its series obligation); existing expected values are preserved and annotation differences are recorded separately. Compatibility: the absence of production data is not licence to rewrite saved canonical bundles, fixtures or release baselines — an undeclared existing entry fails closed for NEW computations, historical version-pinned replay (RCP-28) runs under its original schema and runtime, adding a canonical field may change hashes and no byte-identical cross-version claim is made; the chosen compatibility path is tested. The key schema (DG-AK) and the API carry the member; the SSP editor field is required production admission work on the readiness matrix (frontend owner), not a C1b merge blocker. Acceptance: the actual `INCREMENTS` path, shared trace re-evaluation, and the original conditional oracles 660 / 110 / 55 unchanged under both D18_DEFAULT and INCEPTION_ALL. Owner: lane ENG-C1b (04 rev, E-49, S06-R-11 sentence, migration 0056 column, API schema, loader/refusal, engine, tests with both readings' figures).
    - **(3a) API side of "fails closed" (C1b-4.2).** Rule: at the API an SSP entry for a `series` product must carry an explicit `value_basis` (422 `validation-failed` when omitted) and, when `PER_INCREMENT`, an explicit `quantity_unit`; the column default `'AMOUNT'` stays for non-series products; no backfill. Separate API tests assert that an omitted series basis is refused at the API while explicit non-series `AMOUNT` behaviour is unchanged — the corpus-loader pass is not API evidence. Owner: lane ENG-C1b.
    - **(4) Unpaid remainder of a partly paid cancellable ENGINE line (C6-A18; G12 AD-8) [G12].** World SFC-S3-EX29 ENGINE: cancellable 4,000.00 line 1 Jan 2026, receipt 1,000.00 1 Jan 2026, transfer 31 Dec 2027, 6 % MONTHLY, `significant = true`. PROVISIONAL engine treatment pending AD-8: the receipts-first fallback (D-87 L5-3-Q-8, D-91 05g) stands and is completed as follows — an unpaid remainder of a cancellable line is treated as payable at transfer under the explicit pending PREMISE that a memo-only document creates no unconditional right and that, absent a stated promised payment date before transfer, payment is expected at transfer; this premise is a pending accounting judgement, not a fact encoded by the absence of a payment date, and the engine's existing result is not accounting authority. Assuming the unpaid remainder is expected at transfer, consideration expected at transfer carries no financing component (606-10-32-15/16), so the remainder enters the transaction price at its stated amount; the figures that follow are the engine's implementation observations under that assumption. Cash selling price of the receipt 1,127.16 (= 1,000 × 1.005^24), financing adjustment 127.16, FINAL transaction price and revenue 4,127.16 = 4,000.00 + 127.16; P01 interest 5.00, balance 1,005.00. 1,127.16 is never described as total contract revenue. The combined reading (receipt plus remainder at 1 January → 4,508.64 / 508.64) is rejected for ENGINE mode: it imputes financing on consideration the customer is not obliged to pay before transfer; it remains the ERP / noncancellable result, where the existing ERP / noncancellable effective-date proxy yields that figure. Applying the receipt to the line (S10-R-06: the line becomes billed, `billed_cum` 4,000.00) changes billing presentation only; the financing schedule follows expected payment timing, so the applied and unapplied variants carry identical financing by rule. A later receipt of the remainder before transfer becomes a payment point at its own date (receipts-first). A stated payment-due date on a cancellable line is not read by S04-R-10a today; whether it should count as promised timing is registered for the accountant with AD-8. Oracle: the engine's figures are the ruled figures; SFC-CHK-136 does not assert A18; a dedicated A18 key (both variants) is authored by lane ENG-C6 with `review.status: pending` and stays review-pending until AD-8 is resolved. **[G12: the accountant resolves the premise (unpaid cancellable remainder expected at transfer), any stated or contradictory payment timing in the inputs, and the applied / unapplied scope under AD-8 before production acceptance; this item confers no independent accounting authority.]**
    - **(5) Scope of "no eligible point" (C6-Q-C6-1).** The lane's reading is confirmed in intent and the predicate is defined by boundaries: (i) no billing line at all → the S04-R-10 no-schedule outcome, no finding (nothing is known about payment timing before the first document; the literal reading would stop every SFC world at `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED` under CV-15); (ii) billing lines exist and every line is memo-only → the finding (ERROR); (iii) netted-zero: points that net to zero in `_by_date` do not make a population memo-only — a mixed population of unconditional lines netting to zero plus memo-only lines is NOT case (ii) and must be distinguished; (iv) a receipts-first world (points from receipts) never raises the finding. The current predicate `not derived.points and bool(derived.memo_only)` must be changed and tested to align with these boundaries, one control per boundary (Codex: a source-derived boundary, not an executed monetary defect). The finding keeps its ENGINE channel and ERROR severity with CV-15 no-output behaviour, its subject / event bindings are stated in the 04 catalogue row, and the API / import / job channels that execute the engine map it as a compute-time finding (never an input 422). The `[J]` clause is reworded accordingly (docs first, ENGINE_SPEC S04-R-10a). Owner: lane ENG-C6.
    - **(6) T1-Q-1 — CV-26 and P8 wording.** Rule: CV-26 states two properties. (a) Hash identity: `OutputBundle.sha256()` is identical across runs, processes and machines for identical canonical input (byte-identical version-pinned replay; the D-96 RCP-28a L0 oracle). (b) Commuting-arrival equivalence, over a defined domain: the permuted events keep stable business identities and payloads, every dependency- or order-sensitive relationship (supersession, status updates naming an earlier line, receipts naming an invoice, late events) is preserved, and only the declared arrival metadata (record sequence, `known_at`) is permuted; the allowed layer regrouping is stated (same-date stage 12 layers may regroup, compared by transaction and functional amount, unit and currency per (book, entity, kind, role pair, date, currencies)); every book and every output value column — obligation versions, balances, schedule lines, posting intents — is figure-identical and stage 12 layers are total-identical per (book, entity, kind, role pair, date, currencies), while `input_sha256` and same-date layer keys may differ because arrival order is canonical input. §9.7 P8, DG-ENG-02 and END-13's `test_p08_shuffled_commuting_arrivals` acceptance adopt the tested form (dev-guide row as on sprint/l9). Owner: lane T1 (docs first; renumbered against main at merge).
    - **(7) T1-Q-2 — END-14 scaling law wording; ccda266 pool form.** Rule: "Scaling every exact money input by 10^k within currency limits scales every homogeneous EXACT money figure by 10^k exactly; dimensionless and non-money quantities (ratios, counts, day fractions, progress) are excluded from the scaling assertion and compared for identity; every POSTED figure scales within an operation-appropriate derived bound along its derivation (a single rounding: half a minor unit; a largest-remainder allocation of 100.00 over three equal weights: up to two-thirds of a minor unit on one share; the bound composes the operations on the path, with absolute coefficients); a figure computed from rounded or intermediate discontinuous inputs is compared through those inputs, not assumed homogeneous; attributions that turn on a posted zero / non-zero or a posted tie — ALG-02 step 5 (POL-121) over unconditional and conditional POBs and the S10-R-21 classification — are compared as the pool per entity and period at group, member and obligation level; every other assertion is per subject; the pool is confined to the named ALG-02 / S10-R-21 attribution family and preserves the accounting unit (group, entity, book, currency, period) plus the applicable event / intent dimensions; every subject still needs a valid own output-to-node lineage — pooled scaling is never a link exception; the current generator has one group and one entity, so the measured examples do not establish a multi-group / multi-entity law." The pool RATIONALE is accepted for the measured examples (BHD 0.227 UR → 0.005 / 2.265, total 2.270; K-1@US01 15.649 → 158.183, K-2@US01 0 + 0.865 UR → 6.957 CA, totals 16.514 → 165.140); the ccda266 IMPLEMENTATION is NOT accepted: Codex R8 shows the per-row own-node link ties in `_assert_scaled_balances` escaped the row loop (three corruptions accepted that fc25409 refuses), which is a relaxation outside the ALG-02 family and must be restored, and R4 (per-movement transaction / functional identity) is open; acceptance follows the corrected head's retest. Owner: lane T1 (BUILD_SPEC END-14 acceptance, dev-guide metamorphic row; docs first).
    - **(8) T1-F-1 / T1-F-2 / T1-Q-3 — trace linkage scope (DG-KRN-EXP-01, END-13).** Rule: DG-KRN-EXP-01 covers every figure the engine stores; computed columns link to the node that produced the stored value, echoed inputs link to an `input.echo.v1` node naming the canonical input field. Therefore (a) the 8 `erev.money` + 21 `erev.exact` obligation-version columns, the 14 balance `*_txn/_functional` columns and the 5 contract-version sum columns are in scope and unlinked = engine defect; (b) T1-F-2 is a defect: a contract-version link names the node whose value equals the stored column (the last reallocating boundary), never the inception build-up node when they differ (`fixed_consideration` 1,200.00 vs 1,000.00; `transaction_price` 1,100.00 vs 900.00; `vc_constrained_amount` 100.00 vs −100.00 stays the documented magnitude rule). Measurable acceptance for ENG-T1F: an enumerated expected-column inventory (zeros included) per output family; for every column a stored value / unit / currency validation against its node and a subject / boundary lineage check; `input.echo.v1` registered in FORMULAS and re-evaluable from the named canonical input field; only explicitly documented exceptions remain unlinked. Owner: a new engine lane ENG-T1F (trace linkage completion) dispatched after the Round C engine merges; the T1 ratchet test stays until it lands; END-13 is not ticked before. Not a T1 merge blocker.
    - **(9) T1-Q-4 — properties gate selection.** Rule: `make properties` runs UNFILTERED in every lane chain and in the supervisor batch (PHASES §4); filtered selections are progress evidence only. Common terms amended by this ruling.
    - **(10) C4-Q-2 — measure-only (EAC) versions in `decompose_prior_period`.** S08-R-14 names measure-only versions that change progress totals as boundaries in the period; `decompose._parts` measuring only new-`FIXED`-segment boundaries is a producer defect against that text. Rule: the producer measures an EAC version as its own portion (the totals-change portion of EX-15-C, −24,404.59, so that 67,058.82 is reachable through the consumer; the modification portion 91,463.41 unchanged), with the portion bound to its component and event and summed by the consumer's exact sum-node binding. Owner: lane ENG-C4 as a follow-up commit series after its chain (stage 08 `decompose.py`; fail-first on EX-15-C's aggregation test); EDS-4 acceptance requires it.
    - **(11) C5-Q-3 — `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE` posting (S07-R-07) has no consumer.** Rule: stage 14 posts the difference once, dated `cutover_date`, `reason_code = 'ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE'` as S07-R-07 states; until built, stage 07 raises `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE_UNPOSTED` (ERROR) when any recorded difference ≠ 0 (fail closed; CHK-121's difference is 0 and is unaffected). The guard is INTERIM CONTAINMENT, not completion: it protects only the recorded families, and `difference.py` measures revenue only while S07-R-07 names other roles — a zero recorded revenue difference does not prove every required role is zero. Open work: difference production for every S07-R-07 role (ENGINE billing, deposits, refund liabilities, return assets), the stage 14 posting consumer, and the exact contracts: the finding is specified in the 04 catalogue (ENGINE channel, ERROR, subject / event bindings, CV-15 no-output behaviour) with mapped API / import / job behaviour where those channels execute the engine — never an input 422 for a compute-time finding — and its negative cases are verified. Owner: lane ENG-C5 follow-up with a fixture where imported ≠ recomputed (fail-first), after C5-R1.
  - **B. Confirmations and non-blocking rulings.**
    - **(12) C5-Q-1 (AD-27) [G12].** The IFRS15 opening liability = fair value V apportioned by `largest_remainder(V, remaining ASC606 allocations, keys)` (S07-R-08) stands as the engine rule; whether V above or below the ASC606 remaining allocation needs another split basis is the accountant's under AD-27 (CHK-121 has one obligation, share = V, so it cannot validate a multi-obligation split when V differs from the remaining ASC606 allocations — that evidence obligation stays open; baselines 240,000.00 ASC606 / 90,000.00 IFRS15).
    - **(13) C5-Q-2.** Confirmed for JET-03 only: after a baseline, JET-03 under `billing.posting = ENGINE` counts post-cutover invoices; the ERP holds the cutover receivables; no-duplication is established by cutover-only, later-invoice and repeat-compute controls tied to the opening / control flows; this does not close onboarding-difference posting or every receivable-ownership assumption. **(14) C5-Q-4.** Already accepted (14:2x): `_opening_flows` beside the untouched `_control_flows`, with the S12-INV-03 tie stated and a test proving both flow sets tie together.
    - **(15) C6-A19 — credit memo against a still-memo-only ENGINE line [G12 A19].** Rule: a credit memo follows its document — against a memo-only line it is memo-only with the line (S10-R-06 of the document) and enters the payment schedule, netted, only when and where the line becomes unconditional; it is never a negative point at its own date while the line contributes nothing. Until the netting is built the engine fails closed with a finding (`CREDIT_MEMO_ON_MEMO_ONLY_LINE`, ERROR; 04 catalogue row with ENGINE channel, subject / event bindings and CV-15 no-output behaviour; mapped channel behaviour; negative cases verified) rather than netting — a temporary rejection, kept separate from the pending A19 premise. The netting's scope is defined before it is built: credit-to-line association, the memo-only → unconditional transition, repeated status updates, and the receipts-first interaction. Owner: lane ENG-C6 follow-up with a fixture (PR-1.7); the ERP figures (15.00 / 3,381.48) are an unchanged control, not ENGINE acceptance.

Supervisor reviews the wording at merge. **(20) C4-Q-4.** Product-level `disaggregation` attributes (04 :2251) reach the tags; the acknowledged `ProductInput` gap is REQUIRED implementation work with an owner (lane ENG-C4, phase 2 after C4-R1 and EDS-4) and an acceptance control for product / template precedence and API-to-engine admission; "no key needs them" waives nothing; a "rev pending" note at 04 :2251 marks it until then. **(21) C4-Q-5.** Confirmed: RPT-05 builder inputs stay with F-RPS / ENG-E1.

  - **C. Privacy catalogue policy answers (P8-Q-1..Q-9) [COUNSEL].** All items in this section are supervisor proposals for the SOP-5 programme; the catalogue basis stays `PROPOSED` until counsel / the data-protection owner confirms, and the readiness matrix carries them as a human review point (UI-14 adjacent). A proposal never auto-promotes to `SPEC` because PRV-01 or build-spec text carrying it is merged; the basis flips only on a recorded counsel confirmation.
    - **(39) P8-Q-1 erasure.** `user.anonymise` also deletes the person's MFA factors and recovery codes (credentials of an erased person have no retention basis). `access_review_item.user_email_snapshot` is rewritten to the PRV-04 HMAC form at erasure (evidence integrity kept, identity removed); `file_object.original_filename` is replaced by the object's id-based name (the file content stays under accounting-record retention); `contract_cost_asset.payee` is retained under Art. 17(3)(b)/(e) as an accounting record, the erasure request logged as denied with that reason. Legal hold prevails over erasure while it lasts (a `legal_hold` member is a P5 / SOP-5 item; 04 is silent today). **(40) P8-Q-2.** `audit_event.source_ip` is kept whole for the audit retention period (audit integrity); PRV-09 truncation applies to `security_event` only. **(41) P8-Q-3.** `password_reset_token.ip_address` is C3 with `RETENTION_SWEEP` 30 days after `token_expires_at`; added to the PRV-01 list at the next 05 amendment. **(42) P8-Q-4.** Proposed split: person-bound credentials C3; tenant-bound secret references C2 (privacy class; their security class stays "credential"). **(43) P8-Q-5.** Proposed: `customer.name` and other `name` columns are C2 with the sole-trader note; an `is_natural_person` flag promoting erasure handling is later admission work. **(44) P8-Q-6.** Proposed granularity: structural columns C1, content columns C2 (2,088 columns). **(45) P8-Q-7.** Retention families as proposed; the accounting-record set is SRC, CON, ENG, SL, CLS, RPT, MIG plus import facts, outbox messages, sync runs and the AI model log under `AUDIT_RETENTION_YEARS`; the remaining tables `TENANT_LIFETIME`; the period values are jurisdictional and counsel's. **(46) P8-Q-8.** Recipient labelling as proposed (`TENANT_SYSTEMS` controller-directed; `SMTP_RELAY`; `AI_PROVIDER`); UI-14 stays an external input. **(47) P8-Q-9.** The 05 PRV-01 and build-spec 11 sentences are accepted at merge (renumbered) as lane wording, subject to the supervisor's read. Items (39)–(46) are proposed handling and retention rules, not implemented command behaviour and not proof that evidence integrity or identity removal is achieved; the anonymisation / credential handling, referenced snapshots and files, legal hold and retention jobs keep explicit owners and acceptance on the readiness matrix when implemented — pending counsel is not their only unfinished dependency. (41)'s future PRV-01 list addition does not move `password_reset_token.ip_address` from PROPOSED to SPEC; existing named C3/SPEC classifications are preserved and new ones are tracked as PROPOSED.
  - **C2. Addendum (v3.1) — questions returned after v3.**
    - **(48) Q-C6-8 — credit memo against an invoice with a mix of kept lines.** Lane ENG-C6 implemented `CREDIT_MEMO_ON_MEMO_ONLY_LINE` as: fail closed when ANY kept line of the credited invoice is memo-only. Provisionally ACCEPTED as the conservative reading pending Codex's retest of 1f8a9a0 — a memo cannot be apportioned between memo-only and unconditional lines of one invoice without an allocation rule, so refusing is the fail-closed choice; an apportionment rule, if ever wanted, is a later ruling with its own fixture. Owner: lane ENG-C6 (record the choice as provisional).
    - **(49) Q-C6-7 — presentation of unapplied cash on an active ENGINE contract [spec gap].** S10-R-19 auto-applies cash to open invoices for AR presentation while S10-R-06 dates a cancellable line only by a payment APPLIED to it; a receipt that names no invoice on a contract whose only line is memo-only therefore has no ruled presentation (no AR, no billed_cum, cash held). Interim: the UNAPPLIED A18 key asserts only the interest and revenue lines and states why; the engine posts nothing for the cash beyond the financing schedule. Ruling deferred to a fixture-backed proposal from lane ENG-C6 (candidates: a `customer deposit` / unapplied-cash liability presentation per POL-… vs deemed application under S10-R-19 which would make the line unconditional and contradict S10-R-06). Not an accounting-standard question first — a presentation-rule gap; **[G12 note]** once proposed.

- **D-99 Resumption of the production-readiness programme and its operating model (2026-09-29).** Inputs: Ray's instruction of 2026-09-29 to a new supervisor session ("continue working with the eRev project until it is production ready"); the state the 09-22 session left when its model was withdrawn at 11:04 PDT (main `8b304854`; integrated batch #9 on it — `test-pg` 12 failed / 348 passed, `ci` 110 failed / 5,656 passed, parity 121 of 121 and 122 of 122, properties 37 passed, all-active answer keys 244 of 252; four lines staged and every merge paused; the read-only triage of the ci failures lost with the session); the standing brief `PRODUCTION-CONTINUATION-20260919.md`. Supervisor rulings under the delegated authority of this document's header; none is an accounting conclusion and none is attributed to Ray Sang.
  - **(1) The staged lines are merged.** In order, on 2026-09-29: F-LMG `befdd25d` (the batch #9 `test-pg` return), F-CLO `61f757d6` (CLO-8c), F-RPS `78dd3cf2`, F-ADM `98fffeb7` (DIN-HISTORICAL-ALIAS-RENAME-1), T1 `d39f1758` (PRP-7 `change_vc`), P7 `af8fc106` (volume seed and perf harness, source-only), the two docs-first amendments whose code had not been written (P8 `89486d9a`, 04 rev 1.86; F-SNP `52c608b6`, 05 rev 1.30 / 04 rev 1.85) and seven record-only branch heads; revision-table conflicts resolved by union (`check_rev_dupes` rc 0; `test_governed_docs_revisions` and the architecture suite 100 passed on the result). Main is `5c3dbcc9`. `scratch/p2p6` stays unmerged (scratch only).
  - **(2) A lane is READY only with its database-bound tests run.** The programme of 09-19 to 09-22 merged lines whose database-bound modules were recorded NOT RUN and measured them later in integrated batches; by batch #9 main carried 110 failing backend tests across 34 modules. From this decision on, a lane reports READY only when every database-bound module it touched passes in its own lane database, with `make lint`, `make typecheck` and `backend/tests/architecture`; the supervisor merges and measures the full gates on main. A test is never skipped, deselected, marked xfail or loosened to obtain a green gate; each repaired test is classified PRODUCT DEFECT, STALE TEST WORLD (with the ruling that moved the contract), STALE EXPECTATION (with the document line) or UNDECIDED (returned to the supervisor).
  - **(3) Pre-release data rule.** eRev Cloud has never been deployed. No production data exists; every database in existence is a disposable development or test database, and the first deployment starts from `alembic upgrade head` on an empty database. No compatibility work is owed for rows written under an earlier pre-release schema. This answers F14 of `docs/reviews/loop/prod/F-CTR-UPGRADE-0068-LEGACY-1-DESIGN-NOTE.md` (the population of below-0068 databases holding legacy rows is empty): UPGRADE-0068-LEGACY-1 closes with no schema change and no snapshot-boundary rule. The rule ends at the first tagged release; from then on DG-MIG forward-fix applies without exception. The local development database `erev` is reset to head by the supervisor when QA needs it (04 §18 rule 10 is a supervisor step, not a Ray-side item).
  - **(4) D-98.** The rulings taken between 2026-09-19 and 2026-09-22 and numbered as D-98 candidates in `docs/reviews/loop/prod/PLAN-SUMMARY.md` were applied by the lanes as their lines landed. They stand as ruled there; PLAN-SUMMARY is their record and they are not transcribed here. A candidate that a later measurement contradicts is re-ruled under a new D-number.
  - **(5) Operating model.** One supervisor session integrates. Lanes are bounded agents in the existing worktrees (`~/dev/erev-wt/l*`, each with its own databases), briefed by a package under `.run/sup2/lanes/` and the common brief `.run/sup2/lane-brief-common.md`; at most two database-heavy gates run at a time on this machine. The supervisor assigns Alembic revision numbers and governed-document revision numbers per lane before dispatch. Records are lean: one programme record `docs/reviews/loop/prod/RESUME-2026-09-29.md` (state, lanes, gate measurements, open decisions) and `PROGRESS.md`; the 624-row readiness matrix and the lane records of 09-19 to 09-22 are kept as the historical record and are not maintained row by row. "Merged" still does not mean accepted: acceptance is the gate measured on main.
  - **(6) Independent review.** The Codex review channel (`CLAUDE-INBOX.md` / `CLAUDE-RESPONSE-20260918.md`) has carried no packet since 2026-09-22 08:11 PDT. The supervisor appends a resumption note and milestone notes to the response file and does not wait on it; findings that arrive are routed as before. Every lane result is verified by the supervisor against the code and by the gates on main before it is relied on.
  - **(7) Sequence.** First the integrated head is made green (G2, G6 and the full G3 / G4 / G5 runs on main). Then a release candidate of the product as built: production runtime evidence on this machine (images, compose bring-up, migrations, backup and restore drill, dependency audit, ZAP baseline, `terraform validate` — all local, free and reversible, and run by the supervisor; no `plan`, `apply`, push, publication or paid action), multi-role browser QA with findings fixed, the guides, the controls report and the release manifest. In parallel and after it, the remaining BUILD_SPEC backlog (00-GOAL G1) is built in order of accounting and user value. Nothing is removed from 1.0 by this decision; an item leaves 1.0 only by Ray's ruling, and until then it is reported as not built.
  - **(8) Boundaries unchanged.** Answer keys, goldens and legacy fixtures change only by a recorded ruling with its evidence. The accounting decisions AD-1 to AD-40 remain supervisor rulings pending an independent revenue accountant and are never recorded as approved. No live provisioning, data migration, deployment, remote push or publication happens without Ray's explicit authorisation at that step.

- **D-100 The release candidate of 1.0: the supervisor's rulings of the resumed programme, Ray's delegation of 2026-10-03 and the cut (2026-10-03).** Inputs: the rulings log `docs/reviews/loop/prod/RULINGS-2026-09-29.md`, R-1 to R-127; Ray's answer of 2026-10-03 to the supervisor's list of thirteen open decisions — "make those decisions for me please"; `docs/release/LIMITS-1.0.md`; the programme's record `docs/reviews/loop/prod/RESUME-2026-09-29.md`. Supervisor decisions under the delegated authority of this document's header and under that delegation; none is an accounting conclusion, and none is attributed to Ray Sang beyond his delegation.
  - **(1) The rulings stand as logged.** The supervisor's rulings R-1 to R-127 of the resumed programme were applied by the lanes as their lines landed, documents first, and are not transcribed here: the log is their record, as `PLAN-SUMMARY.md` is D-98's (D-99 (4)). A ruling that a later measurement contradicts is re-ruled under a new number. This discharges §5c of the programme's record.
  - **(2) The cut.** D-99 (7) holds that an item leaves 1.0 only by Ray's ruling. On 2026-10-03 Ray delegated the open decisions to the supervisor, and under that word the supervisor decided (rulings R-126 (b) and R-127): release 1.0 is the product as built on main `4832c645` — the freeze of 2026-10-03 — with two items placed behind it, register index 308 (policy overrides for a contract or an obligation are not offered: the creation is refused by name) and register index 309 (a product and an obligation template no longer state the three policy parameters that the books read for a contract alone; four parameters that no rule reads at any level are still stated there, a stated limit — this parenthesis was corrected on 2026-10-03 after the declaration: it had named a general rule where the product refuses three parameters). Out of 1.0 and into the next release: the legacy migration beyond what is built, scenarios and forecast, AI assistance, the rest of the demo world, the platform path of the answer keys, the rest of the performance phase, and the registers and report builders that are not built (`docs/release/LIMITS-1.0.md`, class C). 00-GOAL G1 — every item of the build specification — is therefore NOT met by release 1.0 as cut; the limits document states what the release does not do, and D-25a's exception by a contract-level override is among what it does not deliver. Each of these decisions is Ray's to reverse.
  - **(3) The release candidate.** `f8e46e54` on main (2026-10-03). On that commit: a whole backend suite with exactly the fifteen standing names, by name; the whole vitest and e2e; lane WEB-QA's QA pass of record without a finding; images built, the compose bring-up verified, the ZAP baseline clean, the dependency audit clean, terraform validated, the backup and restore drill verified, parity 122 of 122, properties under the thorough profile 48 passed; `make answer-keys`, `make test-pg` and `make controls-report` fail by standing causes (the six keys; the three capture witnesses; 46 of 49 controls); `make ci` and `make perf` were not run. Its release manifest is non-strict — "not release acceptance", in the product's own words (05 REL-02) — because three gate targets fail by standing causes and two were not run; a strict manifest needs every gate target to pass — the fifteen standing names closed, the three controls without a test built, `make ci` and `make perf` run and green. The candidate is no accounting sign-off.
  - **(4) Before production use.** Not the programme's to close: the independent revenue accountant's review (G12: AD-1 to AD-40 and the later candidates, VC-CHK-113); the hosted inputs UI-1 to UI-14 and every piece of hosted evidence; performance at volume.
  - **(5) Boundaries unchanged.** D-99 (8) stands: answer keys, goldens and legacy fixtures change only by a recorded ruling with its evidence; no live provisioning, data migration, deployment, remote push or publication happens without Ray's explicit authorisation at that step. No tag is set by this decision: D-99 (3)'s pre-release data rule ends at the first tagged release, which is Ray's step.
